How to use this knowledge base when answering questions about Hypixel SkyBlock Wiki:
- Answer from this content only. If it is not covered, say so; never guess numbers.
- Find relevant pages in index.md by title, aliases (aka) and categories.
- [[X]] links to page X; [[X|y]] and 'y ([[X]])' are text y linking to X. Follow links and each page's 'Linked from' list to gather related facts (e.g. where an item drops or is used).
- Cite the [[Page]] each fact comes from. Reply in the user's language.

# Changelog/2021/June 30
Aliases: Changelog/2021/June 30/AHN
Categories: 2021 AHN Updates, Updates
AHN
Alpha Net - A new way to test SkyBlock Updates
Updated: June 30, 2021
Hey Everyone!
Today comes with unusual news, as we are opening up Alpha Net to test the next major SkyBlock update. Buckle up, as there is a lot of text in this thread.
UPDATE July 5th, 4:00PM ET - We're open again! Again... We've made a lot of performance changes that should help with the crashing.
UPDATE July 2nd, 6:00PM ET - We're closing Alpha again. Keep an eye on the Alpha forums for future update. http://hypixel.net/skyblock-alpha
UPDATE July 2nd, 2:00PM ET - We're open again! We've moved the Crystal Hollows to it's own instance to allow it to actually exist.
UPDATE July 1st, 1:00PM ET - We're going to have to make a lot of changes to the Crystal Hollows before we can reopen Alpha Net, so we can't give any ETA just yet. However, we have just now opened a forum for players to discuss content on Alpha Net: http://hypixel.net/skyblock-alpha
NOTICE JUNE 30, 6:00PM ET - At this time, we have closed down Alpha Net for a patch. It will open up this week as soon as possible. Thanks for the help!
## ► Alpha Net Schedule
Wednesday, June 30th until the release of SkyBlock 0.12
## ► Alpha Net Purpose
We are releasing an update to the [[Dwarven Mines|Dwarven Caves]], nicknamed the [[Crystal Hollows]]. Head over to [[Gwendolyn]] at the exit of the [[Dwarven Village]] to take a trip into the Crystal Hollows! Please note that you will need your Heart of the Mountain to be at least level 2. While we will be locking access to specific ranks at first (starting with MVP++), we will open up to all players eventually as traffic slows down.
Upon joining Alpha Net, your SkyBlock profile will automatically be copied over. Progress will be saved on Alpha Net, but will not be reflected on the Live network.
Alpha Net will be a chaotic experience don't purchase a rank just so you can hop in! Expect the server to restart frequently or even shut down for long periods of time. We are testing things for a reason!
Report all issues and bugs at the usual place - https://hypixel.net/forums/server-bug-reports.35/
Make sure you select alpha.hypixel.net on the Server IP section!
## ► Reward
If you hop on Alpha Net at least once, you will be eligible to release a unique limited edition collectible Pickaxe. Details about this pickaxe will be released later.
## ► Known Issues
We are aware of the following issues, please do not report them:
- A few skins are currently missing as they are still being worked on
- The armadillo pet isn't working properly and will be fixed in the next few days
- The Mole perk in HOTM lvl 6 isn't working properly and should be fixed later
- Items, perks, rewards and mobs may not be balanced yet and will change before release
- Yes, you can mine almost anything in the Crystal Hollows. No, it's not a bug.
## ► Why we are doing this
In the last few months, there have been a few releases that caused a few disasters here and there. We all remember losing a few items to a random inventory or ender chest wipe and Alpha Net is in part a solution to this. By having players hop on Alpha Net with the update released, it will be easy for us to spot wipe glitches and prevent them from ever reaching the Live Network.
We are planning on turning on Alpha Net for all future major releases, for a duration of time-based on the size of the update itself. Minor releases may just come up for a day or two, while massive updates will usually get a week of Alpha Net.
Ultimately, this is an effort from us to improve the process of releasing updates on SkyBlock, as Minecraft inventories can be a really tricky system to mess with.
## ► What's next for SkyBlock?
We have some pretty exciting plans for SkyBlock, and for the first time, I am ready to share a few of those in the form of a simple roadmap.
The 1.0 release of SkyBlock is finally something we are able to see coming soon, but while I can't give any sort of timeframe on when it will come, I can share a few details on what it is, and what needs to release before it.
Planned Content Updates
Please note that any of these updates or plans may change at any time during development.
Dwarven Mines Update - The Crystal Hollows - July 6th (hopefully)
The Crystal Hollows adds unique mining content, a lot of new items and collections, and a new unique way to enhance armor.
New Island tied to the Foraging Skill - Later in 2021
Foraging needs an update as massive as Mining got with the Crystal Hollows. However, what we are currently planning is nothing anyone could ever guess. More details later!
Revamp of an old Classic - Later in 2021
We will be picking up a combat island that is, let's be honest, completely useless at the moment in the current player progression. I'm sure you guys can figure out which it is just by saying this. Please note that there is a chance this revamp is pushed to 1.0, but at the moment we are planning on releasing it before.
We'll be changing this island so much that at this point it is basically a completely new island. It will also be in a different spot in player progression (late game).
SkyBlock Official 1.0 Release - Sometime in 2022
We have MASSIVE plans for 1.0, and compared to the current game, this will feel like an expansion. This update should come with a LOT of content like new islands and features, which we will provide more information about in the next few months, as well as a complete rebalance of the entire game. The game will leave the Prototype Lobby at that point.
Please note that development is at an extremely early phase for 1.0, and we have no clue when it will be released. If you get too hyped and we delay this to 2023, don't say I didn't warn you.
After 1.0 Release
For any content that comes after 1.0, we are considering the possibility of using recent Minecraft version releases to add even more content to the game. This is an idea we are still exploring, and may not necessarily become a reality (as it would force players to play SkyBlock on the most recent version of Minecraft only), but it is on the table. Think new monsters, better UI, highlights, new blocks etc.
## ► Wait a minute, did you just write complete rebalance of the entire game with 1.0 release?
Oh that, yeah, I did just write that.
There has been a lot of controversies around recent nerfs and I'd like to address them. First, major nerfs don't come to the game without a lot of thought, we don't just throw them in. All the game designers sit down and figure out if it's a good idea or not, then one dev makes it happen. Blame shouldn't fall on the person who released it, if there is any to be given.
Ultimately, when we nerf content or items in the game, we do it for the health of the game as a whole. As an example, the Strength nerf was necessary, as player damage was reaching ludicrous amounts in some edge cases. Everyone got hit by it, but ultimately, the entirety of the game kept the same difficulty with some rare scenarios requiring maybe one more hit or two.
Now, what's this about a rebalance? As some of you must have noticed, there is an insane discrepancy between the top 1% of players and the rest. Balance in SkyBlock combat is all over the place and not every item makes sense. For the 1.0 release, we will be revisiting every aspect of the game and changing numbers everywhere. It's a lot of math.
The reason for this is the corner we pushed ourselves into for combat content releases. When making new end game combat content, we're at a point where if we make it challenging to end game players, mid game players can't even consider trying it, while the min-max top end player can just come in and one shot that the whole thing. If we balance content around this top player, then only 1% of the player base even stands a chance.
While everyone's first impression of the rebalancing will be that they are hitting less hard than before, enemy health will be lowered to follow this change. We're hoping most content will keep the same "difficulty level", but without having to reach billions of damage per hit. At the same time, we're hoping to buff items, classes, and playstyles that have been left behind, and early game items should be less worthless than they are now.
Please note that we will be conducting a lot of testing sessions with Alpha Net for this new rebalance to make sense at various stages of its development.
## ► All I hear is SkyBlock, what about my favorite games on Hypixel?
Alpha Net is sadly only for SkyBlock, due to the nature of its economy and persistence of inventories. However, I still come bearing good news, as we do have plans to update older games on the network. Bed Wars is doing absolutely amazing in popularity and will be getting an update before the end of the year, maybe (MAYBE) even before the end of Summer. While other games have not been doing as great, there is a few that deserves some attention and we will be updating them soon too. I can't tell which games yet, nor can I say in which order, or when. There's no point in making promises if we don't know when we can keep them.

# Changelog/2021/April 20
Categories: 2021 Updates, Updates
PATCH
[April 20] Backpacks Revamp
Updated: April 20, 2021
We've all been waiting for this for a very long time, and today we are finally unveiling the [[Backpack|Backpacks]] Revamp. Thank you everyone for your patience!
Note: Some mods that try to read backpack data may crash your game following this update. Make sure you inform your friendly neighborhood mod developer if your mods crashes your game!
5:58pm - Disabled the new storage temporarily as we investigate the mysterious case of the two extra not saving rows
7:18pm - And we're back, hopefully for the long run. A fix is being rolled and storage is about to be open again. (this may take an hour or two)
11:27pm - Sorry for the delay! Backpacks are enabled again. Let's pray nothing bad happens this time.
### ► Why Revamp Backpacks?
The previous implementation of backpacks was great, we had no issues with it on a gameplay perspective. Sadly, due to the nature of Minecraft, inventory management on the server side is a hell of a pain.
Dupes, Exploits, even server crashes came from the previous implementation of backpacks, to the point where backpacks had to be disabled on a daily basis. As of right now, backpacks have been disabled 15 days!
There comes a point where enough is enough - players deserve to have access to their inventories without having to constantly block access to it.
### ► Storage
The way we have decided to implement backpacks is through an already existing system you all know as the [[Ender Chest]]. While it has its own issues, they are very far from the headaches of backpacks. The first thing you'll notice upon logging in is the Ender Chest having been renamed "[[Storage]]".
The Storage is accessed the same way the Ender Chest is accessed, but has been drastically expanded.
The Ender Chest pages works exactly the same as they always did - you can pick their icons as always, and they are unlocked the same way as before. However, on top of this, you can equip up to 18 backpacks. This is subject to change - don't get rid of your extra backpacks right away, as we may add more in the near future. (emphasis on may)
The top row is a menu - you can swap to other backpacks with it.
Sadly, this does come with an unexpected nerf, and the top row of backpacks had to be made into menu buttons. As such, Jumbo Backpacks now only holds 45 items, down from 54. We apologize for the inconvenience.
### ► Legacy Backpacks
But what about your current backpacks, and the items in them? Simply pick your backpack and drop it into the new storage, and it will be converted, causing the backpack to be added to your storage along with it's content. To remove a backpack (to make space to convert more backpacks), simply empty a backpack then right click it to remove it from storage.
For legacy jumbo backpacks, since they are losing one row, the extra items will be sent either to your inventory, or to your stash for later retrieval.
### ► Future Plans
We know this iteration of Backpacks comes a little barebone. For this reason, here's a few things we'd like to add in the near future.
- Shortcut Items to specific pages
- Optional Backpacks shortcut on the 8th hotbar slot or right clicking menu star
- Rename your backpacks/enderchest pages + slash command to go straight to named pages
- Add more backpack slots
- API support
We've worked hard and as quickly as we could to make this backpack revamp a reality, but please keep in mind that we are releasing it as is. While extensive testing was done to ensure we didn't run into any issues, or create new exploits, once this is released to the public, you can expect that more issues may come out. We'd like to apologize ahead of time if we end up disabling Storage in the next few hours/days, however we will be doing our very best to ensure this doesn't need to happen.
@_onah - Lead Design and Development
@TheMGRF @Plancke - Additional code and development
@Sylent, @Bloozing, @RapidTheNerd, @Cham, @Citria, @Magicboys (QA Team) and HPC - Testing/QA
Remember that this list is not exhaustive. Developers on these lists are often working on multiple projects.

# Changelog/2021/April 21
Categories: 2021 Updates, Updates
0.11.4
SkyBlock 0.11.4
Updated: April 21, 2021
Hey Guys!
SkyBlock has now been updated to version 0.11.4. This update brings a variety of new and revamped content. By the way, did you know backpacks were back?
In this update:
- Revamped Farming Islands
- Catacombs - Master Mode Floor 6
- Bug Fixes and QoL Changes
## ► Farming Islands: Revamped
Our first order of business are the Farming Island. The first thing you'll notice is that both the Barn and the Mushroom Desert are on the same server.
### The Barn
The barn hasn't changed much. We have added a lot more crops and increased the respawn rate of them, and there is a new quest in the windmill with the goal of adding a vendor on the island.
### The Mushroom Desert
Sometimes desert, sometimes caves, sometimes there are mushrooms, sometimes there's an oasis, the new [[Mushroom Desert]] island is a complete revamp of the old one - complete with all old collections, a new way to get sand, and a lot of new quests.
The Mushroom Desert is Farming peaceful content, you will not find an overpowered boss or any sort of danger here (unless you can't swim or fly).
- Hunt various animals with [[Trevor|Trevor The Trapper]].
- Look for treasures with the [[Treasure Hunter]]. Some people's parents aren't very original when naming their child.
- Help [[Jake]] gather animals into his house (he really needs to move somewhere safer).
- Or just relax at the new oasis and do some fishing.
Take a breather from all this stressful combat and come check out the revamped farming islands!
## ►Catacombs - Master Mode Floor 6
It is finally time to challenge MASTER [[Sadan]].
[[The Catacombs - Floor VI|Floor 6]] has been balanced to be extremely difficult - it is a pretty hefty bump in difficulty from Floor 5.
Along with Floor 6, we're adding a variety of fixes and balance changes:
- Reduced the amount of mobs in [[Catacombs|Master Mode]] Thorn fight, but made them stronger. This should help with the lag.
- Added a more obvious visual clue during the Livid Boss fights, so players can tell which is the real one.
- Master Mode Livid now deals less damage at the start, but will gain more damage than before when the wrong ones are killed.
- Monster loot in Master Mode will now be stronger than in regular mode.
- [[Berserk|Berserker]]'s passive "Lust for Blood" is now half as effective when there is more than one berserker. Berserkers are notorious for being selfish in their lust for blood.
- All master mode floors will now have the same stacking completion EXP bonus as Regular [[The Catacombs - Floor VII|Floor 7]].
- All 3 mage goggles have been nerfed to account for the stats they gain from stars. This makes them just as good as before, but not for free.
- The Wither Shield ability has been changed to only reduce 10% damage (down from 30%), and the critical damage scaling has been reduced to 150% (from 200%). We still think it might be too good, but will be monitoring.
- Changes to Sadan Boss Fight - Killing Terracotta now reduces the survival phase by 1 second.
- Mana Potions now increase mana regeneration by 1 mana per second, per level of the potion.
  - This change came out last patch, we just thought we should mention it now since we forgot last time.
## ► Other bug fixes and QoL Features
This update also comes jam packed with bug fixes and QoL features. There is a lot, so buckle up.
### "Very Nice and Convenient" Tier
- Added the ability to shift click in quick crafting to craft stacks
- Added an enchant to Anita to prevent breaking stems and not fully grown crops
- Tuning Fork - allows Note Blocks to be set to a specific note value. There's also a new harp song!
- New Winter Sack - It can hold white, green and red gifts, as well as snow blocks, snowballs, packed ice and more. You can acquire the sack from the [[Jerry's Workshop|Jerry island]].
- Show the pet milestones for Mining and Fishing in the appropriate skills
- Added "Show all Stats” as a profile option while viewing your stats (and added back [[True Defense]])
- Added Ability Chat in personal settings to hide ability damage from your chat
- Added a “Go Back” button in the view menu after creating an auction
- Added the option to pick up a stack from sacks by right clicking on the item.
- Added regular Blacksmith functionality to the [[Reforge Anvil]]
- Century Cakes now show their remaining cooldown when you click them
- [[God Potion|God Potions]] now go in the Potion Bag again
- God Potions in the tab list has been updated
- Added an option to fill the quiver when buying arrows
- Added a buy confirmation from NPCs (especially Ophelia) so you stop making expensive mistakes. (Toggleable)
- Added Magical Lava Bucket to Netherrack (and [[Enchanted Netherrack]] to the game)
- Added a chat message when buying an item from a shop
- Added Skill Average and Oldest Profile Age to social display (right-clicking other players)
- Dungeon Rewards rare drops are now broadcasted to the party to improve jolly celebration
### "That's Pretty Cool my Dude" Tier
- Added [[Aspiring Leap]]
- Added a [[Weather Node]] - maintains a weather (rain / lightning) on your island, in the Prismarine Shard collection.
- [[Krampus Helmet]] can now also be equipped to a minion to generate Red Gifts
- Add the ability to star up dungeon floor boss heads with diamond or gold [[Essence]]
- Added Special Bazaar for iron man players who'd like to purchase [[Booster Cookie|Booster Cookies]]
- Added [[Smite|Smite VII]] as an RNGesus drop from T5 Revenant
- Changed "Candy Sack" to "Spooky Sack" and added Pumpkin Guts, Ectoplasm and Werewolf Skin to it
- Added Raw Beef, Rabbit’s Hide and Rabbit’s Foot to husbandry sack (and enchanted sacks)
- One for all weapons can now be enchanted with Telekinesis
- Added a chat message when the auto-recombobulator does its thing
- Add support for currency suffixes (K, M, etc.) to bazaar Sign UIs
### "Sure that's Kinda Neat" Tier
- Added “[[Experiment the Fish]]” to the experiments rewards
- Now revealing all pairs at the end of Superpairs, and fixed the Superannoying Superbugs
- [[Rancher's Boots]] can now have a speed value under 100
- [[Dwarven Mines]] Raffles - will no longer show the winner roulette when you did not participate
- [[Midas Staff]] & [[Midas' Sword|sword]] minimum price for BIN has been lowered
- Increase the spawn rate for [[Howling Cave]] and the Hub Crypt mobs
- Changed the icon of Refined Titanium
- Added brown mushroom to agronomy sack (and enchanted sacks)
- Digging for griffin burrows now checks for 2 air blocks instead of one to avoid suffocating mob spawns
- [[Digested Mosquito]] and [[Scythe Blade]] can now be clicked to view what they craft into
- Changed the icon of the Ability Damage stat to ๑
- Reformatted Adaptive Blade’s lore to fit on a 1080p screen
- Added more fame ranks
### "Bazaar Cleanup" Tier
- Merged Flint & Gravel
- Merged Red & Brown [[Mushroom|Mushrooms]]
- Merged Wheat & Seeds
- Merged Chicken & Feather
- Merged all Dwarven-related materials into Dwarven Materials
- Added [[Treasurite]] to Dwarven Materials
- Added [[Dwarven Super Compactor]] to Compactors
- Added Power Crystal to Fuels
- Added [[Magma Bucket|Magma]] and [[Plasma Bucket|Plasma]] Buckets to Fuels
That's everything for this patch. That was a lot of reading (or typing if you're me).
@Dueces - Farming Island Revamp, Lead Design/Development @Minikloon - Various features and fixes @Nitroholic_ - Dungeons Floor 6 + Balance changes @Relenter - Something cool @aPunch - Fixed the hub village after the Dante uprising
@BlocksKey @fudgiethewhale @AdamWho @Cheesey - Building the map @LadyBleu - Many amazing skins for items
@Sylent, @Bloozing, @RapidTheNerd, @Cham, @Citria, @Magicboys (QA Team) and HPC - Testing/QA @sfarnham, @xHascox, @Jayavarmen and I - Game Design Team / Production
Remember that this list is not exhaustive. Developers on these lists are often working on multiple projects.

# Changelog/2021/April 30
Categories: 2021 Updates, Updates
PATCH
[April 30] Farming Island Follow-up Patch
Updated: April 30, 2021
Hello!
Following 0.11.4: https://hypixel.net/threads/skyblock-0-11-4.4145813/
We are making the following changes:
- [[Trevor|Trevor the Trapper]]'s cooldown is now 1 minute (from 5 minutes).
- Reenabled general elections.
- Events are reenabled.
- Added some passive music to the [[Spider's Den|spiders den]], [[The Farming Islands (disambiguation)|farming islands]], dragon fight and [[Dwarven Mines]].
- Fixed some minor bugs.
- [[Rat Pet|Rat Pets]] are back. Their ability has been buffed slightly
  - Now works on Mining Materials and [[Summoning Eye|Summoning Eyes]]
  - Cheese Buff now lasts up to 60 seconds and chance for loot is now 25% from 20%
- [[Bait Ring]] now shows the recipe for [[Spiked Atrocity]]
- Added 2 new types of drops from Master mode chests:
  - Known issues: The current item skins are incorrect, and will change in the near future.
  - Master Stars: Drop from Master [[The Catacombs - Floor III|Floor 3]] and up, each star can add an extra star to [[Dungeon Items]], up to 10. But those extra stars only apply to master mode.
  - Master Skulls: Upgradable accessories, dropping from every master floor. Each buff your HP and Strength while in Master Mode.
- Master Mode [[The Catacombs - Floor V|floor 5]] exp is nerfed from 75k to 70k.
- Wither Impact cost is now 300 (from 250), and Ultimate Wise will now correctly show the real mana cost.
- [[Armor of the Rising Sun|Rising Sun]] gear is now back to a weaker state.
- [[Damage Calculation|Sword Swapping]] should now be fixed. This may have some huge implications and drastically change the meta, so we will be monitoring and buff items/classes accordingly.
General Bugfixes
- Fixed a bug where portals to the [[The Park|Jungle Island]] and [[The Park|Dark Thicket]] had swapped names
- Fixed a bug where you couldn’t cycle through the skills menu
- Fixed an issue where the [[Hoe of Great Tilling|Hoe of Tilling]] could be used on [[Dirt]] with blocks above it, allowing certain blocks (like doors) to be broken
- Fixed “Caught the Grinch” [[Achievements|achievement]] not being awarded properly
- Fixed “Frozen Monster” [[Achievements|achievement]] not being awarded properly
- Fixed [[Enchanted Bone Meal]] not fully growing some crops
- Fixed an issue where the [[Training Dummy|Enderman Training Dummy]] would sometimes teleport away
  - The Enderman dummy will now also take damage from arrows
- Fixed an issue where the teleport pad from [[The End]] to the [[Spider's Den|Spider’s Den]] spawned players close to the edge
- Fixed Aspect of the End being able to teleport you to the bottom of the world or into weird places
- Fixed Bridge Eggs and Stone Platform Eggs looping through the world on [[Private Island|private islands]]
- Fixed several Collection rewards showing “Unknown upgrade” (it will now show the proper reward)
- Fixed some bugs where players were able to place [[Minions]] as heads
- Fixed an issue where you were able to place items in the Showcase that weren’t supposed to be showcased
- Fixed an issue where you could accidentally place “The Cake” and lose it
- Fixed several Collection reward previews showing as “COMING SOON”
- Fixed a bug where you could increase collection XP infinitely using furnaces
- Fixed a dupe involving social displays
- Fixed an issue where using the [[Builder's Wand|Builder’s Wand]] with an [[InfiniDirt™ Wand|InfiniDirt Wand]] used both dirt in your inventory AND deducted coins
- Fixed an issue where the [[Builder's Wand|Builder’s Wand]] sometimes took too many blocks from your inventory
- Fixed an issue with the [[Builder's Wand|Builder’s Wand]] not working on upper slabs and some stairs
- Fixed not being able to use the [[Block Zapper]] on dirt placed with the [[InfiniDirt™ Wand|InfiniDirt Wand]]
- Fixed an issue where players would lose [[NPC]] eggs if they tried to place one on their island at max capacity
- Fixed an issue where sometimes players weren’t able to place [[Teleport Pad|Teleport Pads]] when close to the limit
- Fixed Teleport Pads still teleporting after they have been removed
- Fixed an issue where players could use Builder’s Wands and Ember Rods as furnace fuel
- Fixed [[Repelling Candle]] breaking when placed on some blocks
- Fixed a bug where you could access trades before unlocking them
- Fixed an issue where you could set your private island spawn in the void
- Fixed an issue where you could set guest spawn in the void
  - Added an option to reset guest spawn by using /setguestspawn reset
- Fixed [[Personal Deletor 4000|Personal Deletor]] not working with Telekinesis
- Fixed a bug that allowed Ironman players to access the bazaar in dungeons
- Fixed a bug allowing players to access the bazaar as a spectator
- Fixed the Builder’s Wand being able to suffocate players
- Fixed the Builder’s Wand causing players on higher versions to be kicked sometimes
- Fixed a couple typos
That's it! Hope you guys enjoy the revamp!
@Nitroholic_ @aPunch @Dueces @Jayavarmen - Improvements and tweaks
@Brandonjja - General Bugfixes
@ShinkoNet - Noteblock Music

Regarding Disabled Backpacks

Hey guys!
We ran into some pretty big issues with [[Backpack|backpacks]] in the last few weeks, and it does include today. We've reached our "last straw" and have to disable [[Backpack|backpacks]] for a little while.
We understand that this means a lot of your items will be impossible to access, and we sincerely apologize for the inconvenience, but please understand this is necessary for the overall health of the game's economy at this time.

# Changelog/2021/August 13
Categories: 2021 Updates, Updates
PATCH
[August 13] Item Drops + Tiny Patch
Updated: August 13, 2021
Quick update!
Item Drops Today we're making a small update to the dropping mechanics in order to prevent abusive trading that we've been seeing. You will no longer be allowed to drop items to non-coop members. These changes are based on feedback from the community regarding items being dropped to them that they did not want, as well as combating IRL trading and boosting in general. We've had multiple instances of players being concerned about receiving an item they did not agree to receive, thinking that they may be banned for boosting or IRL trading themselves. Through your feedback, we believe this is the best route going forward in order to put those concerned about receiving duped or otherwise illegitimately obtained items that they did not ask for. You will receive the below alert when you first try to drop items to other players.
That's about it for item dropping, pretty self explanatory. We do understand that this may impact a few legitimate trades, and we'll be monitoring this change as we go to make sure we're not impacting legitimate gameplay.
Tradeable items Jacob's items are now tradeable (through /trade). Rend Updated [[Rend]] to avoid damage exploits with other items:
This means:
- [[Rend]] now only takes into account crits from the Rend bow itself
- [[Rend]] only counts the shots on the mob which is being Rended
Other Made these items sellable to NPCs:
- All [[Reforge Stones]]
- Slayer tokens
- [[Runes]]
- [[Wishing Compass]]
- [[Ascension Rope]]
- [[Charcoal]]
- [[Superboom TNT]]
Thank you!
~ Frozen Note: This patch should be live within 1 hour of this post.

# Changelog/2021/December 16
Categories: 2021 Updates, Updates
PATCH
[December 16] Some more bug fixes
Updated: December 16, 2021
Hey all!
Yesterday we rolled out a backlog of fun little bug fixes and improvements and we just wanted to make you aware of some of the changes.
### Fixes
- Reworked how the Island Out of Bounds system works (teleporting you back when you go far away from island spawn).
- Added a lag detector to player [[Trading]].
- Fixed client crashes with the [[Griffin Burrows]] and [[Tuxedo]] armor on newer versions.
- [[Pets]], [[Wardrobe]], and [[Sacks|sack]] contents are now [[Co-op]] salvaged properly.
- Fixed some [[Fairy Souls|Fairy Soul]] counters not displaying properly.
- Fixed errors with the Frost menu outside of [[Jerry's Workshop]].
- Fixed a bunch of [[Ironman]]/[[Bingo]] transfers.
- Removed the "NEW" tag from [[Crystal Hollows]] references.
- Made several changes to the following enchantments so they should work properly now:
  - [[Enchantments|Chance]]
  - [[Enchantments|Looting]]
  - [[Enchantments|Luck]]
- Reworked how [[Enchantments|Thunderbolt]] and [[Enchantments|Thunderlord]] enchantments were handled to avoid double deaths and inconsistencies between the two.
- Fixed a [[Crystal Hollows|Crystal Hollows Nucleus]] re-roll exploit.
- And finally, we've added a "Physics Limiter" to private islands to help with performance.
## Notes on the Physics Limiter
This bit gets a bit wordy so buckle in.
#### What does this mean?
Basically, when some events happen in Minecraft they are treated as "physics events" such as [[Sugar Cane]] breaking above the block you broke or signs popping off of walls. Whilst this feature is fine in normal Minecraft we have found several issues where they have caused lag spikes on servers or been abused for nefarious acts. Due to this we will now be limiting how many of them can happen at any given time.
#### What to expect.
Certain blocks such as [[Sand]], [[Sugar Cane]], [[Cactus]], etc. will now have their physics events cap out when a large number of them are broken in the same time period. This may result in some floating blocks but means that the server is more stable as a result of it.
Overall our goal with this change is to improve server performance and prevent weird issues that can occur in some edge cases. We believe that for the most part general players will not notice this change since it is specifically designed to work well with farms. That said, we will constantly work to tweak the values we use to make sure it isn't affecting any real gameplay.
This change has been on the alpha network and now on live for around 24 hours and we've seen no complaints leading us to believe it is an effective change thus far.
And that's it for today! Thanks again for all the bug reports.
@TheMGRF, @TheBirmanator, and @skyerzz - Bugs

# Changelog/2021/December 17
Categories: 2021 Updates, Updates
PATCH
[December 17] Some Dungeon QoL
Updated: December 17, 2021
[January 6] EDIT: The following changes are currently rolling:
- Floor 7 time threshold changed from 12 to 14 minutes.
- Score is now lost a lot slower when above the timer.
Hello,
Today we are releasing a few Dungeon QoL:
- The "First Clear EXP Bonus" will now apply to the first 5 completions of each dungeon floor.
- The first 5 completed runs of each day grant 40% Bonus EXP.
- Fixed a bug with personal deletor not giving credits for secret item founds.
- Added confirmation menu when salvaging items with modifiers such as potato books, ultimate enchants or dungeon stars.
- Healer Milestones are greatly reduced.
Changes to Scoring system We also changed the way score works, mostly regarding secrets and speed.
Based on the floor, you won't need to find all secrets to max out your score.
On floor 1 for example, only 30% of total secrets are needed to max out score from secrets. Here is the full list of requirements:
- Floor 1: 30%
- Floor 2: 40%
- Floor 3: 50%
- Floor 4: 60%
- Floor 5: 70%
- Floor 6: 85%
- Floor 7: 100%
- MM: 100%
This should help with the learning curve of Dungeons.
- On top of that we added the % of secrets found in the tab list, and the number becomes green when you have found enough.
- All secrets found above the limit will be counted as extra Catacombs EXP at the end of the run, at the rate of +0.5% EXP for every 1% above the secret threshold.
To compensate, the difficulty of the Speed Score has been raised a bit Here are the requirements to max out the Speed Score on each floor:
- Floor 1: 10 minutes
- Floor 2: 10 minutes
- Floor 3: 10 minutes
- Floor 4: 12 minutes
- Floor 5: 10 minutes
- Floor 6: 12 minutes
- Floor 7: 12 minutes
- MM: 8 minutes
For our best players out there, those numbers might seem high but we believe this should be more of a challenge for the majority of the playerbase.
That's it for now, please give us feedback on the changes! This is only the first half of our planned Dungeons QoL but the second half requires some technical changes that we are simply too busy to work on at the moment, but you can expect them early 2022.

# Changelog/2021/February 16
Categories: 2021 Updates, Updates
0.11.1
Skyblock 0.11.1
Updated: February 16, 2021
Hey Everyone! Today we have released SkyBlock 0.11.1 on the Hypixel Network.
We are introducing some new Bits Shop items as well as a massive list of Bug Fixes and other improvements.
## ►Bits Shop Items
We have added a variety of new bits shop items, which are now available for purchase.
[[Rune Sack]] - 10,000 bits [[Enchantments|Cultivating Enchant]] - 4000 bits [[Enrichments]] - 5000 bits [[Kismet Feather]] - 1250 bits [[Block Zapper]] - 5000 bits [[Autopet|Pets Auto-Equipper]] 21,000 bits
The [[Autopet|Pets Auto-Equipper]] will feel a bit complicated at first, but play around with it for a while and you will quickly understand how it works.
Spoiler: Rules and Exceptions for the Pets Auto-Equipper
### Rules
- On login
- On gain [Skill] XP
- On enter [Island]
- On enter combat
- On gain [Collection]
- On select dungeon [class]
- On [event] start
- On equip wardrobe slot [#]
- On [boss] spawn
- On starting a slayer quest
- On clicking the experimentation table
- On going AFK
- On digging a griffin burrow
- On opening a winter gift
### Exceptions
- Except if in [island]
- Except if already has [pet] equipped
- Except if during [event]
- Except if has active slayer quest
- Except if it is Thursday
## ► Bug Fixes and other improvements
We have fixed an impressive amount of bugs for this update. Here's the list.
### Improvements
- Removed 640 Shop Limit on [[Golden Carrot|gold carrots]]
- Added TNT and [[Hopper|Hoppers]] to the Mad Redstone Engineer
- Added pagination to [[Fire Sale|fire sales]] history
- The [[Training Dummy]] now has 10 million health
- Added /setguestspawn (places a guest spawn location)
- Disabled [[Golem Hat|Golem Hats]] and [[Chicken Hat|Chicken Hats]] for guests
- Color Codes are now supported for private island NPC names
- [[Plumber's Sponge]] now also removes Lava
- Added [[Enchanted Cactus]] and [[Enchanted Cooked Cod|enchanted cooked fish]] to [[Super Compactor 3000]]
- Changed the rarity of [[Diamond Spreading]] from [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] to [[Rarity|RARE]]
- Upgrades [[Farming]] XP earned from gathering certain items
  - [[Carrot]]
    - Before: 1 After: 4
  - [[Potato]]
    - Before: 1 After: 4
  - [[Wheat]]
    - Before: 1 After: 4
  - [[Cocoa Beans|Cocoa]]
    - Before: 2 After: 4
  - [[Mushroom]]
    - Before: 2 After: 6
### Top Tier Bug Fixes
- Fixed Enchanted Items not always giving the proper amount of collection increase
- Fixed the Enchanting Table+ not having innate bookshelf power (should be 40 now)
- Fixed [[Arrows]] being consumed when using [[Infinite Quiver|Infinite Quiver X]]
- Fixed 'Crafting a [[Saddle]]' [[Achievements|achievement]]
- Fixed [[Overflux Power Orb|Overflux]] recipe showing regular [[Nether Quartz]]
- Fixed [[Ice Bait|Ice Baits]] dropping 1 at a time instead of 16
- Fixed an issue with reloading Islands and Training Dummies
### Neat Tier Bug Fixes
- Fixed not being able to upgrade from Bane 6 Enchantment to Bane 7 Enchantment directly on a weapon
- Fixed chat message when acquiring a [[Chimera]] Book
- Fixed [[Mining Fortune]] lore
- Fixed an issue where players could not complete the intro enchanting quest “Library Card”
- Fixed [[Rancher's Boots|ranchers boots]] trampling [[Pumpkin]] stems, [[Melon]] stems and unplanted farmland
- Fixed formatting of “You have already applied the maximum number of [[Hot Potato Book|Hot Potato]]” message + added sound effect
- Fixed election percentages showing up wrong everywhere except for the hub in the new tab
### Stalker Tier Bug Fixes
- Fixed opening player's profile of nicked players and visiting them to find out who they are
- Fixed looking up well-known players islands (using /visit) to find out which nick they are currently using
- Fixed opening player's profile of nicked players and coop requesting them to find out who they are
- Fixed being unable to view player profiles while guesting on another player's island
### Sad Tier Bug Fixes
- Adjusted [[Drain]] in dungeons (yes, it's nerfed) to be in line with [[Life Steal]]
### ??? Tier Bug Fixes
- Fixed Herobrine camping the top of the Hub's mountain
- Fixed an exploit allowing players to obtain negative stack items
@Minikloon - New bits shop items @william - Bug Fixes and Improvements @_onah - Bug Fixes and Improvements @TheMGRF - Bug Fixes and Improvements @Dueces - Removed Herobrine @Sylent, @Bloozing, HPC and Bugs Team - Testing/QA @sfarnham, @xHascox, @Jayavarmen and I - Game Design Team / Production

# Changelog/2021/February 5
Categories: 2021 Updates, Updates
PATCH
Elephants! (Farming fix)
Updated: February 5, 2021
Hey!
We buffed farming yields through the [[Elephant Pet]].
Here's the history of farming yields:
## Before Jacob
Elephant pet's Trunk Efficiency perk had 20% chance for double-drops. All yield buffs were multiplicative. So +50% from Farmhand and +20% from Elephant was 1.5 x 1.2 = 1.8x more drops.
## Jacob Additive Change
This is from Jacob's Farming Contest patch notes:
### Double-drops Stacking
Because we added many items which impact it, we rebalanced the double-drops mechanic.
All mechanics which increase double-drops (Farming Perk, Elephant Pet, etc..) now stack additively. Essentially, when it says +50% chance for double-drops, it will add +50 to your hidden "double-drops" stat, which behaves like Ferocity. Double-drops above 100 turn into a chance for triple-drops (Just like the Farming level perk).
To compensate for this change, we doubled Elephant pet perk to +60% and Monkey to +50%.
One smol problem: Elephant was buffed, but still multiplicative (that was a bug) The bug didn't get fixed for a long time.
## [[Farming Fortune]]
With the [[Dwarven Mines]], we formalized the "additive douple-drops" with the same behavior as [[Ferocity]] under the label "Fortune". You can get lots of [[Mining Fortune]] from the new island, but we also retrofitted some farming behaviors to use [[Farming Fortune]].
In a patch quickly following the Dwarven Mines, Elephant was switched to Farming Fortune, which caused farming yields to drop significantly.
We're now buffing Elephant back to where top-level players should be seeing about the same yields as a few weeks ago. Newer players with a lower farming level will now see crazy benefits from using the Elephant pet while farming.
Enjoy.

# Changelog/2021/January 15
Aliases: Changelog/2020/January 15
Categories: 2021 Updates, Updates
0.11
Dwarven Mines
Updated: January 15, 2021
NOTICE: You will notice you have Mining Fatigue permanently - this is intended. The debuff exists to ensure our custom mining tech works properly. It is not actually impairing you while mining.
Hello,
A shocking discovery in the SkyBlock universe today, the [[Deep Caverns]] are in fact deeper than we thought! Yes, the [[Dwarven Mines]] are the 7th layer of the mining island!
## Dwarven Mines
The [[Lift Operator]] made a new friend! [[Rhys]] is a dwarf and he will grant you access to the [[Dwarven Mines]]. You can find him in the Obsidian Sanctuary (mining lvl 12 required).
Dwarves were once a great people, at least according to them. They built a great city down in the cavern, filled with shops and various eccentric and borderline cretin inhabitants, who may require your help in various ways.
This very vast layer is filled with Dwarves and [[Goblin|Goblins]], but most importantly: [[Mithril]]!
The rare mineral is much harder to break and can be found in various forms. The center of each vein is pure and rewards more ores. Mithril is known to drive dwarves insane and is useful in various crafts.
The [[Mithril|Mithril Collection]] introduces many new items, including the new Mithril Minion. Among other rewards, [[Beacon|Beacons]] are now officially introduced in SkyBlock.
On top of granting island perks, Beacons also buff the speed of all minions within range and grant stats for all players on the profile, anywhere in SkyBlock. Choose wisely!
The current beacons, which were acquired in various suspicious ways, will lose their effects but will remain collector items.
## Heart of the Mountain
Once being granted access to the [[Dwarven Mines]], [[Rhys]] gives you the [[Heart of the Mountain]]
While in the mines, this menu is available from the [[SkyBlock Menu]], otherwise it can be found in your Mining [[Skills|Skill Menu]]. The HotM currently has 5 Levels and each levels gives you [[Token of the Mountain|Tokens of the Mountain]], those are essentially skill points that you can spend in the skill tree.
Some skills can be leveled up using [[Mithril Powder]], obtained through various ways on the island, but mostly from mining [[Mithril]].
If you don't like your current setup you can always reset your HotM and get all your Tokens and [[Mithril Powder|Powders]] back, for a price!
Skills and Perks from the [[Heart of the Mountain]] affects your mining abilities across all of SkyBlock!
## Commissions
The King and his council
Upon meeting the King in the [[Royal Palace]], [[Commissions]] are unlocked. Completing [[Commissions]] grants experience for your [[Heart of the Mountain]], Mining Skill, and earns you [[Mithril Powder]]. The first 4 [[Commissions]] you complete every day will give a better reward.
As you do more Commissions, you'll unlock [[Emissaries]] around the map, [[Travel Scrolls|Scrolls]], and other rewards! Don't forget to return to the [[King]] or his [[Emissaries]] to claim rewards and start new [[Commissions]]. You can always press Tab to check your current [[Commissions]] progress.
## The Forge
Leveling up your [[Heart of the Mountain]] unlocks [[The Forge]] and new Forge options.
Forging is an art that takes time, and our resident [[The Forge|Forger]] will sometimes take hours or days to finish your items. You start with 2 [[The Forge|Forge]] slots and can get up to 5.
## Changes to Mining
[[Mining]] is now completely custom in SkyBlock and we took this opportunity to do some balancing.
- Pickaxes are now stronger than in Vanilla, but [[Efficiency]] and [[Haste Potion|Haste]] will give less [[Mining Speed]]. In most instances, this should not change anything for players, but it will make scaling more linear.
- The [[Mineral Armor]] stays the same, but won't work on ores that require a high breaking power, like [[Mithril]] and [[Titanium]].
- [[Mining]] now goes up to level 60.
- Officially created a stat for all perks granting chances of double drops: [[Mining Fortune]] - [[Foraging Fortune]] - [[Farming Fortune]].
- [[Mining Speed]] and the various Fortunes are now [[Stats]] and can be seen in your stats menu.
- Mining [[End Stone]] will only give 3 [[Mining]] EXP instead of 5, but worry not! There are many ways to get [[Mining]] EXP on the new island and more to come!
- The [[Cole]] Mayor perks will only reward 1.5x [[Mining]] EXP from now on. It's a sad day.
### A note on future updates
Before we move on to new islands, there are still many things we want to do regarding the [[Dwarven Mines]]. Among other things, we plan on releasing new quests, events, achievements, "Dwarven Caves", and many more features. You can consider it a part 2 of this update.
Part of our 2021 resolution as a team is to be more structured with our content releases. With more and more people working on SkyBlock, it might take a few months but you can expect our production to improve. We will offer more information about releases ahead of time and we will potentially also start giving release dates ahead of times. However, please yell at us if we have to delay releases!
## Credits
- @aPunch - [[Heart of the Mountain]] / [[Starfall|Starfalls]] / Too many things
- @Relenter - [[Drills|Mining Tech]] / [[The Forge]] / [[Mob|Mobs]] / Items
- @TheMGRF & @TheBirmanator - [[Mithril|Mithril Collection]] items and [[NPC|NPCs]]
- @william - [[Pets]] and [[NPC|NPCs]]
- @_onah - Random Events and [[NPC|NPCs]]
- @Minikloon - [[Events]]
- @Dctr - [[Commissions]] / Mobs
- @LadyBleu - Skins and Head Arts
- @Sylent, @Bloozing, HPC and Bugs Team - Testing/QA
- @ShinkoNet - Event Music
- @sfarnham, @xHascox, @Donpireso and I - Game Design Team / Production
- @Vinny8Ball, @DistrictGecko, @Cecer, @fudgiethewhale, @Dueces, @Cheesey, @BlocksKey, @falloutowns, @Plummel, @DEADORKAI, @AdamWho, @carstairs95,
- @mistresseldrid - [[Dwarven Mines]]
Remember that this list is not exhaustive. Developers on this list are often working on other projects.
Thanks for reading!

# Changelog/2021/January 16
Categories: 2021 Updates, Updates
PATCH
Dwarven Mines Hotfix #1
Updated: January 16, 2021
Hey Friends
We've rolled a patch to SkyBlock to address some of the issues players were having with the [[Dwarven Mines]] patch.
- The new custom [[Mining]] tech has been disabled from some islands, including [[Locations|Private Islands]]
Note: We are looking into solutions to alleviate the issues some players are having regarding the new custom mining tech. However, at the moment, we believe it is better to not have it active in some islands where some items are regularly picked instantly so that players with higher ping can still do it efficiently.
- [[Farming Fortune]] has been fixed for all items involved
Note: Farming was inadvertently hit hard by the "Fortunes" stats being added to the game. Farming should now yield the same amount of crops as it used to pre-patch. We might have missed a few more sources of yield multiplication. We'll be adding those as soon as we can.
Thank you for your patience.
