# Changelog/2021/May 26
Categories: 2021 Updates, Updates
PATCH
Upcoming nerfs to Strength Stat & Aiming Enchant
Updated: May 26, 2021
Hello!
In the next SkyBlock update, we will be applying nerfs to two game components which have been in the game since its inception.
- [[Strength]] will no longer provide 20% of its value as base weapon damage.
The (simplified) damage formula is currently as follows:
damage=(5+weapon damage+strength/5)×(1+strength/100)× (1+crit damage/100)×(1+additive multiplier/100)
It will become:
damage=(5+weapon damage)×(1+strength/100)×(1+crit damage/100)× (1+additive multiplier/100)
The current damage function currently has 2 problems in our opinion:
1. Its multiplicative nature leads to a very large difference between low/midgame players and top-end players. This makes it challenging to create new combat content for a vast range of players.
2. The strength conversion + multiplicative features makes it hard for players to gauge which weapon is stronger when looking at two weapons. It's also hard and delicate to tune your stats composition to deal the most damage.
Removing the "strength / 5" will help with both those points. It's not a silver bullet and it will nerf player damage for endgame players, but we think it's a necessary step in the right direction to have a better game for everyone.
- Aiming becomes Dragon Tracer and only works on dragons.
We believe Aiming has been toxic for the game for at least 2 reasons:
1. Players can basically kill every single monsters in area constantly, at the expense of other players
2. It trivializes the point of ranged combat across the game, aka... you don't need to aim anymore
Everyone who had Aiming on their bows will have it converted to Dragon Tracer automatically. We're using this lull in the meta where [[Bonemerang]] is the top non-mana ranged weapon to transition this enchant out of the game.
By the way, here's an upcoming bow:
Easier to acquire than Bonemerang!
Thank you for your comprehension!

# Changelog/2021/May 31
Categories: 2021 Updates, Updates
0.11.5
[May 31] SkyBlock 0.11.5
Updated: May 31, 2021
Hey!
As many of you have been awaiting, we are releasing a new slayer boss for the [[Enderman]] mob.
With this are a ton of items relevant to both this slayer boss and other combat situations like dungeons.
We've got:
- A new standard of strong [[Bows]]
- Multiple [[Support Items]]
- New resource mechanics in the form of [[Soulflow (Mechanic)|Soulflow]] and [[Mana|Overflow Mana]]
- A new [[Voidling Minion]]
There are also some balance changes, so even if you're not interested in grinding Slayer, keep reading!
New Area: [[Void Sepulture]]
New zone to grind [[Enderman Slayer]] in [[The End]]
We wanted a little more space in [[The End]] for players to annihilate [[Enderman|Endermen]]. In the very depths of the The End, outside the Dragon's Nest on the right of the doors (-562, 7, -312), you may find the entrance to the [[Void Sepulture]].
Void Sepulture entrance, requires Combat 25
Inside the sepulture you will find [[Voidling Fanatic|Voidling Fanatics]] which offer way more [[Combat|Combat XP]] than [[Zealot|Zealots]] or regular [[Enderman|Endermen]].
A challenge for everybody
Tier II, III and IV of the Seraph should be able to offer a challenge for players of any kind.
Voidgloom Seraph IV
The boss has three phases, each one stacking the abilities of the previous phases.
In fact there's a few more abilities which didn't fit in the icon, so we added a right-click menu breaking it all down!
► Rewards
There are 18 drops and 24 recipes for this Slayer as well as 2 unique perks.
One of the earlier item ties into the economy and is a new side resource:
-> Create [[Soulflow (Mechanic)|Soulflow]] Eat [[Soulflow (Mechanic)|Soulflow]] is used in abilities and passives of different items:
"Costs 2 Soulflow per 5s in combat"
Once you consume [[Soulflow (Mechanic)|Soulflow]], it becomes bound to your character and is displayed in the Soulflow battery talisman line.
Collected soulflow is displayed on this accessory
► Other Changes
[[Slayer]] Changes
- You may only damage a Slayer boss if you've unlocked it
- Up to 5 damagers will gain XP from slayer minibosses
- Changed price of starting Slayers from 100/2k/10k/50k/100k to 2k/7.5k/20k/50k/100k
- Knockback no longer works on Slayer bosses
- Changed the skill notification in the action bar from (274,308/1,200,00) to (22.9%) format
General Changes
- Removed the base weapon damage bonus from the Strength stat
- Changed Aiming to Dragon Tracing
Read more about these changes HERE
- [[Magma Cube Pet]]: Nerfed 3rd perk from 100% to 50% stats
- [[Wands|Healing Wands]]: Increased duration from 3s to 7s, but doubled their mana cost
- [[Jerry-chine Gun]]: Now 30 base mana, costing +30 mana per consecutive shot. Resets after not shooting for 4 seconds. Turned to single-target damage
- [[Crown of Greed]]: Increased coins cost to 100 coins per damage point
- [[Warden Helmet]]: Changed to 20% damage (additive)
- [[Flower of Truth]]: From 100 dam / 360 str to 160 dam / 300 str
- Changed NPC sell prices:
  - [[Obsidian]]: 12 -> 9
  - [[Ender Pearl]]: 10 -> 7
That's it for today!
Thanks to sfarnham, Cheesey, fudgiethewhale, cheesycommunist, TacNayn, and other SBN members.

# Changelog/2021/November 2/0
Categories: 2021 Updates, Updates
PATCH
[November 2nd] Rune removal and bugfixes
Updated: November 2, 2021
Hello!
This patch brings a new feature, as well as some bug fixes.
Rune Removal The [[Runic Pedestal|Rune Pedestal]] has a new option, where you can remove runes on items free of charge! This process will, however, destroy the attached rune forever.
Bug Fixes
- Fixed an issue where [[Co-op|coops]] would sometimes not gain the correct amount of items in their collection
- Fixed an issue where the collection would go into negatives if more than 2.1 billion items were collected
  - You now can collect up to 9 quintillion items into collections. We're assuming this will be enough for the foreseeable future.
  - Please note: This change may take a few hours to roll out fully. We will update this thread when it is enabled.
  - Players who previously went over the limit and now have a negative collection will be fixed soon.
- Fixed an issue where the teleport ability of wither impact would not work on private islands
- Fixed an issue where items with right-click abilities would trigger their ability when interacting with interactive blocks (chests, furnaces, etc.)
- Fixed an issue where the [[Reaper Armor|reaper set]] would not apply the healing bonus to healing wands
- Fixed an issue where some temporary stat modifiers would not work
  - Most notably is the [[Final Destination Armor|Final Destination]] and [[Reaper Armor|Reaper]] sets' bonus [[Defense]].
Extras
- The in-game day count is now randomized in [[Crystal Hollows]] and [[End Island]].
  - We are aware that it can be annoying if you join a crystal hollows instance when it has almost no time left. To counter this, we are making changes such that you will spawn in crystal hollows instances which have sufficient time remaining before they close. We are also looking at increasing the total time a crystal hollows instance has before it restarts.
- The Melody's [[Harp]] minigame now uses a ping offset so you should be able to get some more accurate scores!

# Changelog/2021/November 2/1
Categories: 2021 Updates, Updates
0.12.1
SkyBlock Patch 0.12.1 - The Museum
Updated: November 2, 2021
Hello,
Do you consider yourself a bit of an archeologist? Do you like roaming museums for hours on end? Well even if you don't, this update is still right for you!
### The Museum
The Museum is located on the right side of the Mountain in the Hub. Once there you can talk to the Curator who is in desperate need to fill his Museum. He's looking for a list of items in particular, and if you donate those items to him, you'll be able to display them on the stands.
There are 4 types of items you can donate: Weapons, Armors, Rarities, and some special items. The first 3 types of items will grant you rewards, while special items are just for bragging rights.
You can already see some of the rewards in the Curator Menu, but currently, the rewards are "coming soon." Due to the nature of some of the rewards, and because we are in the process of changing many parts of the game, we decided to release the Museum first, then later update the Museum with the rewards in an upcoming update.
Every important update from now will add new Museum items and each donation will increase the rewards.
#### Item rankings
Most items in the game have a date of creation, which is the exact moment that the item was created for the first time. Items placed in the museum will display this creation date. If your item is one of the first 1,000 of that type to be created in SkyBlock's history, it will show your item creation ranking above the item when you display it in your museum. Only the 1,000 oldest items will get this line. Eventually this leaderboard will settle as more players donate older and older items, so don't start bragging too soon!
### Soulbound System
Items donated to the Museum are soulbound or co-op soulbound, depending on if you are in a coop or playing solo. Soulbound items are a new type of items that cannot be traded in any way. Once the item is soulbound, it will be yours forever. Along the same lines, co-op soulbound means you can trade it with your coop members, but no one else.
You have to choose carefully before deciding to donate an item to the Museum, as you won't be able to sell it anymore. You will, however, still be able to use the donated item.
Donating any item in the special category doesn't create soulbound items, so you'll still be able to trade those.
The Soulbound system is also there to serve as an item sink, lowering the amount of those items in the economy.
### Eleanor Q. Goldsworth III
The Museum Curator calls her an assistant, but Eleanor works for a much more important person. For the modest sum of 10 million coins, Eleanor will evaluate your Museum and grant you a rank and rewards based on this evaluation.
But once again, most of the rewards are Coming Soon™
For now each of the 5 ranks will unlock bank upgrades, including 2 new ones. If you already purchased bank upgrades, do not worry you still have them.
A brief moment at the top
That's it for now for the update, but we do have a couple other things to talk about...
### Tyashoi Alchemist
Some of you probably noticed the appearance of the new Tyashoi Alchemist in the Village. He will stay there for another 5 days and can reward players with a fun new armor set. This is a one time event, but it isn't anywhere near the scale of the Dante event. We simply felt like doing a community puzzle for Halloween.
After experimenting a bit with the different puzzles we learned a decent amount and now have a better grasp of the type of hunts the community likes. If you enjoy it, he will come back later with much harder puzzles, less guessing, and more deduction. 🙂
### Upcoming Plans
It might come as a shock, but we have been hard at work with updates and R&D lately.
Nether Revamp The next combat island is making good progress and will be the first of the next three major updates to release. The Fishing and Foraging updates will release at a later date.
1.0 and other various updates After the release of the Design Threads, we started working on multiple of the features presented in those threads, such as the Museum, and will be releasing bits of the updates constantly on the way to 1.0.
In fact, the 1.0 update will mostly present things that we didn't even talk about in the design threads: new content. The next step after the Museum is the SkyBlock Level System, Colored Dyes, Rusty's lost and found, Dungeon QoL, and a couple other features we would like to release before the end of the year.
On top of that, we started working on a couple secret updates that we hope to release by the end of the year. No spoilers.

# Changelog/2021/November 29
Categories: 2021 Updates, Updates
PATCH
SkyBlock Bingo Events
Updated: November 29, 2021
Hello,
The Bingo event is coming in hot! On top of the current content we announced, the team wants to work on SkyBlock Events and game modes. Our first major game mode has arrived - Bingo.
## What is Bingo?
A Bingo Card has 25 different Goals. Some goals are as easy as collecting a few resources while some are harder like killing a tough boss. Each month the 25 goals will change randomly so that no two Bingo Cards are the same. 20 of these goals are Personal Goals that you must complete yourself, while the last 5 are Community Goals that can be reached collectively by everyone playing Bingo! Everyone who plays Bingo will receive rewards for contributing to the Goal.
The more you participate in Community Goals, the more Bingo Points you'll gain at the end of the event. Each Goal rewards 1 Bingo Point. Completing a full row, column, or diagonal - getting a BINGO! - rewards you with an additional 5 Bingo Points!
## How do I play?
At the start of every month, starting December 1st, players will be able to talk to Bingo in front of the Map in the Hub.
Bingo Profiles are similar to [[Ironman]], there is no trading, no [[Bazaar]], no [[Auction House]], and you can't even spend [[SkyBlock Gems|Gems]] while playing Bingo. The important thing to remember is that Bingo profiles are temporary - they will be deleted at the end of the event.
During the first 7 days of the month you'll be able to complete as many Bingo goals as you want. Once the event is over your profile will disappear after a few days, but you'll have time to transfer a few items to your main profile in case you found something interesting.
All the Bingo Points that you earn during the event will be stored in your SkyBlock account. You can can spend them on any of your main profiles. Keep in mind that most of the shop rewards are Co-op Soulbound, so spend your Bingo points on whichever profile you prefer!
## Shop Rewards
Once you collected enough Bingo Points, you can spend them in the [[Bingo (NPC)|Bingo Shop]], but some of the shop rewards are only obtainable after reaching higher Bingo Ranks. These Ranks are unlockable in the [[Bingo (NPC)|Bingo Shop]], granting you access to more Bingo Shops items and changing the color of your Bingo profile icon that appears above your head and on the scoreboard.
The Bingo Event is meant for fun, which is why most of the shop rewards are cosmetic. But competitive players will find that there are items fit for showing off their progress. More items will be added for later Bingo events.
## ► Beta Season?
Everything might not be perfect during this first season. Goals could be unbalanced and reward costs may not be finely tuned. We will listen to feedback and try to improve the Event in the future.
If Bingo is popular, we will consider ways to expand the mode, including more types of goals and a co-op version.
## Update on future content:
The Bingo update is indeed one of the secret features we teased in the 0.12.1 thread. Not much has changed regarding our plans since last time and we are still aiming to release a few things before the holidays. It's possible that SkyBlock Levels will have to wait for next year, however.
Most of our efforts are currently aimed towards the Nether Update. With every new island, we always try to up our game and we hope you won't be dissatisfied with this one. We still estimate that it will be finished in early 2022.
That's it for now, have fun!

# Changelog/2021/November 8
Categories: 2021 Updates, Updates
PATCH
[November 8] Bugs, QOL, and more Bugs.
Updated: November 8, 2021
Hey all!
Today we're bringing you a whole weeks worth of bug fixes and QOL updates to all areas of SkyBlock.
## Bug Fixes
- Fixed being able to spam titles with the [[Spirit Bow]] outside of [[Dungeons]].
- Fixed the [[Melancholic Viking]] furnaces taking any type of raw fish, even special ones.
- Fixed a coin dupe exploit with [[Kat]].
- Fixed being able to put rare flowers in Flower Pots, breaking the original item.
- Fixed the [[Hypixel SkyBlock OST|Sky of Trees]] music track not playing on the Foraging island.
- Fixed placing [[Minions|Minion]] skulls on custom tile entities.
- Fixed an issue where items dropped from breaking doors would not stack properly.
- Fixed a bug where falling entities would persist in worlds after players left.
- Fixed [[Frosty]]'s text being cut off in the [[Jerry's Workshop|Jerry Island]].
- Improved the logic of the [[Cocoa Chopper|Coco Chopper]] axe and [[Turbo-Crop|Turbo-Cocoa]] enchantment so they should work better now.
- Fixed a dupe with the Healing and Stun bows in dungeons when the player is a ghost.
- Fixed the [[Crystal Hollows]] spawn protection not working when a player respawns at an entrance.
- Fixed [[Pumpkin Minion|Pumpkin]] and [[Melon Minion|Melon Minions]] not working when offline.
- Fixed Pumpkin and Melon seeds not dropping when broken by hand.
- [[Enchanted Bone Meal]] now works on Grass Blocks and Tall Grass.
- [[Hard Stone]] from [[Hard Stone Minion|minions]] now give [[Mining]] XP.
- Fixed [[Hound Pet]] perk "Finder" increasing all drops.
- Disabled some item abilities like "[[Great Spook Staff|Spook]]" in the [[Dark Auction]].
- Fixed getting 2 of the same dungeons reward items resulting in only 1 being given.
- Stopped eggs hatching [[Chicken|Chickens]] in [[Dungeons]] and [[Dungeon Hub|Dungeon Hubs]].
- Fixed an exploit to get infinite [[Raw Soulflow]] from minions.
- The "Your Skills" icon in the [[SkyBlock Menu]] now finally says "Click to view!" instead of "Click to show rankings!"
- Fixed [[Armor of the Pack|Leggings of the Pack]] and [[Armor of the Pack|Helmet of the Pack]] using the same recipe.
- Updated [[Oringo]]'s message to reference the [[Flower House]] instead of the old [[Colosseum]].
- Fixed Efficient Miner and [[Mining Fortune]] not working on Dwarven Gold.
  - Due to this Dwarven Gold by default now drops slightly less Gold.
- Fixed Efficient Miner treating [[Ores|Redstone Ore]] and Glowing Redstone Ore as two different ores.
- The [[Block Zapper]] now correctly removes "top-half" slabs instead of producing [[Null]] blocks.
- Fixed [[Repelling Candle|repelling candles]] being placeable in hubs on certain blocks.
- Fixed bow runes not appearing on [[Bonemerang|Bonemerangs]].
- Fixed the command that runs on the clickable text when the [[Carpenter]] says that you can now gain [[Carpentry]] experience.
- Fixed an issue where players could dye [[Sheep]] outside of their island, and also with items using a dye material ([[Cake Soul|Cake Souls]], [[Tasty Mithril]], etc.).
- Fixed being able to enchant items in a stack size greater than one in the [[Enchantment Table|enchanting table]].
- Made the failed [[Goblin Raid]] message show the correct number of minutes.
- Fixed items getting lost if warping servers with an item in the cursor.
  - This also means whenever an inventory is closed, the item will go into the inventory instead of dropping on the ground.
- Made [[Rusty]] only accept items in a stack of one.
- Fixed items in storage/backpacks not being salvaged in /coopsalvage.
- Fixed an issue where clicking [[Wardrobe]] slots would delete items on the cursor.
- Fixed crafted [[Spooky Sack|Spooky Sacks]] overwriting the inventory of the previous Spooky Sack.
- Fixed [[End Stone Protector|Endstone Protectors]] leaving behind blocks after dying.
- Fixed [[Ember Armor]] glitching blocks in the [[Obsidian Sanctuary]].
- Fixed an issue with infinitely spawning [[Kalhuiki Tribe Member|Kalhuiki Tribe members]] in their village.
- Fixed [[Tarantula Boots|Tarantula]] and [[Spirit Boots]] working in Dungeon danger rooms.
- Fixed the [[Maddox Batphone]] recipe being incorrect.
- Fixed a bug where rapidly equipping and un-equipping the [[Stereo Pants]] would make the music persist.
- Made improvements to the recent Quick Craft changes so its more responsive.
- Fixed a lot of typos.
- Fixes to warps:
  - "/warp forge" now exists along side "/warpforge" and now actually requires a [[Travel Scrolls|Travel Scroll]].
  - Crystal Hollows now have [[Travel Scrolls|their own Travel Scroll]].
  - Both the [[Travel Scroll to the Dwarven Forge|Dwarven Forge]] and Crystal Hollows Travel Scrolls are obtained from [[The Forge]] as an item cast.
- Added new aliases to warps (/warp <alias>):
Spoiler: Warp Aliases
- [[Private Island]]: "home", "island"
- [[Hub Island|Hub]]: "hub", "village"
- [[Crypts]]: "crypt", "crypts"
- [[Spider's Den]]: "spider", "spiders"
- [[Blazing Fortress]]: "nether", "fortress"
- [[Dungeon Hub]]: "dungeon_hub", "dungeons", "dhub"
- [[Crystal Hollows]]: "crystals", "hollows", "ch"
- Build Fixes:
  - Fixed missing barrier blocks in the [[Obsidian Sanctuary]] and [[Dwarven Mines]].
  - Fixed a hole leading out of the Dwarven Mines.
  - Made the floating [[Colosseum]] [[Fairy Souls|fairy soul]] accessible again.
  - Fixed floating Fairy Souls in the [[Spooky Festival]] hub.
  - Fixed [[Nether Wart]] growing on top of Nether Wart next to the [[Alchemist]] shop.
  - Fixed [[Wheat]] growing on [[Cobblestone]] in the water tank.
  - Fixed a light post in the Winter hub missing some blocks.
  - Fixed being able to [[Ender Pearl]] into the [[Dark Auction]].
  - Fixed holes in the [[Birch Park]], [[Dungeon Hub]], and Winter [[Bazaar Alley]].
- Fixed many head items that should not be place-able being able to be placed.
Spoiler: A lot of items
- [[Arachne's Calling|Arachne's Keeper Fragment]]
- [[Arachne Crystal]]
- [[Arachne Fragment]]
- [[Titanium]]
- [[Enchanted Titanium]]
- [[Refined Titanium]]
- Titanium Alloy
- [[Gemstone Chamber]]
- [[Glacite Jewel]]
- [[Jungle Heart]]
- [[Treasurite]]
- [[Hunk of Ice]]
- [[Hunk of Blue Ice]]
- [[Soulflow (Mechanic)|Soulflow]]
- [[Raw Soulflow]]
- [[Drills|Fuel Tank]]
- [[Redstone Key]]
- [[Wizard's Crystal]]
- [[Plasma]]
- [[Golden Plate]]
- [[Mithril Plate]]
- [[Romero and Juliette Quest Items|Secret Gift for Juliette]]
- [[Blast o' Lantern]]
- [[Power Orbs|All "Orb" items]]
- [[Maddox Batphone]]
- [[Royal Pigeon]]
- [[Braided Griffin Feather|Braided Griffin Feathers]]
## QOL
- [[Melody]] now has her own zone "Melody's Plateau".
- When mining in the farming islands sand is now replaced with sandstone instead of bedrock.
- The [[Chunk]] NPC in the [[Crystal Hollows]] now sells [[Superboom TNT]].
- Added new bazaar categories and items:
  - All [[Goblin Egg|Goblin Eggs]]
  - [[Arachne's Calling|Arachne Keeper Fragments]]
  - [[Soul String]]
  - [[Tasty Cheese|Cheese]]
  - [[Worm Membrane]]
- The Hub and Jerry island no longer uses the custom mining tech (Mining Fatigue).
- Added the player Vault to the Bank in the Jerry island.
- Made numerous changes to items in Sacks:
  - Moved [[Ice]] from the [[Mining Sack]] to the [[Winter Sack]].
  - Moved [[Enchanted Ice]] from [[Mining Sack|Enchanted Mining Sack]] to the Winter Sack.
  - Added [[Enchanted Snow Block|Enchanted Snow]] and [[Enchanted Packed Ice]] to Winter Sack.
  - Added [[Stone]] to the Mining Sack.
- [[Enchanted Bone Meal]]:
  - Now uses the vanilla "green" particles as well as extra custom purple particles.
  - Now correctly uses the bone meal sound effect from 1.17+ or an alternative on lower versions.
- [[Magical Lava Bucket|Magical Lava Buckets]] can now be used as an infinite furnace fuel source.
- Menus like the player trading menu and Dwarven Forge now use the Coin Talisman item textures to represent coins instead of [[Gold Nugget|Golden Nuggets]].
  - Coin items in menus will also change color depending on the amount of coins associated with the item.
- [[Crystal Hollows]] instances will now last longer (5 hours, instead of 2).
- [[Diorite]], [[Granite]], and [[Andesite]] now drop [[Hard Stone]] in the Crystal Hollows.
- The Crystal Hollows will now contain 3 [[Fairy Grotto|Fairy biomes]].
- When entering the Crystal Hollows via a warp (not the Minecart track) it will now put you at a random entrance.
- Crystal Hollows instances will now also prioritize newer instances so you don't join as one is ending.
- [[Arrows|Explosive Arrows]] can now destroy weak walls like Superboom TNT.
- [[Titanium]] is no longer enchanted since it was not visible anyway.
- The [[Block Zapper]] and [[Bazaar]] will now try to prioritize items into sacks over your inventory.
## Temporary QOL
Whilst [[Enchantment Table|enchanting tables]] are disabled due to some fairly large bugs we have made some temporary QOL additions to allow players access to enchanting. These will be reverted once the underlying issue is resolved.
- All players now have access to "/enchantmenttable", "/etable", "/et", etc.
- The enchanting table in the Hub village has been buffed to be max level.
And that's it! Along with many more internal fixes and tweaks we have made that didn't make the list. Hope you enjoy all the new QOL features and bug fixes.
@TheMGRF - Bugs and QOL @TheBirmanator and @Dueces - Additional Bugs

# Changelog/2021/October 13

[October 13] Some QOL and fixes!
Updated: October 13, 2021
Hello everyone!
Here's some nice quality of life stuff we've added in the latest patch.
[[Magical Mushroom Soup|Mushroom Soups]]
- For some reason soups stopped stacking a while back, this is basically down to the fact that we fight against vanilla Minecraft Mushroom soup mechanics. To help combat this, both custom Mushroom Soups (that give flight) now have custom player head textures and stack properly.
Other Fixes

- Added new container checks to the dupe detector, since there were many places players could hide [[Blacklisted Items]] safely.
- Fixed a bug where the main [[SkyBlock Menu]] would constantly refresh itself causing it to "flicker".
- Tweaked our handling of some custom tile entities to help prevent crashes.
That's it for now, enjoy!

I Call That... Additional search settings have been added that allow you to filter groups between a given class level and/or dungeon level range.
On top of that there's now a sort groups section that allows you to organise groups by most recently queued, most players or highest dungeon level requirements!

- [[Sirius]] heard rumors from the SkyBlock underworld and decided to offer the new [[Nether Artifact]] in the [[Dark Auction]]:
## Balance-related Bugfixes
- Fixed [[Power Orbs]] priority. The [[Plasmaflux Power Orb|plasmaflux]] buff will now correctly be applied before [[Radiant Power Orb|radiant]].
- Fixed [[Last Stand]] enchant to grant % defense instead of flat defense.
- Fixed [[Gloomlock Grimoire]] Extreme Measures debuff lasting longer than it was supposed to.
## Other Bugfixes
- "[[Achievements|It Never Ends]]" achievement now procs on [[Ornate Zombie Sword|Ornate]] & [[Florid Zombie Sword|Florid Zombie Swords]].
- Fixed [[Sponge Armor|sponge]]/[[Shark Scale Armor|shark armor]] stacking.
- Fixed some damage reduction talismans stacking. ([[Zombie Talisman|zombie]], [[Spider Talisman|spider]], [[Ender Artifact|ender]], [[Wither Artifact|wither]], [[Wolf Talisman|wolf]])
- Fixed a typo in the lore of [[Spirit Pet|Spirit Assistance]]
- Actually fixed deleting pending [[Co-op]] invites using /coopadd:
That's it! 🙂 PS: At the time of reading this, the patch might still be deploying.

# Changelog/2021/September 13
Categories: 2021 Updates, Updates
PATCH
[September 13th] Crystal Hollows Patch
Updated: September 13, 2021
Hello,
Last week we released a >PSA< regarding some changes to the [[Crystal Hollows]], the patch is released as of today, and the changes are as follow:
1/ The [[Shops|NPC]] sell price of [[Gemstone|Gemstones]] has been nerfed from 8 to 4
- Rough: 4
- Flawed: 320
- Fine: 25,600
- Flawless: 2,048,000
- Perfect: 10,240,000
2/ The Pristine chat message now correctly displays the amount of gems obtained.
3/ Added a price upon removing gems on items, with confirmation
- 1 Coin for Rough
- 100 for Flawed
- 10,000 for Fine
- 100,000 for Flawless
- 500,000 for Perfect
4/ Added a feature inside the [[Geo|Gemstone Grinder]] that unlocks gemstone slots on regular items. Not all items have locked slots, some items have only a few locked slots.
This change would only apply on new pieces of gear, all current armors/gear in the game have all slots unlocked by default. Any new piece dropped after 10:30 EST on September 13th (a few minutes ago), will have potential locked slots.
The full list of gemstone slots and unlock prices changes can be found >here<
5/ Introduce Combat Slots, Mining Slots and Defensive Slots, to break away from Universal Slots. Which allows us to add more slots on items without worrying about their power level skyrocketing.
If you currently have a gemstone in a slot that won’t support it in the future, do not worry, you’ll be able to remove that gemstone for free.
6/ Item lore is now edited to display gemstone slot details: the type of slot, if it is unlocked or not, and the quality of the gemstone inside the slot if there is.
7/ The [[Gemstone Mixture]] recipe is changed to:
- 320 [[Sludge Juice]] instead of 500
- 4 Fine Jade/Amethyst/Amber/Sapphire instead of 2
8/ [[Crystal Nucleus]] Changes:
- Upon completing, new bonus of 800 HotM EXP every time.
- New bonus of 1 Fine Gemstones of each type on top of current rewards
- Add rare rewards
  - 1 gemstone mixture
  - 1 Fortune 4 book
  - Jaderald is approximately 50% more common
- Add very rare rewards
  - Make flawless gemstones a bit more common.
  - 1 new pet item that gives Mining Speed and Mining Fortune
9/ Make the Artifact of power upgrade rarity when you put 7 perfect gemstones on it.
10/ The default rarity of the [[Divan's Drill]] will remain [[Rarity|MYTHIC]]. However, we will officialize a rarity above [[Rarity|MYTHIC]], called [[Rarity|DIVINE]], with its own reforge stats. This rarity will be kept exclusive to items of the caliber of the Divan’s Drill. [[Rarity|DIVINE]] will be added as an AH filter. [[Enchanted Book|Enchanted Books]] cannot be [[Rarity|DIVINE]]. Currently only mining related reforges have Divine stats, because the Divan's Drill is the only legitimate Mythic item.
11/ Add chat toggles in settings to Pristine and Compact messages.
12/ The [[Jungle Pickaxe]] is changed to give 1 Sludge Juice instead, with 3% chance.
13/ Increase the odds of [[Divan's Alloy|Divan’s Alloy]] dropping by 20%.
14/ Change [[Scatha Pet|Scatha]]’s 2nd perk to increase odds of finding regular chests instead. Change the 3rd perk to only work on adjacent hardstone instead of every block. Some extra details on the worm spawning buffs: Currently it checks if the players are in a small tunnel, but the requirements are often too small, worms will now be able to spawn in larger tunnels.
15/ Pristine and Compact enchantements can now be on the same items.
16/ Slight buffs to how many stats the Sapphire, Ruby and Amethyst gemstone slots provide.
Spoiler: Click for details
SAPPHIRE OLD (Intelligence values, per item rarity) ROUGH, 1, 1, 1, 2, 2, 3 FLAWED, 2, 2, 2, 3, 3, 4 FINE, 3, 3, 3, 4, 4, 5 FLAWLESS, 4, 4, 5, 6, 7, 8 PERFECT, 5, 6, 7, 9, 10, 12
SAPPHIRE NEW (Intelligence values, per item rarity) ROUGH, 2, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6 FLAWED, 4, 4, 5, 6, 7, 8 FINE, 6, 6, 7, 8, 9, 10 FLAWLESS, 8, 9, 10, 12, 14, 16 PERFECT, 10, 12, 14, 17, 20, 25
AMETHYST OLD (Defense values, per item rarity) ROUGH, 1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6 FLAWED, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 8 FINE, 3, 4, 5, 6, 8, 10 FLAWLESS, 4, 5, 8, 11, 14, 18 PERFECT, 5, 7, 10, 15, 20, 25
AMETHYST NEW (Defense values, per item rarity) ROUGH, 1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 7 FLAWED, 3, 4, 5, 6, 8, 10 FINE, 4, 5, 6, 8, 10, 14 FLAWLESS, 5, 7, 10, 14, 18, 22 PERFECT, 6, 9, 13, 18, 24, 30

17/ [[Gemstone Crystals|Ruby]] and [[Gemstone Crystals|Jasper Crystals]] will now have a chance to drop from Crystal Nucleus.
18/ Players that have too many [[Heart of the Mountain]] tokens will have those tokens reset! EDIT: nvm didn't work
19/ The description of the [[Jungle Amulet]] is now improved to better explain what it does.
20/ Added a toggle on Heart of the Mountain perks
21/ Fixed a few bugs:
- Fixed a recipe issue with [[Storm's Armor|Storm Armor]] pieces
- Fixed [[Spicy Goblin Omelette]] giving [[Mithril|Mithril Ore]] instead of 20 [[Mithril Powder]]
- Fixed [[Sunny Side Goblin Omelette]] not applying stats correctly
- Fixed lots of recipe items not retaining their item data when crafting
- Fixed the issue where [[Gemstone Sack|gemstone sacks]] would yell at you for having a full inventory all of the time
That's it, thanks for reading!

# Changelog/2021/September 4
Categories: 2021 Updates, Updates
ANNOUNCEMENT
PSA - Upcoming Gemstone Changes
Updated: September 4, 2021
Hello,
Ever since the release of the Crystal Hollows, a larger and larger portion of the total coins income of the game has been from Gemstones being sold to NPCs.
While the total amount of daily coin income didn’t really rise much compared to before, the main issue is that less and less players would participate in the large coin sinks of the game, in favor of gemstone mining.
More coins income and less coins expenditures = inflation.
The changes below are currently aimed for the end of the week, but it is still time for you to give feedback.
EDIT: Thread updated with all the >round 2< changes!
Here is the list of proposed changes:
1/ Nerf the NPC price from 8 to 4
- Rough: 4
- Flawed: 320
- Fine: 25,600
- Flawless: 2,048,000
- Perfect: 10,240,000
2/ Fix the Pristine chat message, currently displaying a lower amount than players actually get.
3/ Add a price upon removing gems on items, with confirmation
- 1 Coin for Rough
- 100 for Flawed
- 10,000 for Fine
- 100,000 for Flawless
- 500,000 for Perfect
Note: Those numbers are meant to be a small fee, after debating values we felt those would not feel punishing. But please give your feedback on that removal fee.
4/ Add a feature inside the Gemstone Grinder that unlocks gemstone slots on regular items. Not all items have locked slots, some items have only a few locked slots. Examples:
- Aspect of the End:
  - Sapphire slot is free, no cost to unlock
- Flamebreaker Armor:
  - Ruby slot is free
  - Amber slot is 5k coins + 1 Fine Amber
  - Jade slot is 20k coins + 4 Fine Jade
- Necron Armor:
  - Jasper Slot: 250k coins + 4 Flawless Jasper
  - Combat Slot: 250k coins + 1 Flawless Jasper + 1 Flawless Sapphire + 1 Flawless Ruby + 1 Flawless Amethyst
This change would only apply on new pieces of gear, all current armors/gear in the game would have all slots unlocked by default.
Note: Once players obtain a full set of Perfect Gemstones, they technically don’t need to obtain new gemstones, as swapping gemstones from an item to a new one doesn’t cost gemstones.
We believe that is completely OK. The Crystal Hollow isn’t meant to be a place where players will perpetually need to invest a lot of time and effort.
However we also value the bazaar price of gemstones to remain high, and if there aren’t any gemstone sinks it might get lower and lower with time.
That is the main reason why those unlock costs are being introduced. Please give us your opinion on those.
The full list of gemstone slots and unlock prices changes can be found >here<
5/ Introduce Combat Slots, Mining Slots and Defensive Slots, to break away from Universal Slots. Which allows us to add more slots on items without worrying about their power level skyrocketing.
If you currently have a gemstone in a slot that won’t support it in the future, do not worry, you’ll be able to remove that gemstone for free.
6/ Higher drop chance of Jaderald
7/ Change gemstone mixture recipe
- 320 Sludge Juice instead of 500
- 4 Fine Jade/Amethyst/Amber/Sapphire instead of 2
8/ Crystal Nucleus Changes:
- Upon completing, new bonus of 800 HotM EXP every time.
- Give 1 of each Fine Gemstones on top of current rewards
- Add rare rewards
  - 1 gemstone mixture
  - 1 Fortune 4 book
- Add very rare rewards
  - Make flawless gemstones a bit more common.
  - 1 new pet item that gives Mining Speed
9/ Add a way to see the gemstones on an item without using addons. (Visible in the stats)
10/ Make the Artifact of power upgrade rarity when you put 7 perfect gemstones on it.
11/ The default rarity of the Divan's Drill will remain Mythic. However, we will officialize a rarity above Mythic, called Divine, with its own reforge stats. This rarity will be kept exclusive to items of the caliber of the Divan’s Drill.
Divine will be added as an AH filter.
Enchanted Books cannot be Divine.
12/ Add chat toggles in settings to Pristine and Compact messages.
13/ The Jungle Pickaxe shall be fixed.
14/ Increase the odds of Divan’s Alloy dropping a bit.
15/ Change Scatha’s 2nd perk to increase odds of finding regular chests instead.
Change the 3rd perk to only work on adjacent hardstone instead of every block.
Some extra details on the worm spawning buffs: Currently it checks if the players are in a small tunnel, but the requirements are often too small, worms will now be able to spawn in larger tunnels.
16/ Make sure Pristine and Compact are compatible on the same item.
17/ Slight buffs to how many stats the Sapphire, Ruby and Amethyst gemstone slots provide.
18/ Ruby and Jasper Crystals will now have a chance to drop from Crystal Nucleus.
19/ Players that have too many Heart of the Mountain tokens will have those tokens reset!
20/ The description of the Jungle Amulet will be improved to better explain what it does.
Thanks for reading, and let us know what you think!

# Changelog/2021/September 7
Categories: 2021 Updates, Updates
PATCH
[September 7th] Weaponsmith City Project
Updated: September 7, 2021
Hi all! After being fully funded, the [[City Projects|Weaponsmith project]] has arrived in the Village!
Notable additions include:
- [[Rosetta]], the early game gear vendor
- [[Jax]], the arrow forger
- 10 new types of [[Arrows]]
- New Target Practice minigame
This content can be found north of spawn by the Weaponsmith NPC.
## [[Rosetta]]
Rosetta's shop is fully stocked with 4 new armor sets and their corresponding weaponry. Each armor set is better than the previous but costs more coins and requires a higher combat level.
This should give newer players a clearer path towards acquiring better gear and introduce them to SkyBlock's combat system much better. It also gives players the option to specialize in either melee or magic gear
## [[Jax]]
Jax has set up shop in the brand new Archery Range beneath the Weaponsmith's house. After proving yourself in the Target Practice minigame Jax will forge players up to 10 new types of arrows.
These arrows have unique effects such as:
- Exploding on impact
- Chance to cause monsters to be stuck in their place
- Ignoring defence
- + more
In order to unlock higher tiers arrows, players must complete multiple tiers of the Target Practice minigame. In this game players need to shoot a set of targets within a certain amount of time. This game is found directly next to Jax, simply step on the pressure plate to begin!
We've also added a new early game Shortbow to the Weaponsmith which may help players improve their time!
Thanks to the players who help test the update on Alpha network as well as @Minikloon for assisting in the development.

# Changelog/2022/January 25
Categories: 2022 AHN Updates, Updates
AHN
SkyBlock Alpha (Jan. 25th, 2022) - Balancing Patch
Updated: January 25, 2022
Hi! You may have noticed recently that we rolled some SkyBlock balancing changes to the Alpha network. Since then, we have gathered feedback and rolled even more changes today. Below is the cumulative list of balancing changes. Let us know if you have any issues while testing these changes on the Alpha Network!
Note that this list is subject to change. Values may be tweaked and additional balancing changes may be made before this is patch rolled live.
Golden Dragon Pet
- Add a base magic find of 10 on golden dragon (so +0.5 every 10 levels, starting from 0)
- The Shining Scale perk grants the magic find and strength to the BASE stats of the pet, not directly to the player (that's an indirect buff)
- Buff "Legendary Treasure" perk of golden dragon from 0.2% to 0.25%
- Show the exact damage boost of the golden dragon legendary treasure, aka use more decimals, it's not very precise atm.
Turtle Pet
- Buff Turtle Tactics from 20% to 30% defense
- Change Genius Amniote to give 30 defense for every player around you, max of 5 players
- Add a legendary perk: Turtle Shell: When under 25% maximum HP, take 25% less damage.
JellyFish Pet
- Change Radiant Regeneration to reduce mana cost of the Power Orbs by up to 50%, instead of the aoe healing. Rest of the perk is unchanged
- Replace Hungry Healer to Stored Energy: Reduces the cooldown of Wish by 1 (0.01 per level) second every 2000 HP you heal whilst in dungeons.
- Change Powerful Potions to: While in dungeons, boosts the effectiveness of Dungeon Potion by 50% (0.5% per level)
Scatha Pet
- The Scatha Pet is now upgradable:
- Rare->Epic costs 125m coins, 64x Enchanted Hard Stone, and 7 days
- Epic->Legendary costs 250m coins, 256x Enchanted Hard Stone, and 14 days
- Scatha pet drop chance is now 4x higher from Scatha mobs
Baby Yeti Pet
- Nerf Ice Shield of the Baby Yeti from 100% to 50% of your strength as defense on Epic Baby Yeti.
- Nerf Ice Shield of the Baby Yeti from 100% to 75% of your strength as defense on Legendary Baby Yeti.
Enchantment Changes
- One For All is now additive damage and not multiplicative, granting +500% damage.
- Made Titan Killer scale from 50 cap to 70% cap between 5 and 7
- Made Prosecute scale from 35% to 40% cap between 5 and 6
- Fire Aspect deals 40% of your total damage per second.
- Venomous now increases damage inflicted on the monster by +0.2/0.4/0.6/0.8/1/1.2% damage per hit, stacking up to 50 hits globally with other players.
- Thunderlord deals 15/30/45/60/75/100% of your total damage when it procs
- Thunderbolt deals 8/16/24/32/40/60% of your total damage when it procs
- Sharpness 5/6/7 now 25/35/45%.
- Lethality 5/6 now 15/20%.
- Ender Slayer 5/6/7 now 60/75/90%.
- Bane of Arthropods 5/6/7 now 40/50/60%.
- Cleave 5/6 now 15/20%.
- Critical 5/6/7 now 50/70/100 Crit Damage.
- Cubism 5/6 now 50/65%.
- Execute 5/6 now 1/1.25%.
- Giant Killer 5/6/7 now 0.5/0.65/0.8%.
- Prosecute 5/6 now 0.5/0.65%.
- Smite 5/6/7 now 40/50/60%
Item Changes
- Gemstone base Sell Price nerfed from 4 to 3
- Nether Wart Sell Price nerfed from 3 to 2
- Infinityboom TNT insta blows up walls and crypts when tnt placed near them
- Maxor Armor Crit Damage increased from 30 to 45 on each piece
- Each Piece of Maxor Armor gets 5% extra arrow damage
- Nerfed End Stone Sword to 50% damage reduction max
- Voidwalker Katana - Receive 3% less damage from endermen when held
- Voidedge Katana - Receive 6% less damage from endermen when held
- Vorpal Katana - Receive 9% less damage from endermen when held
- Atomsplit Katana - Receive 12% less damage from endermen when held
- Final Destination Armor - buffed Strength from 50->100
- Final Destination Armor - buffed intelligence multiplier from 1.25->1.5
- Final Destination Armor - Vivacious Darkness now only procs while sneaking in combat
- Final Destination Armor - Vivacious Darkness now grants +100% damage against endermen
Miscellaneous
- Nerf Wither Skeleton Pet's Wither Blood perk from 50 to 25% damage to withers.
- Nerf Merciless Swipe of the Tiger Pet from 50% to 30% ferocity
- Fishing coins treasures get buffed with fishing level, the max coins at each level are multiplied by (1 + fishing level / 2)
- Doubled the odds to get ultra rare rewards in the experimentation table.

# Changelog/2022/January 6
Categories: 2022 AHN Updates, Updates
AHN
SkyBlock Alpha (Jan. 6th, 2022) - Stranded Mode Additions, QoL, & Bugfixes
Updated: January 6, 2022
Hi! Alpha is again open in order to test Stranded mode! Note that any Stranded mode profiles and islands that you created before will still be there when you join Alpha again.
Big Changes * New Villager trading system. Fully custom trades with a new menu. * Villagers are now persistent mobs. * Name tags and nameable mobs. Naming a mob turns it into a persistent mob. Name tags are dropped rarely from fishing loot.
Other Changes Unless otherwise noted, the following fixes/changes only apply to Stranded mode: * Added several QoL vanilla recipes (bows, hoes, axes) * [[Skeleton|Skeletons]] killing [[Creeper|creepers]] now drop music discs * [[Saplings]] now correctly stack in your inventory when dropped from leaves * [[Redstone Pigman|Redstone pigmen]] now spawn properly when conditions are met * Added 20 cobblestone to the minion when you create a new island * Fix a dupe with [[Cow|cows]] and [[Vanilla Objects/Blocks|lava]] * Changed [[Builder]] NPC to be unlocked when you unlock all collections * [[Zealot|Zealots]] now drop end stone * [[Zombie Minion|Zombie minions]] properly simulate [[Lapis Zombie]] drops when [[Sea Lantern Hat]] upgrade is applied * [[Charcoal]] counts towards coal collection (in all profile modes) * Charged creepers drop diamonds * [[Ghast|Ghasts]] spawn lower in the sky * Added [[Weaponsmith]] NPC * Added [[Rosetta]] NPC * Added [[Elizabeth]] NPC * Added [[Fear Mongerer]] NPC * Added [[Baker]] NPC * Added [[Oringo]] NPC * Added [[Amelia]] NPC * Collection is properly given when fishing enchanted versions of items (enchanted gold, enchanted iron, etc.) * Crops grow faster * Fixed island NPC skill requirements being off by 1 * Tweaked the natural spawning system to use weighted random spawning. Hopefully this leads to a more natural feeling mob distribution. * Removed Oringo warp message that prompts you to go to the Hub. * Fixed [[Trick or Treater]] appearing as adult zombie on newer MC versions (all modes) * Fixed [[Training Dummy]] nameplates appearing as "Armor Stand" when reloading your island (all modes) * Fixed crops that grow via minions not rewarding collection if the seeds were placed by a player * Other things that I've since forgotten!
Original Stranded Mode post: https://hypixel.net/threads/alpha-network-stranded-mode.4709364/
We are getting close to being able to release this mode! Let us know if you find any bugs while testing and, as always, we are open to suggestions for improving this mode if you have any. Enjoy! :)

# Changelog/2022/July 25
Aliases: Changelog/2022/July 25/AHN
Categories: 2022 AHN Updates, Updates
AHN
[Alpha] SkyBlock 0.13.1 - The Hex, Stats Menu, & Shen's Auction
Updated: July 25, 2022
Hello!
We are deploying a patch which we will test over the next 1-2 weeks on the Alpha Network.
#### Upcoming Features
The Hex It's an all-in-one tool to upgrade max out items. It's available on Alpha in the Hub at (-64, 70, -105).
Bazaar Menu Enchantment books and trading essence was added to the Bazaar. Another row was incorporated in the menu, a new category will come to fill it soon. New items were added for certain functionalities included in The Hex.
Stats Breakdown Menu The stats menu(s) available where you're used to has been upgraded to show where all of your stats are from. This menu is still under development and will evolve while on alpha.
New Stats
- 7 Wisdom Stats for different skills, which increase skill XP gain. (For example, Slayer nows grants Combat Wisdom!)
- Fishing Speed
- Health Regen
- Vitality, which boosts incoming healing (good for tanks!)
- Mending, which boosts outgoing healing (good for healers!)
Balancing Changes Some balancing was implemented on existing content. A full patch notes will come with the official release.
Shen's Auction There is a new piece of content for endgame players setup under the Auction House at (-52, 72, -87). Shen sells cool items in a new type of NPC auction where X top bidders earn the item. One of the items open up the Regalia Room, which has a shop for more items and rotating cosmetics.
This update introduces a new auctioning system into the backend and thus we suggest heavily testing and trying to break it.
Note 1: Normally, Shen's Auctions will reset every SkyBlock year (around 5 real days) but on Alpha (for the sake of testing), they reset every SkyBlock day! (Around 20 minutes) Note 2: Normally, you must have certain ranks in the Museum to access Shen's Auction but once again for the sake of testing the requirement is removed and all players can access these auctions.
There is a also a change in the build on the End island. This piece of content is unavailable at this time on Alpha.
#### Backend Changes
We've done a refactor (rewrite) of many systems in the game. The goal has been to increase the rate at which we can ship updates.
Systems touched include, but not comprehensive:
- Upgraded Java runtime from 8 to 17! (this can cause bugs in literally everything)
- Moved a lot of stuff from code to JSON data:
  - Furniture
  - Item Skins
  - Pet Skins
  - Minion Skins
  - Sea Creatures
  - Islands data (like resource areas)
  - Zones (like NPC locations)
  - Objectives
- The Objective system was revamped
- Unified Requirement system (like slayer reqs on items, but across the game...)
- Custom Mobs system
- Feature-toggling system (for example Dojo disabling menus)
- Accessories stacking
- Parkour/Race system
- The Bazaar's client-side (what's on the game server)
A Dev Blog will come post-release regarding the JSON data changes and what it means for the game.
What all these backend changes mean is that bugs may have crept up anywhere in the game. We recommend importing your profile to check if your items/island have unexpected behavior. Then what would be really helpful for us is for you to play 1 hour of SkyBlock as you would normally. Creating new profiles can also find issues nobody would expect!
You can report bugs here: https://hypixel.net/bug-reports/create?server_ip=alpha.hypixel.net Make sure that the Alpha toggle is selected when you report bugs!
Slots may be very limited on the Alpha network, if it's unavailable, I suggest just playing on the live server instead! As usual, the network may open and close arbitrarely.
Thanks and enjoy!

Updated: November 20, 2022
Ladies and Gentlemen! We're opening Alpha today to test some cool new features regarding with the Leveling system.
The goal this time is for you guys to hop on and test out the following features:
- New Leveling sub-menu located in the SkyBlock Menu
- The new Guided Suggestions feature
- The new To-Do List feature
- The new Ways to Level Up menu

At this time we are planning on keeping Alpha open at most for a few hours, as a result, the Alpha network may periodically close and re-open.

If you encounter any issues - small, major or critical, please report them here and make sure you select alpha.hypixel.net!

- Some tasks don't have a maximum XP, meaning they count for an infinite% of your task exp.
- Some quests are listed multiple times in the Story Tasks menu. Those quests have the same name!
- Some menus may look like they have random panes! That's just the formatting! Intentional!
- The Crystal Nucleus and Reaper Pepper tasks report the incorrect amount of maximum SXP that can be earned.
- Currently missing a Spider Essence shop task.
- Talisman objectives are incorrect for Talisman families (such as zombie talisman -> ring -> artifact).
Thank you everyone for reading!

Alpha Testing 11/22/22 - Kuudra
Updated: November 22, 2022
#### Alpha is open again today with changes to Kuudra. The main new features includes:​
- Kuudra boss balancing and bugfixing
- Kuudra Infernal Tier
- Kuudra Party Finder
- Crimson Essence shop
Kuudra, the Party Finder and the new Crimson Essence shop can be accessed on the Crimson Isle in the Forgotten Skull, or via /warp kuudra.
At this time we are planning on keeping Alpha only for today, but please note that if a lot of bug reports come in, we'll have to close early to fix said bugs before we open again.
EDIT: Alpha testing how now concluded. Thank you for testing.
If you encounter any issues - small, major or critical, please report them here and make sure you select alpha.hypixel.net!

# Changelog/2022/October 27
Categories: 2022 AHN Updates, Updates
AHN
Alpha Testing 10/27/22 - The Garden
Updated: October 27, 2022
Hey Guys! We're opening Alpha today with some pretty hefty updates to be tested.
The Garden can be accessed by talking to a Stranger on your private island if you are above SkyBlock Level 5. This is a coop instance, so you can hop on there with your coop friends!
The goal this time is for you guys to hop on and ensure you are able to access the garden and use most of it's features. It's also a good time for you guys to prep plans for the live release in case you want your farms ready as quickly as possible!
At this time we are planning on keeping Alpha at most for a day or two, but please note that if a lot of bug reports come in, we'll have to close early to fix said bugs before we open again.
NOTE: If you are using a mod that hides grass, this will interfere with your plot cleaning duties!
NOTE 2: Please note that some balancing was made to ensure testing was doable in an appropriate amount of time. For example, the Composter will be running a lot faster on Alpha than it will on release.
If you encounter any issues - small, major or critical, please report them here and make sure you select alpha.hypixel.net!
#### Current Known Issues - Please do not report these!
- If a Coop member has unlocked a visitor, it may not show properly in the logbook
- An issue where only one player per coop can complete the tutorial objective with Duke at a time
  - Partially fixed currently.
- An issue where cleaning up a plot may not finish the tutorial objective "Clean your garden plot" until your rejoin the instance
- A lighting issue on some parts of garden plots where some chunks will remain unlit
- Kicking a guest from the Garden will give a broken message
- Island Crystals can be placed in protected areas
- All island crystals look like wheat crystals on the Garden
- An issue where players can skip some tutorial sequences by visiting someone else's garden
- Some Holograms (text on top of items) may be off center. Literally unplayable.
- Some party warp features may not function correctly (such as warping guests or following, or bypassing garden visit settings)
  - It now requires that party members need to have visited the Garden (go talk to Sam!)
- Tile Entities may not paste properly from schematics
- Water and Lava stops flowing when pasted from a schematic
- Sam's name may not disappear properly when she moves to a different location
- Some items cannot be used on the Garden but will not provide an error message yet
  - Should be fixed, but if you find anymore of these items let us know!
- If it is not possible to get a new visitor (as not enough are unlocked), the Next Visitor timer still ticks down. This is a lie, no one will show up.
- Not all coop members are currently notified when upgrades are applied or actions are taken
- You can warp a coop member to the garden even if they have not reached the SkyBlock level requirement
- Lots of oddities with the Builder's Ruler (including null blocks)
- When visitors move forward in their line, they may behave weirdly and their name may not follow properly.
  - They are just a little awkward with each others nothing to worry about
- Slayer quests are not disabled in the Garden.
- Reentering the Fear Mongerer's commission menu changes the candy type he provides.
- Some items have right-click in their lore twice. They're very excited about being right-clicked.
  - They are no longer excited about being right clicked.
- While cleaning a plot, the scoreboard only displays up to 90% completion.
- Sometimes the Stranger shows up in the air and falls forever into the void.
- Trevor the Trapper gives Moogma Pelts instead of normal Pelts.
- Prismapump and Mycelium Dust did not work correctly in the Garden
- You can burn down your barn.
- Melon Dicer & Pumpkin Dicer don't carry over NBT data when being upgraded
- Composter is currently eating fuel and crops but not giving out compost.
Note, 10/28/22: We will be opening Alpha sporadically today as we test a few things here and there!

# Changelog/2022/September 2

AHN
[Alpha] SkyBlock 0.15 - SkyBlock Levels
Updated: September 2, 2022
Hello everyone!
Today we will be releasing an upcoming patch of SkyBlock to the Hypixel Alpha Network.
## ► SkyBlock Levels
Levels is a new way to tie up multiple forms of progression into one Level.
- Your SkyBlock level (along with the ways to gain XP) can be viewed in Your Profile.
- You need 100 XP at any level to level up.
- Your Level is displayed in the tab list as well as in your chat messages.
This update brings other content changes and additions as well, so I suggest you seek it all out and test it! Along with this, I suggest you attempt testing on your profile and creating a new one.
Please note that this update is still being continuously balanced throughout its time on Alpha and all values are subject to change.
You can report bugs here: [1]
Make sure that the Alpha toggle is selected when you report bugs!
Slots may be very limited on the Alpha network! And as usual, the network may open and close at any point.
Thanks and enjoy!

# Changelog/2022/April 1

PATCH
[April 1] Tool Reforges Patch
Updated: April 1, 2022
Hey all.
This update is unrelated to April Fool's I swear.
Proof this isn't April's Fool
All jokes asides it really isn't.
Features
- Added a new set of Tools Reforges available in the Blacksmith.
Breakdown of the new reforges
- Now showing reforges passives on Reforge Stones:
Small Bugfixes
- Fixed /coopadd not checking for co-op invitation settings
- Prevent abilities being used on launch pads when traveling between islands to avoid beginners from terminating their own lives by misclicking
- Prevent players from creating a pet rule too similar to one they already have so they don't turbo-spam the chat when farming wheat and stuff
- Update the Quiver Arrow for more conditions like dropping a bow, picking up a bow, selling a bow...
- Added the Personal Vault button to the solo Bank menu when accessed from the Personal Bank
- Music Pants now properly auto-play when joining a server, but you have to move out of spawn a little bit
- Stop grinches from dropping infinite gifts by healing them
- Prevent guest vehicles (pig pet, etc..) from interacting on islands
- Added a 10 stacks limit to Werewolf armor to avoid abusive cases
- Fixed the Deep Storage achievement
- Fixed the bazaar misreading your inventory being full when claiming a buy order. Another solution is underway regarding stash being full
- Stopped Kalhuiki Door Guardian from scamming keys when the door is already open
- Fixed Obsidian Sanctuary diamond blocks not increasing diamond collection
- Updated the Farming Talisman to make it work in the updated mushroom desert
- Prevent fished coins from being grabbed by hoppers to create null skulls
Smaller Bugfixes
- Renamed an NPC who was named Terry to Tony
- Changed delimitations of the Pigmen's Den and Lapis Quarry to avoid the wrong blocks being generated in the wrong zone
- Now clearing the personal compactor filters when auctioning to avoid nasty fish surprises when buying one
- Remove the enchant glint when removing all enchants from an item
- Fixed Aspiring Leap cooldown
- Fixed bone meal not dying default leather armor
- Disabled Mooshrooms exploding from shears and producing vanilla mushroom stew
That's it for today, more to come!
Note: The update is currently being rolled out and not all fixes might be live yet.

# Changelog/2022/April 18
