[April 18] QoL Bug Fixes
Updated: April 18, 2022
Hey all!
My name is Landon and I am a new Minigame Developer on the Hypixel Network. The past few weeks, I have been working on fixing some of the more minor SkyBlock bugs, so here is a run down of what we've fixed.
### ▶ Bug Fixes
- Message received on earning pelts is now grammatically correct.
- The Spooky Festival Leaderboard message no longer cuts off rewards at the bottom.
- Fixed NPC Jamie providing false information.
- Arachne's Fragment's placing message is now correct.
- Barry's Mayor Perks were formatted incorrectly.
- Slimes are now referred to as the proper name across the game.
- Fixed multiple messages prompting the user to visit the hub while on a stranded profile.
- Fixed the Horseman's Candle prompting the user to use the item in the hub while on a stranded profile.
- Fixed several Auction House message inconsistencies.
- Fixed many plurality errors in messages.
- Fixed a bug when a player would use the auction, their co-op members would be shown their nicked name.
- Fixed grammatical inconsistencies when there are more than 2 island members.
- Fixed a bug where the Travel Scroll for the Dwarven Mines and the Dwarven Forge would send the same chat message.
- Fixed multiple inconsistent chat colored messages.
- Fixed the MVP++ description in the Community Shop being identical to MVP+
- The Auto Recombobulator now sends the correct chat message.
- Updated the message send explaining potion effects sent by specific NPCs.
- The message sent when removing aPunch Century Cakes is now grammatically correct.
- Fixed more chat color inconsistencies.
- Fixed certain messages sending multiple + symbols when receiving rewards.
- Fixed a bug where the Bazaar 5 stack option would be for 160 items not 320.
- Fixed Bazaar menus saying Buying when the user is Selling and vice versa.
- Fixed certain co-op related messages having multiple spaces.
- Fixed inconsistency in messages relating to Magic Find (between Kill Combos and Rare Drops)
- Fixed spelling inconsistencies when playing on an Ironman profile.
- Fixed multiple typos in the lore of Minions.
- Fixed a typo relating to the Crypt Ghoul.
- Fixed the Tarantula minion saying "Cave Spiders" instead of "Tarantulas".
- Fixed typos relating to the Training Dummy.
- Fixed multiple formatting issues with dialogue sent from the Joyful Viking.
- Fixed outdated information related to Salvaging (it now accurately displays what is collected)
- Fixed messaging inconsistencies when attempting to gift to a co-op member.
- Fixed multiple dialogue typos with NPC Jake.
- Fixed lore typos with the Great Spook Staff Ability.
- Fixed Cult of the Fallen Star having extra spaces in dialogue.
- Fixed multiple coloring inconsistencies in lores relating to the Bank.
QoL Changes
- Changed requirement to spawn the Carpenter NPC from VIP+ to VIP.
- The Fel Sword now keeps track of the current kills (and extra damage earned) in the lore of the sword under the explanation of the ability.
That's all for today! Thank you to all the players who continue to submit bug reports to assist our team, these changes would not have been possible without you all. Remember to submit bugs you find here!
I look forward to seeing you all on the network!

# Changelog/2022/April 20
Categories: 2022 Updates, Updates
0.13
SkyBlock Patch 0.13 - Crimson Isle
Updated: April 20, 2022
Hello,
Today the Blazing Fortress is emerging from its chrysalis, finally born anew in the form of the Crimson Isle. Many memories were forged on this island but it is time for new stories to be told.
In this thread, we will present the largest SkyBlock release to date.
## ► The Crimson Isle
Centuries of eruptions from the Volcano turned this island into this infernal landscape. The island inhabitants regrouped into two opposing factions on each side of the Volcano. While their relations have been mostly peaceful since the treaty, trouble is starting to brew on the island.
## ► Factions
The requirement to enter the Crimson Isle is Combat level 24.
Near the island entrance, you'll find Elle of the Nether in a predicament. Help her, and she will tell you more about this place and the two factions.
The Taika people compose the Mage Faction in Scarleton, while the Piglins are on the opposite side of the island in Dragontail, home of the Barbarian Faction. Somewhere along the questline, you’ll be able to join either of those factions and progress in their respective quests.
What is a Faction?
This update introduces new Quest and NPC mechanics such as improved objective systems, dialogue systems, NPCs moving, and more. Each Faction has its own questline and story for you to play through.
On top of that, you will get access to the Town Board of your Faction, giving you Daily Missions. You will need to gain reputation to move up the ranks of your Faction, giving you access to new shop options, better daily missions, and more.
Choosing a Faction isn’t a strategy, so feel free to pick whichever fancies you the best. You can always change factions later if you feel like it by talking to Elle. You will need to lose all your current reputation to amend and restore your trust with the other faction before you join them.
## ► Enemies
The Crimson Isle is an extremely hostile area with tons of new enemies, and 5 of them stand above the others both in strength and in their reward. Those 5 mini-bosses are known as: Bladesoul: A giant wither skeleton who rules over the stronghold after defeating the strongest of the Blazes in it, now wearing his body as a crown. The Barbarian Duke X: A Piglin who consumed way too much Sulphur and gained immense strength at the cost of his sanity, now ruling over his people on the outskirts of the city. Mage Outlaws: Imbued in their search for power, they got banned from Scarleton and are always up to demonstrate their power to anyone who challenges them. Ashfang: A mighty Blaze that resides behind the wastelands, in the ruins of an old bastion. The Magma Boss: The mightiest of them all... This titan can is found in the magma chamber, in the depth of the Volcano. When defeated, each one of those creatures drops essential material, and each has a chance to drop Kuudra Keys.
On top of that, Vanquishers are monsters that rarely spawn when you kill another monster on the island, similar to Special Zealots in the End. However, we improved the pity system to make it scale even more than the one used with Special Zealots. If you ever spawn a Vanquisher, make sure other players help you take them down, as they will benefit from loot sharing, and sharing is caring!
Honorable mention to the Matriarch, mother of all Hellwisps. You can find this dreadful creature in the depth of the Volcano. Come close to it, and it will try to eat you alive. While that doesn’t sound great, it’s the only way to obtain Heavy Pearls, and it only creates 3 of them every IRL day.
## ► Kuudra
Kuudra is the name of a massive lava kraken that appeared on the Crimson Isle. You can access this monstrous anomaly from the Forgotten Skull. It will require you to have completed at least one of the Faction Questlines.
There are five tiers of difficulty to this boss fight, but only two will be released today. We are planning significant changes to this part of the content, so we will not give any ETA for the release of the last three tiers.
You will have to make a party to start a fight, as there won’t be matchmaking until a further release. Win or lose, players will be able to open a free chest at the end of each fight, but the content of this free chest will be smaller if you lose.
Winning, however, spawns an extra chest that can only be opened using a Kuudra Key. Those players will be able to obtain new armor pieces, as long as other valuable items.
## ► New Armors
Among all the new armors released with the Crimson Isle, the best ones are found inside the Key Chest at the end of the Kuudra Boss fight. The Crimson Armor: An armor perfect for melee DPS that extends the range you can hit enemies from. The Aurora Armor: This mage armor will see some changes in the future. It is closely tied to the Aurora Staff, which you also get from Kuudra. The Terror Armor: This set makes anyone look like a natural-born archer with its devastating passive. The Fervor Armor: Among all the tank armors in SkyBlock, this one rules them all. The Hollow Armor: A support armor that will give interesting choices to the players. (This armor will release at a later date)
Each one of those armors is using the new Set Bonus system. Even if you are only using two pieces of those sets, you’ll still benefit from their passives, but the effects will get stronger with each extra piece.
## ► Essence Changes
Until now, various essences obtained from Dungeons were used to turn items into Dungeon gear and then apply stars on them. From now on, stars will give extra stats not only in Dungeons but instead give 2% additional stats on gear pieces, as opposed to the 10% they still give in Dungeons.
On top of that, some of the new armor items in the Crimson Isle can have more than five stars, up to 15 for some of them. But those will require Crimson Essence, obtained from defeating bosses and salvaging nether gear. You can see all the details on that system at the Blacksmith of each Faction.
## ► Attributes
Attributes are a new gear system exclusive to the Crimson Isle.
When obtaining a piece of gear on the island, they will spawn with Attributes, which are extra passives. Each Attribute can be fused by simply combining two pieces with similar attributes. You can think of them as extra enchantments in a way. They go all the way to level 10 and, once again, can be fused at the Blacksmith of each Faction.
## ► Equipment
For the first time since we initially talked about equipment in our design threads a few months back, we will finally release these 4 new armor pieces (Gauntlet, Necklace, Cloak and Belt) with the Crimson Isle.
You can equip them by simply right-clicking them like any other piece of armor, and they can be found in your Profile menu inside the SkyBlock Menu.
Equipment has its own reforges that you can obtain from new reforge stones, and they can also have their own set bonuses. We will add more equipment pieces to earlier parts of the game in the future.
## ► Dojo
Between the tension between the Mage and Barbarian Faction, one man called Master Tao has dedicated himself to conjuring peace and tranquility within the island. As a martial arts sensei, he has derived his teachings in a handful of tests, open to whoever thinks they are worthy of the Black Belt.
## ► Accessory Bag changes
We’ve also made some changes to the Accessory System. Moving forward, you will no longer be able to reforge accessories individually anymore; instead, reforges can only be applied to the accessory bag itself using Power Stones. By default, you’ll be able to select any of the Starter Powers to apply to your accessory bag, but as you continue progressing within the game, you’ll get access to Power Stones which will allow you to modify your stats more precisely.
Speaking of precision, you’ll also be able to tune your stats further using the new Tuning System. Every 10 MP will grant you 1 Tuning point, which you can spend on whatever stat you’d like. Accessories will now grant Magical Power (MP) based on their rarity. The total amount of MP determines the amount of stats gained through reforges.
You can talk to Maxwell in the Hub to learn more about it.
Some explanation on why we made those changes
The previous system “worked”; however, there were a few issues both on the player side and our side. For the players, having to obtain a new reforge stone every time they got a new accessory was less than ideal, and we wanted to create a system that permanently unlocks all build options; that way you can swap between builds with very little effort and no cost, after unlocking them.
The second issue was related to balancing. We believe accessory progression is vital in SkyBlock; it makes players have to go through the whole game and complete each piece of content until they obtain all the accessories. We want to keep adding accessories and let players complete their bags, but each accessory we release means a lot of “free stats”.
For us on the design side, this isn’t where the value of accessories is; the two major requirements for accessories are:
- They must be part of the game's completion and feel fair to obtain; unlocking a legendary artifact should be quite hard.
- Whether it’s unlocking a new common accessory or even recombobulator any of your accessories, each step on the progression needs to feel rewarding.
To be quite honest, it felt too rewarding; obtaining an epic accessory could give you 10 Critical Damage and recombobulating it an extra 5.
What the new system accomplishes is:
1. It makes the progression clear and makes changing builds easier than before.
2. It adds a logarithmic curve to the progression; in short, it buffs early/mid-game players and nerfs more late/end game players while also shifting more of that power to other sources. That is most important to us because it helps with content balancing and makes it less scary to add new accessories to the game.
3. The difference in stats given by accessories compared to armor and weapons is going to be much less than what it was before, which allows harder gameplay like slayers to be more accessible to players who do not have a ton of accessories.
4. While new accessories will reward fewer stats than before the update, we are still confident that obtaining a new accessory, even common, will still feel like a significant progression.
5. The Crimson Isle is introducing a TON of new sources of power: Damage gemstones got a slight buff, four new pieces of equipment got released, those pieces have their own reforges, we introduced the new strongest armors in the game, there are a few new accessories, the accessory bag has extra slots, and we even made stars work outside of dungeons. If there was a perfect moment to nerf accessories, it was now. Even players who have fully maxed accessory bags will recuperate those stats in a heartbeat and go way beyond.
## ► Collections
What would be a new island update without new collections? There are now four new collections: Mycelium: This can be obtained in the Mystic March in front of Scarleton Red Sand: Can be shoveled from the Burning Desert. Sulphur: A new magical material emanating from the Volcano during eruptions. It can also be dropped from corrupted mobs. Magmafish: Obtained from Lava Fishing.
## ► Blaze Slayer
This 5th slayer category is even more challenging than the Voidgloom. Still, if you are up the task, there are a lot of interesting and gabagool rewards to obtain. This content is best played in the Smoldering Tomb, in the back of the island. The Smoldering Tomb also has large veins of Opal, a new Gemstone.
## ► Lava and Trophy Fishing
If you climb your way to the top of the Volcano, you’ll find a little hut where a goblin lives. Odger isn’t very smart, but he’s smart enough to teach you about Trophy Fishing.
Trophy Fishes are special Fishes you can only catch by fishing in particular ways. There are 18 of them than can be found in different tiers of quality; find all Diamond Trophy Fishes to unlock all the rewards!
On top of that, there are tons of new lava fishing content, including bosses, armors, and plenty of loot.
## ► Loot Sharing improvements
You might remember the Loot Sharing System we used for Ghosts in the Dwarven Mines; we’ve since made some improvements to this system and are happy to announce that Loot sharing will work all across SkyBlock. The system shares loot with other players if they deal at least 1% of damage to the mob up to a maximum of five players per mob.
Part of this is a new system we call “Shared Loot” which allows us to modify how many drops a loot-shared player will obtain on participating in a mob kill. In some instances, like slayers, fishing, and the mythological event, loot-shared players will receive much less rewards in comparison.
## ► Abiphone
The mage faction is the inventor and leading producer of the Ability Phones. Abiphones can be used to call various NPCs, but not all NPCs have Abiphones.
You can walk to any NPCs compatible with this feature and right-click them with your Abiphone. Most of the time, they will accept to give your their contact only if you help them, but once you do, you’ll be able to access their features directly from your Abiphone.
## ► Future Plans
First off, we would like to thank everyone who helped test the update on Alpha. The update entered development hell during testing because of multiple performance issues puzzling the team for weeks. Your support really kept us fully motivated the whole way.
In the next few months, we plan to: Release a post update patch to fix any issue that arises from this update. Work on the Kuudra fight and release the last three tiers and change the first two. Spend some time “fixing” the game, including many bugs and minor/major quality of life improvements like Rusty changes. Release the next gear balancing patch. Release a secret update. Start working on SkyBlock Levels and other 1.0 features.
## ► From Hypixel to You
The Crimson Isle isn't just anether update; it is the biggest update the Hypixel Team has ever worked on for SkyBlock, even surpassing the Dwarven Mines. From game designers to developers to artists to quality assurance, the team has spent countless hours working on this update to bring you the best gameplay. We are looking forward to seeing players play it finally. We will be providing updates to the island after release to ensure it is in the best state possible.
Credits:
- @TheMGRF : Achievements, Volcano eruptions, Collection Performances, and more!
- @Dueces : Kuudra Boss fight, items, mobs, daily missions and more!
- @TheBirmanator : The Dojo, Rescue Missions, and more!
- @Dctr : Lava fishing content, Ashfang, and more!
- @Relenter : Main quest, many mobs and items, and more!
- @JacobRuby : Multiple mobs and minibosses, and more!
- @aPunch : Faction system, Attributes and essence, and more!
- @Minikloon : Blaze Slayer, accessory bag changes.
- @LadyBleu : NPC Skins and item arts.
- @ShinkoNet - Crimson Isle Music.
- @Sylent, @Bloozing, @Citria, HPC and Bugs Team - Testing/QA
- @xHascox and @Jayavarmen - Game Design Team
- @Vinny8Ball, @fudgiethewhale, @Cheesey, @Plummel, @DEADORKAI and @Rozsa - Crimson Isle
Remember that this list is not exhaustive. Developers on this list are often working on other projects.
Thanks for reading!

# Changelog/2022/April 21
Categories: 2022 Updates, Updates
PATCH
[April 21] Crimson Isle - The Fixes
Updated: April 21, 2022
During the development of the Crimson Isle update we fixed a lot of bugs around the rest of SkyBlock to not only help improve gameplay but also aid in our performance improvements to make this update possible. This post is an attempt at forming a list of those fixes though not all of them were documented. This list is not extensive and does not cover every bug fix, tweak, or change made alongside the update and does not include changes made since the update's release.
- Fixed certain NPCs not sitting in 1.8.
- Fixed some missing sounds in some noteblock songs.
- Fixed many issues on the Farming Islands:
  - Now shows "Zone" instead of "Loc" in the player list.
  - More zones have actual names that show in the scoreboard now.
  - Updated zone descriptions.
  - Updated Trapper GUIs.
  - Fixed some blocks not being mineable when they should be.
- Fixed the name of the Viking Shop.
- Removed the Builder Legacy shop and moved items to the new one.
- Fixed Blaze Pet perk stacking.
- Changed how Training Dummies handle their health and deaths.
- Fixed the back arrow now appearing in the Gemstone Sack after toggling the filter.
- Fixed Mineral armor not working on some Redstone Ore and Dwarven Gold.
- Fixed Night Savers giving Day Savers from the menu.
- Made visual improvements to the Feather Talisman, Ring, and Artifact.
- Added back buttons to Rosseta's Shop.
- Fixed Farming for Dummies only applying 4 times.
- Fixed Squid health bars floating too high above the mob.
- Leaflet armor now works when in a forest biome!
- Fixed Pigman and Redstone Pigman increasing kills on Revenant Armor's Zombie Bulwark ability.
- Added a cooldown to the Snow Shovel.
- Fixed several issues with the Dwarven Mines Raffle event:
  - Fixed the wrong times being reported near the end of the event.
  - Fixed the title effects not appearing for all players.
  - Fixed rewards not cycling through all players.
- Auto-Recombobulator now works on more items.
- Fixed Double Jump boots not using the right amount of intelligence.
- Fixed Beacon Profile Stats sometimes resetting.
- Added Mining Fortune support to new blocks:
  - Cobblestone
  - Gravel
  - Glowstone
  - Obsidian
- Fixed the Master Enchanter achievement not working with new enchantment table system.
- Fixed the Arcadia achievement not working.
- Fixed the Crystal Hollows pass showing the wrong time in some places.
- Fixed Banners crashing players on 1.8.
- Fixed not being able to interact with some entities using a Fishing Rod on private islands.
- Fixed the Enchanted Bookshelf turning into a normal Bookshelf when you break it.
- Fixed Red Sandstone slabs not working with the Block Zapper correctly.
- Updated the skill descriptions for Combat & Mining.
- Made the Crystal Hollows Metal Detector distance color use aqua to be more visible.
- Made Crystal Hollow chests always face the right direction now.
- Fixed some cases where the Crystal Hollows Jungle Temple would improperly remove and apply effects.
- Bal Pet's Protective Skin effect now displays "IMMUNE" in the scoreboard.
- Upon finding a Divan Alloy a new message and sound are sent to all players in the lobby.
- Skills can now show overflow experience.
- Updated Superboom TNT's lore to reflect the new insta-break feature.
- The Dwarven Forge warp now makes you face south (the correct way).
- Fixed Easter/Summer themed schematics not pasting during the real world event.
- Staggered the pasting of event shematics.
- Fixed some farming sprite blocks (flowers) playing double sounds when broken.
- Fixed Magma Cube/Slime Minion spawning not having enough space.
- Fixed Magma bow consuming double ammo.
- Fixed more placeable heads.
- Fixed inconsistent capitalization in Spooky Event.
- Fixed several items not counting towards collection XP.
- Fixed breaking a Jukebox while playing a custom song preventing new songs from being playable.
- Fixed being able to join outdated versions of SkyBlock when an update is rolling.
- InfiniDirt Wand can no longer be used on Tile Entities.
- Fixed the Shop Confirmation setting toggle displaying the wrong amount of coins.
- Fixed island guests being able to use Reforge Anvils.
- Blocked /opencannonbuymenu from working if you don't have a Snow Cannon in hand.
- Fixed soo many typos.
- Made countless performance changes to every aspect of the game.
- And many more...
And that's it for today folks! Note: There will more fixes over the next week for issues directly related to the Crimson Isle update.

# Changelog/2022/April 25
Categories: 2022 Updates, Updates
PATCH
[April 25] Rusty Unleashed
Updated: April 25, 2022
Hello! Patch notes!
Rusty & Telekinesis
The Telekinesis enchant has been replaced with the Auto-pickup perk unlocked at Combat VI.
Auto-pickup perk unlocked!
Telekinesis has been removed from all items in the guide.
At the moment, auto-pickup behaves the exact same as tele did, but we might change it for slayer, fishing, dungeons, etc...
Because of this change, we had to find a new use for Rusty, the Janitor of the Gold Mines.
He has been promoted to SkyBlock Janitor and now offers a shop with one-time quest rewards, such as ring of love, cheetah talisman, etc...
Rusty's Shop
Material Stash
Item stash is a feature which stores items which couldn't fit items in your inventory when you have to pick them up.
Material stash is the same, but it stores stackable items instead, like sacks.
This feature has been in the game before and we now brought it back.
Other Features
- Prevent a lot more (abilities) outgoing damage when using Soul Esoward
- Add a 20s grace period before BIN auctions can be bought
BIN grace period in action
- Added search and filtering to the enchantments guide menu
Enchantments guide search
Changes
- Reduced Corrupt Bait NPC sell price to 1
- Changed Corrupted Fragment from 500 to 20
- Changed Eccentric Painting in the DA to a bundle which unwraps 9x of the power stone
- Fire and Lava talisman now grant immunity everywhere, except in Crimson isle where it grants damage reduction
Buffed Fire Talisman (and Lava)
Bug Fixes
- Don't unlock the Dungeon Hub fast travel by default
- Properly show recombed weapons on Weapon Rack+
- Stopped using Salmon Armor outside water taking mana away
- Add an FX when attempting violence on the Chest+ furniture
- Properly show ether transmission in etherwarp merger recipes
- Fixed deleting crafted furnitures if your inventory is full
- Fixed grammar in soul-storing items
- Fixed Spelunker potion V recipe
- Fixed clicking auction house sending too many messages sometimes
- Fixed Grappling Hook (and some other inappropriate rods) consuming baits
- Fixed Hellfire Rod & Inferno Rod not carrying over enchants/data when upgrading
- Fixed typo in rare drops / pet drops
- Fixed clicking chests with an ability item
- Approximate fishing minions' output when generating >1000 items, like other minions do.
  - This will allow some players to log into the game again. =S
Exploits
- Add limit to star sentry dropped starfalls
- Fixed minion items UUID shift
- Despawn ghosts when their Y is too high, to save the noobs
Enjoy! :hypixel_smile:

# Changelog/2022/April 29
Categories: 2022 Updates, Updates
PATCH
[April 29] Crimson Isle Weeks 1 & 2
Updated: April 29, 2022
The Crimson Isle update has now been out for just over a week so here is a collection of all the fixes we've made since then!
## Bug Fixes
- Fixed Wheat Minions not producing Wheat.
- Fixed Jump Pads not working in The Park.
- Fixed being able to use teleport abilities during the mage council meeting.
- Fixed Odger saying some quest dialogue out of order.
- Fixed an issue with the Quiver not detecting arrows correctly.
- Added caps to the Snail Pet perks.
- Fixed Final Destination not working when many players were around.
- Fixed certain Rune trails not appearing.
- Fixed Minion spawned mobs appearing outside the Minion spawn area.
- Fixed many issues with the Kuudra fight and Cannons.
- Mushroom Cow Pet perk "Mushroom Eater" no longer works on player placed blocks.
- Fixed the Kuudra fight reputation requirement requiring more than the set amount of reputation.
- Fixed another bug with the Master Enchanter achievement so now it actually works!
- Disabled Loot Share on private islands and dungeons.
- Glass is once again no longer capped in the Builder shop.
- Fixed Wither Spectres having multiple health bars.
- Fixed being able to corrupt the Quazzi and Typoeus slayer bosses.
- Fixed Kat not calculating pet upgrades correctly.
- Fixed purchasing the new minions not counting towards minion progress.
- Fixed items displaying the incorrect mana cost when enchanted with Ultimate Wise
- Fixed a rare pet stack caused by rescue missions.
- Fixed the Rabbit Hat not working everywhere on private islands.
- Fixed Sulphur Ore not sending a message when using too weak of a tool to mine it.
- Fixed townboard quests being unable to pick quests under certain circumstances.
- Fixed mana enchantments giving bonuses when they shouldn't.
- Fixed Trophy Fishing using the wrong pet perk boost internally.
- Fixed some instances where mobs would revive themselves in a "half dead" state.
- Fixed certain non-dungeon items being able to be converted into dungeon items.
- Fixed a lot broken Fairy Soul locations.
- Fixed not being able to donate starred armor to the museum.
- Fixed the Compact enchantment giving regular Enchanted Sand when breaking Red Sand.
- The Mages Guild Dean will now only consume the Batphone given from the Crimson Isle main quest.
- Fixed the Withers from Witherborn armor being seen as hostile enemies when escorting Elle and Ugo.
- Added fishing XP for some missing lava fishing items.
- All Trophy Fish now reward fishing XP.
- Fixed certain slayer quests not loot sharing correctly.
- Fixed being unable to complete some townboard quests.
- Fixed mana bombs staying active after killing Kuudra.
- Fixed dead team members being unable to open loot chests at the end of the Kuudra fight.
- Fixed two empty lines in a row showing on the scoreboard in the Kuudra fight.
- Fixed Elle talking about orbs when everyone is dead in the Kuudra fight.
- Fixed Dungeon mob drops not applying stat boosts and enchantments correctly.
- Made the Matriarch minigame only work with melee attacks.
- Fixed Dungeon Secrets not counting when you were using a Personal Deletor.
- Fixed races kicking players once they reached and completed a certain stage.
- Fixed Matriarch knockback slowly drowning players whilst in the zone.
- Fixed a bug where party members could bypass the Crimson Isle requirement level.
- Fixed Hot Armor not getting set bonus from previous types.
- Added Salvages to Flaming Fist.
- Fixed a variety of Dojo stat issues.
- Fixed a variety of crafting recipes.
- Fixed the Common Dolphin Pet having the Legendary abilities.
- Fixed even more typos!
- A lot of performance improvements!
- And many more that didn't make the list...
## QOL
- All items are now fireproof on the Crimson Isle.
- Added baits to Bazaar.
- Re-added Relenter's NPC into the Village :)
- Auto Smelt now produces coal from more logs.
- Added Maxwell and Jacobus to the Stranded mode.
- Certain Trophy Fish items now show they can be used as bait.
- Guards in the Crimson Isle now give warning messages before attacking.
- The townboard daily quests will no longer give quests for disabled features/tasks.
- The Dojo Mastery challenge now spawns target time holograms.
- A 2nd Batphone can now be taken from Elle during the Crimson Isle main quest.
- Swapped the response order from the queens guards to make it less likely to miss click on the option that kills you.
- Mycelium and Red Sand minions now only require 1 player in a co-op to have the required reputation.
- Updated the Guildford race rewards to be more clear.
That's it for right now folks, thanks for reporting them and glhf!
Bugs squashed by: @TheMGRF, @TheBirmanator, @Relenter, and others

# Changelog/2022/August 1
Categories: 2022 Updates, Updates
PATCH
[August 1] Thunder Revolution Update, QoL, and Bug Fixes
Updated: August 1, 2022
Hey all!
Recently, we've worked closely with our community SkyBlock team, The Nerds, to fix a few bugs and make some improvements to existing content.
#### ► Bug Fixes
- Fixed the removed enchantment, Telekinesis, still appearing in the enchantment guide.
- Fixed the Wisp Pet's Extinguish ability not working in Kuudra fights.
- Fixed the Compact enchantment not working on netherrack.
- Fixed Odger's Tooth accessories stacking their Magic Power.
- Fixed an exploit that caused the Matriarch to softlock indefinitely.
- Fixed some miniboss loot displaying weirdly tilted.
- Fixed a visual bug with Ashfang where the gravity bomb particles would move when a blaze is caught in it.
- Fixed Transmission Tuner applying twice when used in an anvil. The max amount of Transmission Tuners that can be applied to an item has been increased to 4 to account for this.
- Fixed sacks that are not owned being able to be opened from Collection menus.
#### ► Quality of Life
- Added options to disable Sea Creature and Ability Cooldown chat messages, as well as an option to disable Ability Cooldown sounds.
  - To access these options, open the SkyBlock Menu, click on Settings, then click on Personal. There are buttons to open the Soundsmenu and the Chat Feedback menu.
- Added auto-pickup to miniboss loot.
  - Miniboss loot will now be picked up automatically a few seconds before the boss podium disappears or if the players leave the server.
- Blessings and loot are now much easier to pick up.
  - You should no longer have to briefly pause on top of drops to ensure you pick them up. Enjoy zooming by drops with 400 speed!
  - This affects Dungeons and Crimson Isle minibosses.
- Added the current health bonus to the New Year Cake Bag.
  - This was something small, but a lot of people were asking for it.
- Added some missing items as well as the Slayer minion items to the Slayer Sack.
  - Added Undead Catalyst, Revenant Catalyst, Spider Catalyst, Null Atom, Raw Soulflow, Soulflow, and Crude Gabagool.
- Added an Insert Inventory button to the Sack of Sacks.
  - This will take all available items from your inventory and insert them into your Sacks.
#### ► Balancing
- After tons of feedback from the Nerds, we have completely revamped the methods for charging the Empty Thunder Bottle.
  - Our goal was to make the bottle charge passively while doing normal tasks, but also have several methods to specifically farm for Thunder Charge. You can view all of the new methods on our Official Wiki Page, here.
  - Thunder boss will now spawn several Sparks when killed, which can be collected with an Empty Thunder Bottle.
    - Previously, one of the best ways to charge the Thunder Bottle was from soaking damage from the Thunder boss until it despawns. We wanted to keep this method alive while also allowing players to farm Thunder kills.
- Thunder Armor is no longer Soulbound to be consistent with Magma Lord Armor.
- The Pulse Ring accessory has been given a few new perks.
  - This includes a small Magic Find boost as well as a Thunder Charge multiplier for the Empty Thunder Bottle.
- A few issues with Ashfang have been fixed.
  - We've fixed an issue where Ashfang Followers would get stuck on ledges when being pulled in by Gravity Bombs.
  - In addition, we've flattened the Ashfang arena to make it easier to line up shots for the Blazing Soul projectiles.
A huge thank you to the Nerds and our community for reporting these issues! Please continue to report issues here.

# Changelog/2022/August 18
Categories: 2022 Updates, Updates
0.14
SkyBlock Patch 0.14 - Shen's Auctions, The Hex, RNG Meter and more!
Updated: August 18, 2022
Hello,
With this major technical update for SkyBlock, we are about to enter a phase of making the impossible happen.
Today we present a few modest improvements to the SkyBlock hub and the game.
Please note that at the time of writing this, SkyBlock is still in maintenance! We thought we'd release the news ahead of time.
## ► Bazaar Improvements
One of the richest Hub villagers is looking to become even richer, and they have been working extra hours lately to add many functionalities to their service:
- Enchantment books that are otherwise impossible to find in enchanting tables are now added to the Bazaar.
- You can now trade every kind of Essence in the Bazaar.
- Added a search option.
- Most modifier-type of items have been added to the Bazaar.
Not only is this quite useful, but this also allows us to do two important things!
- Because all item modifiers will either have a shop price or a bazaar price, this will allow us to calculate the price of items accurately, no matter the amount of work on the item.
- It allowed us to create the Hex.
## ► The Hex
For players at any stage of the game, it can be a bother to work on a new sword or bow; there are so many enchantments and modifiers to apply, and sometimes you might even forget something!
The Hex allows you to apply every single modifier an item can have directly from its menu. It will take the items directly from your inventories and backpacks, and if you are missing an item, it'll buy it directly from the Bazaar! (rip ironman)
The menu also makes it easier to read what is or isn't on the item yet, and if you are missing some exp to apply an enchant, you can even buy the bottles of enchanting directly from it!
To unlock the Hex, you'll have to talk to Eleanor in the Museum; from there, and if you have the requirements, you'll find the Hex and The Handler in the Hub behind the Library.
## ► Carpentry
The Carpentry Skill is no longer a VIP only skill! Everyone can start collecting Carpentry EXP now, and the rate is the same for everyone.
Some of the features in the Hex have Carpentry level requirements, and in the future, this skill will unlock new content.
## ► RNG Meter Changes
Love it or hate it, randomness is core to SkyBlock. Everyone's game experience is different; some have it easy, while others have to persevere until they finally reach their goal. It can create some very exciting or extremely frustrating moments. While we do not want to take anything away from those exciting moments, there have to be some limits on the frustration of never ever finding what you want.
The changes to the RNG Meter are as follows:
- The RNG Meter has been added to the Catacombs (Floor 1 to Master Mode 7)
- Doing S(+) runs or defeating Slayer bosses will start accumulating EXP in your Meter.
- You can now choose any RNG drop you have unlocked and start working towards it.
- As your progress accumulates, you'll gain increased odds of dropping the selected item until you find it.
- If you do find the selected item before the Meter is full, the Meter will reset. Otherwise, you'll reach 100% completion, and the item will automatically drop on the next Dungeon Run or Slayer Boss.
- Finding another rare drop will not reset the Meter anymore.
- At any point, you can decide to swap the item you have selected, and it will transfer the accumulated EXP towards it.
- You can now earn Slayer RNG Meter EXP starting level 3, instead of 4.
The 33% progress increased the odds from 1% to 1.65%
The Slayer RNG Meter will keep your current progress, and in the case of the Catacombs Meter, you will gain some of your EXP retroactively but the ratio is very low, for the sake of the economy.
► Stats Breakdown Menu
The stats menu(s) available in "your profile menu" has been upgraded to show where all of your stats are from.
If you want to learn more about it, here's an objectively mediocre guide about it.
New Stats:
- 7 Wisdom Stats for different skills, which increase skill XP gain. (For example, Slayer now grants Combat Wisdom!)
- Fishing Speed
- Health Regen
- Vitality, which boosts incoming healing (good for tanks!)
- Mending, which boosts outgoing healing (good for healers!)
Fishing Speed also works differently as before. It used to remove a % of the remaining time available to spawn the fish, the system now simply adds a value and it linearly makes the particles spawn faster.
If you reach 300 Fishing Speed, the particles will appear instantly, but there is an exception with the Crimson Isle, where 350 Fishing Speed is the value you have to reach for maximum efficiency.
► Shen's Auction
NOTE: Shen's Auction will be opened at a later time. We will update this newspost when it's ready to go!
There is a new piece of content for endgame players set up under the Auction House. With the proper Museum requirements, you can talk to Damia and access two new types of auctions.
The first auctions are 3 Accessories that are being sold each SkyBlock year. The Artifact of Control is being sold in only ten exemplaries each year.
The Pandora's Box is a unique accessory, and only 20 of them are put in auction each year. This accessory is the only one that cannot be recombobulated. To increase its rarity to Mythic, you will have to complete all the challenges that the box holds.
The Shen's Regalia is an incredibly valuable accessory that allows you to enter the Regalia Room, where you will find a very special shop with multiple new rare items. A maximum of 40 Regalias can be bought each year.
On top of that, Shen himself is selling items from his personal collection, but only for the richest players. Most of those items are not particularly strong or useful, but they might appeal to the collectors out there. You will be notified of the auction when the item is being auctioned for the very last time.
To obtain those items, you will have to bid on them, and Ironman players have access to those auctions. All you need to do is, have one of the top bids by the end of the SkyBlock year, simple, right?
But who's Shen?
Shen is...a very important person, but he's not ready to reveal himself just yet.
## ► Backend Changes
We've done a refactor (rewrite) of many systems in the game. The goal has been to increase the rate at which we can ship updates.
Systems touched include:
- Upgraded Java runtime from 8 to 17!
- Moved a lot of stuff from code to JSON data:
  - Furniture
  - Item Skins
  - Pet Skins
  - Minion Skins
  - Sea Creatures
  - Islands data (like resource areas)
  - Zones (like NPC locations)
  - Objectives
- The Objective system was revamped
- Unified Requirement system (like slayer requirements on items, but across the game...)
- Custom Mobs system
- Feature-toggling system (for example, Dojo disabling menus)
- Accessories stacking
- Parkour/Race system
- The Bazaar's client-side (what's on the game server)
A Dev Blog will come post-release regarding the JSON data changes and what it means for the game.
## ► Balancing Changes
Some balancing was implemented on existing content.
## ► Enchantment Changes
- Protection: Changed from 3/6/9/12/15/18/21 - > 4/8/12/16/20/25/30 (levels 1-7)
- Flame reworked to match Fire Aspect.
- Rend: Fix a bug that causes defense to apply twice; now, Rend deals true damage.
- Combo: Increases damage by +1/2/3/4/5% (additive) for every kill, capping at 2/4/6/8/10 kills lasting 2/4/6/8/10 seconds (levels 1-5)
- Swarm: Gain +2/4/6/8/10% damage per nearby mob, caps at ten mobs.
- Bank: Additionally, enemies drop 0.5/1/1.5/2/2.5 more coins when killed.
## ► Enderman Slayer Changes
- Void Swords: Nerfed damage bonus to 100/150/200/250
- Final Destination: Full Set Bonus: Strength 100 > 30, multiplies Intelligence by 1.5x - > 1.25x
- And nerfed attack speed to +20
Tier 2:
HP: 15,000,000 HP - > 12,000,000 HP
1st shield: 10,000,000 HP - > 8,000,000 HP
2nd shield: 5,000,000 HP - > 4,000,000 HP
Yang Glyphs starts at: 7,500,000 HP - > 6,000,000 HP
Tier 3:
HP: 66,666,666 HP - > 50,000,000 HP
1st shield: 44,444,444 HP - > 33,333,333 HP
2nd shield: 22,222,222 HP - > 16,666,666 HP
Yang Glyphs starts at: 44,444,444 HP - > 33,333,333 HP
Nukekubi Fixation starts at: 22,222,222 HP - > 16,666,666 HP
Tier 4:
HP: 300,000,000 HP - > 210,000,000 HP
1st shield: 200,000,000 HP - > 140,000,000 HP
2nd shield: 100,000,000 HP - > 70,000,000 HP
Yang Glyphs starts at: 200,000,000 HP - > 140,000,000 HP
Nukekubi Fixation starts at: 100,000,000 HP - > 70,000,000 HP
1st Broken Heart Radiation: 250,000,000 HP - > 175,000,000 HP
2nd Broken Heart Radiation: 150,000,000 HP - > 105,000,000 HP
3rd Broken Heart Radiation: 50,000,000 HP - > 35,000,000 HP
## ► Pets
Ender Dragon
Renowned, Superior, Superior Blood and Legion are now additive with each other rather than multiplying with each other.
"End Strike": Deal +0.25% (multiplicative) damage per level - > deal +2% (additive) damage per level
Golden Dragon
"Dragon's Greed": Gain +0.5% Strength for every 5 Magic Find, caps at 100 Magic Find (+0.5% strength for every 5 MF at level 200)
"Shining Scales": Add a cap at 1b gold collection (10 digits)
Ammonite
Nerf base SCC stat: 0.07 per level - > 0.05 per level.
"Heart of the Sea" now applies the SCC to the Ammonite's stats.
Dolphin
"Pod Tactics" (Legendary Rarity): Now gives +1 SCC per level for each player (max 30% instead of 25%)
Flying Fish
"Quick Reel": Now grants 0.75 Fishing Speed per level at Legendary rarity (up to 75)
Scatha
"Wormhole" now unlocks at Epic rarity
Legendary 4th Perk: Gemstone Power: Gain +20% (+0.2% per level) more Gemstone Powder from all sources.
(additive with the HOTM powder perk)
Golem
"Last Stand": activates at 15% - > 25% HP
Phoenix
"Rekindle": 3m - > 1m cooldown
Endermite
"Pearl Muncher": Now only works on Ender Pearls dropped from killing enemies, instead of from any pearl dropped.
"Pearl Powered": Also give +30 Strength & +10 Magic Find for 10s, effect doesn't stack.
Tiger
"Apex Predator": Deal +0.2% damage per level - > +1% damage per level (this is additive)
Lion
"King of the Jungle": deal +0.15% - > +1.5% damage per level (this is additive)
## ► Miscellaneous changes
- Mithril NPC price 10 - > 8
- Combat XP gained from Hub Wolves 10 - > 15
- Combat XP gained from Howling Spirits 15 - > 22
- Combat XP gained from Pack Spirits 15 - > 22
## ► A note on future updates
Here's a quick recap of what you can expect in the next few updates!
Happening pretty soon but in no specific order:
- End Island QoL Update
- Kuudra revamp
- Garden Update (part 1)
- SkyBlock Levels (part 1)
- New bits items
Happening moderately soon:
- Wizard Portal
- Garden Update (part 2)
- SkyBlock Levels follow up
Happening in a little while:
- Foraging update
## Credits:
@aPunch - Bazaar additions + Hex + RNG Meter + Stat changes + Backend changes & Balancing @JacobRuby - Hex animation @LandonHP - Shen's Auctions content @ConnorLinfoot - Backend implementation of Shen's Auctions @Minikloon - New stat menu + Backend changes @Sylent, @Bloozing, @Citria, HPC and QA Team - Testing/QA @Jayavarmen - Game Design @AdamWho and @Cheesey - Auction House and Hex room
Remember that this list is not exhaustive. Developers on this list are often working on other projects.
Thanks for reading!
Huge thanks to all the players who reported bugs on Alpha, we fixed 181 of them!

# Changelog/2022/August 27/0
Categories: 2022 Updates, Updates
PATCH
[August 27] Patch 0.14 Bug Fixes 3, Trilogy Complete
Updated: August 27, 2022
Hey all!
Here are some more bug fixes following the 0.14 patch from last week.
Some non-0.14 fixes have slipped in there too.
Objectives
- Fixed Lazy Miner's quest, updated Rusty's dialogue to fit a little better and adding the Lazy Miner's pickaxe in Rusty for 200 coins.
- Fixed objectives not always starting on the private island when creating a new profile.
- Fixed Rare Relics quest.
- Fixed objective triggers involving crafting an item, like the Wheat minion in the Hub.
- Fixed skill level objective triggers not always activating.
General Fixes
- Deployed a fix to the /visit command, may be disabled again as it causes performance issues under certain conditions.
- Added a line to Romero when the player gives it a Magical Lava Bucket (instead of the accepted Enchanted Lava Bucket).
- Fixed the Mending stat not applying under certain conditions. Namely, when healing other players.
- Fixed applying pet skins to pets also placing down the skin as a block on private islands.
  - We'll look into it and if there is demand, we may add an item in the game to convert any skull item in the game to cosmetic skulls to place down on islands for fun/clout.
- Fixed the 'Hide Pets' toggle in the Pets Menu not refreshing when being clicked.
General CHANGES
- Added Salmon, Pufferfish, Clownfish and their enchanted equivalents to the Fishing Bag.
- Updated the stats breakdown on the top-level menu to show more important stats.
- Updated Scatha Pet's Wormhole perk to actually behave like its lore prescribes. This means it won't break all kinds of blocks anymore.
- All Slayer bosses will now damage allied mobs summoned by players.
- Switched Booster Cookies to grant +25 Wisdom.
  - Added Carpentry, Runecrafting, Social Wisdom to match previous behavior.
  - The reason is to make it clearer what it does. It does make that buff additive, so it's a buff for some players and a nerf for others.
Booster Cookie now offers +25 Wisdom
That's that! :hypixel_smile:

# Changelog/2022/August 27/1
Categories: 2022 Updates, Updates
PATCH
[August 27] Maddox is Mad
Updated: August 27, 2022
Hello.
This morning I woke up and received a call from Maddox himself. He tells me: "voidgloom is kill" I say "no".
I could hear the mix of additive fear and multiplicative rage in his voice. He refused to elaborate, hung up (and went on cooldown).
Maddox, but very angry.
So we took another look at Summoning Ring on slayers.
Since yesterday they take %hp damage every second from being within 12 blocks of a slayer boss. The formula for the %hp/hit has now been reduced a lot:
%hp Damage per hit from Slayers to Allied player mobs
The point of %hp damage is to future-proof the bosses against allied mob healing in the future.
Furthermore, to avoid losing cool souls, the Seraph will now recall the player's mob(s) when their hitshield is broken.
The original intent for the change was to make players fight the boss as it was intended, with katana on hitshield. But, not only was ferocity changed since hitshield was created, it's still causing extra carpal tunnel than necessary. Leaving summons as an alternative seems like an alright deviation from the original design.
Mana Cost has also been added to the Summoning Ring:
Mana Cost on souls
Please let me know if you are still mad like Maddox, because I am!! :mad:

# Changelog/2022/August 5
Categories: 2022 Updates, Updates
PATCH
[August 5] Chili Pepper Update
Updated: August 5, 2022
Hey!
Gabagool price landed a lot higher than we predicted, excluding a lot of players from this economic niche.
As a solution, we've reduced the RNG element of running [[Hypergolic Gabagool|Hypergolic fuel]] by introducing a new Combat collection: [[Chili Pepper]]!
There are also some general changes to Blaze Slayer to make it a slightly less infernal experience.
#### General
- [[Kelvin Inverter]] now has a 100,000 [[Coins]] NPC price
- [[Blazetekk™ Ham Radio|Blazetekk Radio]] range increased to 55 blocks
- Sorrow Armor changed:
Now 50/80/70/40 True Def, but with a 2-Piece Set Bonus
#### [[Wisp Pet]]
- Increased Blaze Combat XP by 10% to 30/40/45/50% per rarity
- Increased True Defense by 5
- Renamed Coldfusion Perk to Ephemeral Stability
#### [[Inferno Minion]]
- Now gets (the same) max Inferno buff at 10 minions, meaning you need less of them!
- Drop rate for [[Hypergolic Gabagool|Hypergolic]] unique drops increased by 10%
- [[Hypergolic Gabagool|Hypergolic fuels]] now commonly drop [[Chili Pepper]].
- Expired [[Hypergolic Gabagool|Hypergolic fuels]] now always drop a [[Hypergolic Ionized Ceramics]]. Combine 8 into an [[Inferno Vertex]].
  - Make sure to use an [[Super Compactor 3000|sc3k]] on your minion, as it drops on expiry and your minion might be full!
Guaranteed Vertexes using Science
#### [[Chili Pepper]]
Chili Peppers are a new item which drop somewhat frequently from [[Inferno Minion|Inferno Minions]] running Hypergolic Fuel.
You should get between 5 and 30 per minion per day, depending on how many minions you have on your island.
It's not required to be able to kill the [[Inferno Demonlord]] in order to run Inferno Minions.
Self-descriptive!
The Chili Pepper collection has 9 recipe unlocks with many consumables useful for all players.
Habanero Tactics for your armor pieces at [[Chili Pepper|Chili Pepper VII (7)]] Used with the Smoldering Polarization from the consumable at [[Chili Pepper|Chili Pepper I (1)]]
Alternative [[Sulphuric Coal]] recipe from [[Chili Pepper|Chili Pepper II (2)]], which should reduce Hypergolic Gabagool price
[[Jalapeno Book|Jalapeno Books]] to upgrade the stats on your Deployables' auras (1 book per item, doesn't stack)
There are more items in the collection so make sure to check it out in-game.
#### [[Bits Shop]]
- [[Inferno Fuel Block|Inferno Fuel Blocks]] now available at 64x (a stack) for 3,600 [[Bits]]. Can still purchase 1 item for 75 [[Bits]]
- Added 2 new Stacking Enchants:
Tier X preview of the new stacking enchants in the [[Bits Shop]]
#### Bug Fixes
- Fixed Skull Chest++ deleting items over 255 slots
- Fixed [[Vampiric Vitality|Mana Vampire]] being usable without consuming mana
- Fixed [[Aurora Staff]] kills not properly displaying drops on the client
- Fixed a barrier acquisition method
Note: The update might be actively deploying while you read this..
Enjoy!

# Changelog/2022/December 1
Categories: 2022 Updates, Updates
0.17
Happy Holidays from Hypixel!
Updated: December 1, 2022
SkyBlock 0.17 - Winter Update
Notes:
- This patch note was not fully about SkyBlock. Only contents about SkyBlock are available below.
- This patch note was not labeled as version 0.17. However, based on the Lobby NPC that shows 0.17 - WINTER UPDATE, with the SkyBlock version change a day prior, it was decided to use 0.17 to label this patch note.
## SkyBlock Winter Island Update
The Winter Island will remain open throughout the entire month of December and contains many new features!
### New Winter Sea Creatures
You can fish drakes now?
There are 2 new winter sea creatures: The Nutcracker and the Reindrake, both with their new unique items. The Reindrake also rewards gifts to every player who shoots it while it is still alive!
### New Mining Area - The Glacial Cave!
The zone inside the cave is now an Ice Mine. If you look carefully, you'll find special items hidden inside the ice!
Don't forget to bring a Silk Touch pickaxe!
### North Stars!
During the whole month of December, collecting Gifts has a chance to reward you with North Stars, which can be spent in a special Event Shop located inside the Winter Island.
### Ice Essence Shop
Similarly to the recent Spider and End essence shops, the Ice essence shop lets you spend all your precious Ice Essence on multiple new perks.
### Miscellaneous
- The loot table of gifts has been improved to reward more coins, new items, and no more Bottles of Jyrre...
- There is a new Glacial talisman line and Frozen Scythe upgrade obtainable from Ice Mining in the Glacial Cave.
- Ice Essence can now be obtained while fishing on the island and from multiple Winter Sea Creatures.
- The giant snowman shop is now a large house with more things to purchase!
## SkyBlock December Extreme Bingo
Starting today and for the first 2 weeks of December, the SkyBlock Bingo Event is Extreme! The tasks are a lot harder but reward double points!
This is the first Extreme Bingo event, so let us know what you think.

# Changelog/2022/December 8/0
Categories: 2022 Updates, Updates
0.17.1
SkyBlock 0.17.1 - Kuudra Infernal Tier, Party Finder and more!
Updated: December 8, 2022
Hello,
Today we are releasing the final tier of the Kuudra boss fight, as long as the Kuudra party finder and more!
► Kuudra Infernal Tier
The fifth and final tier of Kuudra has been released, but be warned: it is extremely difficult!
The Infernal tier introduces:
- 4 new attributes
- 1 new boss phase
- 3 Kuudra Teeth instead of 1 (Tiers 3 and 4 will now reward 2 Kuudra Teeth)
► Kuudra Party Finder
Like the Catacombs Party Finder, you can now search for or create parties for each Kuudra Tier.
► Crimson Essence shop
Vulcan is a new NPC that appears right next to the Kuudra Boss fight's entrance once you complete a faction quest. They offer 8 new perks, 4 related to the boss fight, and the remaining 4 provide perks useful on the Crimson Isle.
► Kuudra Boss Collection
Obtaining the Kuudra pet through RNG simply wasn't the way to go. We are introducing a new Boss Collection, especially for Kuudra, that you can find in your Collection menu.
Completing higher tiers of Kuudra will reward more progression in that collection. For example, the basic tier gives 1 progress, while the infernal tier gives 5.
► A couple of item nerfs
The Wither Cloak will now remain active for a maximum of 10 seconds, followed by a 10 second cooldown. The cooldown will be reduced to 5 seconds if you choose to deactivate it yourself. We believe the item will remain very strong but will no longer offer permanent invulnerability.
The Werewolf armor passive is very niche but was overpowered in that niche. The teammate healing has been nerfed from 10% to 1%. The armor should still excel in its intended use.
► Bug fixes
- Fixed some mobs attacking guests and not being affected by intimidation accessories
- Fixed crafting fireworks deleting other items in the crafting grid
- Fixed Emerald Minion lore displaying incorrect time between actions on Stranded mode
- Fixed Snow minion not using storage while player is on the server
- Fixed Foraging minions sometimes growing vanilla trees
- Fixed not being able to Quick-Upgrade Mushroom Minion using Brown Mushroom
- Fixed "The Park - Spawn" Fast Travel warp being labelled as obtainable in Cocoa Beans V collection instead of Birch Collection VIII
- Fixed Flower Minion XII not showing up in the Flower Minion Recipes menu
- Fixed the crafting recipe for Clay Minion 1 using a Wooden Pickaxe instead of a Wooden Shovel
- Fixed Glowstone Gauntlet saying kills instead of blocks mined
- Fixed incorrect plurality when filling your Quiver with 1 arrow
- Fixed typos when creating a profile
- Fixed space missing between "enchanted hopper" and "here" in Automated Shipping
- Fixed Chicken minion simulation not producing raw chicken
- Fixed an issue where dialogue was being sent with no delay between lines in a lot of places
- Fixed the NPCs walking through the Perk furniture during the forgotten skull meetup
- Fixed Elle saying she’s giving you a batphone but actually doesn’t
- Fixed the quest log showing objectives in the wrong order
- Fixed the dean being visible when you are trying to steal his research
That's it for today; thanks for reading!

# Changelog/2022/February 2
Categories: 2022 Updates, Updates
PATCH
[February 2] Balancing Patch
Updated: February 2, 2022
Hello,
We are releasing quite a few balancing changes to pets, enchantments, and items today:
## Enchantment Changes
In short, the goal of enchantment balancing is to nerf One for All and buff regular enchantments so they match the strength of One for All. One for All should be in a spot where it is still the best option for raw damage in multiple scenarios, but a fully maxed out enchantment setup can out-dps OFA in most situations.
To buff enchantments, we not only made higher tiers scale harder than lower tiers, but we also revamped some of the less useful enchantments, such as Fire Aspect, Venomous, Thunderlord/bolt.
- One For All is now additive damage and not multiplicative, granting +500% damage.
- Titan Killer buffed to 2/4/6/8/12/16/20% per 100 defense, and now caps at 6/12/18/24/40/60/80% for levels 1-7.
- Giant Killer buffed to 0.1/0.2/0.3/0.4/0.6/0.9/1.2% per 1% HP, now caps at 5/10/15/20/30/45/65% for levels 1-7.
- Prosecute buffed to 0.1/0.2/0.3/0.4/0.7/1.0% per % HP the target has left.
- Prosecute now caps at 40% at level 6.
- Execute level 6 buffed from 1.2 to 1.25%.
- Fire Aspect 1/2 deals 3/6% of your total damage per second.
- Venomous now increases damage inflicted on the monster by +0.3/0.6/0.9/1.2/1.5/1.8% damage per hit, stacking up to 40 hits globally with other players. Stacks last 5 seconds.
- Thunderlord deals to 20/40/60/90/125/160% of your total damage when it procs.
- Thunderbolt deals 10/20/30/45/65/80% of your total damage when it procs.
- Sharpness buffed to 5/10/15/20/30/45/65% bonus damage for levels 1-7.
- Lethality now correctly reduces a % of the current defense of the enemy monster.
- Lethality is changed to:
  - 1.2/2.4/3.6/4.8/6/9% defense reduction per stack.
  - Max of 4 stacks instead of 5.
  - Duration of the effect reduced from 8 to 4 seconds.
- Ender Slayer buffed to 15/30/45/60/80/100/130% on endermen for levels 1-7.
- Cleave level 6 buffed from 18% to 20%.
- Critical levels 6 and 7 buffed to 70/100 Crit Damage.
- Smite buffed to 10/20/30/40/60/80/100% on Zombies for levels 1-7.
- Cubism buffed to 10/20/30/40/60/80/100% on Slimes and Magma Cubes for levels 1-7.
- Bane of Arthropods buffed to 10/20/30/40/60/80/100% on spiders and silverfishes for levels 1-7.
- Caster's lore now shows correct values.
- Impaling buffed to 25/50/75%.
- Impaling now works on all Sea Creatures.
- Impaling can now be added to fishing rods.
- Fortune level 4 buffed from 40 to 45 Mining Fortune.
- Power level 6 and 7 buffed to 50/65%.
The reason why Fire Aspect, Thunderlord, and Thunderbolt enchantments are now the only ones to multiply with all other damage increments is because they are creating new instances of damage (there is another number appearing on the screen). We believe it is more intuitive if all damage values scale the same way.
And here are a couple more enchantment-related changes:
- Doubled the odds to get ultra rare rewards in the experimentation table.
- Doubled the odds to find Chimera Books from Minos Inquisitors.
## Pet Changes
Some of those changes might seem big but a large amount of thinking and testing led to them. Please test and let us know.
Golden Dragon Pet:
- Add a base magic find of 10 on golden dragon (+0.5 every 10 levels, starting from 0)
- The Shining Scale perk grants the magic find and strength to the BASE stats of the pet.
- Buff "Legendary Treasure" perk of golden dragon from 0.2% to 0.25%.
- Show the exact damage boost of the golden dragon legendary treasure (more decimals).
Turtle Pet:
- Buffed "Turtle Tactics" from 20% to 30% defense
- Change "Genius Amniote" to give 30 defense for every player around you, max of 5 players
- Add a legendary perk: Turtle Shell: When under 33% maximum HP, take 25% less damage.
JellyFish Pet:
- Change "Radiant Regeneration" to reduce mana cost of the Power Orbs by up to 50%, instead of the aoe healing. Rest of the perk is unchanged
- Replace "Hungry Healer" to "Stored Energy": Reduces the cooldown of Wish by 1 (0.01 per level) second every 2000 HP you heal whilst in dungeons.
- Change "Powerful Potions" to: While in dungeons, boosts the effectiveness of Dungeon Potion by 50% (0.5% per level)
Scatha Pet:
- The Scatha Pet is now upgradable:
- Rare->Epic costs 125m coins, 64x Enchanted Hard Stone, and 7 days
- Epic->Legendary costs 250m coins, 256x Enchanted Hard Stone, and 14 days
- Scatha pet drop chance is now 4x higher from Scatha mobs
Baby Yeti Pet:
- Nerf "Ice Shield" of the Baby Yeti from 100% to 50% of your strength as defense on Epic Baby Yeti.
- Nerf "Ice Shield" of the Baby Yeti from 100% to 75% of your strength as defense on Legendary Baby Yeti.
More Pet Balancing:
- Wither Skeleton Pet's "Wither Blood" perk nerfed from 50 to 25% damage to withers.
- Blaze Pet's "Nether Embodiment" nerfed from 20% to 10%.
- Blue Whale Pet's "Bulk" nerfed from 3 defense to 1 defense per 20 HP.
- Tiger Pet's "Merciless Swipe" nerfed from 50% to 30% ferocity.
## Item Changes
- Gemstone base Sell Price nerfed from 4 to 3.
- Nether Wart Sell Price nerfed from 3 to 2.
- Infinityboom TNT insta blows up walls and crypts when tnt placed near them.
- Maxor Armor Crit Damage increased from 30 to 45 on each piece.
- Each Piece of Maxor Armor gets 5% extra arrow damage.
- Nerfed End Stone Sword to 50% damage reduction max.
- Voidwalker Katana
  - Receive 3% less damage from endermen when held.
  - Damage to endermen buffed from 100% to 200%.
- Voidedge Katana
  - Receive 6% less damage from endermen when held.
  - Damage to endermen buffed from 150% to 300%.
- Vorpal Katana
  - Receive 9% less damage from endermen when held.
  - Damage to endermen buffed from 200% to 400%.
- Atomsplit Katana
  - Receive 12% less damage from endermen when held.
  - Damage to endermen buffed from 250% to 500%.
- Final Destination Armor
  - Buffed Strength from 50->100.
  - Buffed intelligence multiplier from 1.25->1.5.
  - Vivacious Darkness now only procs while sneaking in combat.
  - Vivacious Darkness now grants +100% additive damage against endermen.
## Miscellaneous
- Fishing coin treasures get buffed with fishing level, the max coins at each level are multiplied by (1 + fishing level / 2.5). This does not apply on private islands.
- Nerfed maximum damage of Fabled reforge from 20% to 15%.
- Changed End Stone Sword recipe to be more consistent with other sword recipes
- Changed Fishing Minion recipe to use a Fishing Rod instead of a Wood Sword
- Changed Golem Hat recipe to use 5 Iron Blocks instead of 40 Iron Ingots, which fixes it being uncraftable
- Added a description to the Hyper Furnace
- Fixed typo in Booster Cookie Menu
- Fixed Guests being able to collide with entities
- Added a way to craft Stairs the opposite way
- Fixed upgrading minions from the menu not giving Carpentry exp
- Added Enchantments Guide menu to the Enchanting Skill menu
- Fixed not being able to add 7th/8th coop members after purchasing the profile upgrade.
- Fixed Oringo not appearing on Stranded islands if you were not online at the start of the event.
There you go, the game is perfectly balanced. See you in a few weeks for another balancing patch.

# Changelog/2022/February 7
Categories: 2022 Updates, Updates
PATCH
[February 7] Some Bug Fixes
Updated: February 7, 2022
Hey all!
Here is a small collection of bug fixes from over the last week.
## Fixes
- Added an Entity Limiter to private islands.
- Fixed a rare server crash with the Armadillo entity.
- Fixed an issue where Skeletal Defense would fail to proc.
- Fixed some issues with the Drill menu that could lead to the drill item being duplicated or removed.
- Prevented throwables being used in The End island.
- Fixed Showcase Blocks stacking and subsequently deleting themselves when you pick them up.
- Fixed Enchanted Wet Sponge not working in the Super Compactor.
- Fixed some missing item skill XP values when collecting from minions.
  - Categories
  Mining Enchanted Packed Ice (12,800 XP) Enchanted Hard Stone (57.6 XP) Combat Enchanted Blaze Rod (7,680 XP) Farming Enchanted Cactus (12,800 XP) Super Egg (28.8 XP) Hay Bale (1.8 XP) Enchanted Hay Bale (288 XP) Tightly Tied Hay Bale (41,472 XP) Polished Pumpkin (7,680 XP) Alchemy Enchanted Sugar (16 XP) Enchanted Sugar Cane (2,560 XP) Mutant Nether Wart (7,680 XP)
- Prevented spawning Villagers from spawn eggs again.
- Added lots of additional checks to prevent manipulation of NPCs and other entities in Crystal Hollows and Dungeons.
- Lots of internal fixes that make rolling updates a lot smoother.
## Notes on the Entity Limiter
This is pretty simple in comparison to the previously mentioned physics limiter but here we go.
#### What does this mean?
Basically, when an entity spawns into the world (either mob or item) it is added to a global counter of how many entities of that type can exist at any given time. Once this limit has been reached new entities of that type will not spawn in the world.
This has been active for about a week now with very few complaints but ultimately allows us to better control drops from farms and reduce load on private islands.
#### What to expect.
Certain entities like mob drops or Cactus will now have their total limited when a large number of them are in the world space. This shouldn't affect average players but is mainly aimed at extremely large farms or people abusing lots of entities to lag servers.
It is also worth noting that breaking chests filled with items should still be fine with this change but we do not guarantee it will always work. Best to just take items out by hand.
Overall this change will make a positive improvement to server performance and prevent extra load from unneeded entities whilst leaving players largely unaffected.
And that's it for today, thanks again!

# Changelog/2022/January 13
Categories: 2022 Updates, Updates
PATCH
[January 13] Armadillo, Double Jump, and more fixes!
Updated: January 13, 2022
Hi all!
Welcome to our first set of patch notes for 2022! Over the past few weeks we've been working to fix a bunch of issues that cropped up over the holiday period, so here is a nice break down of what we have done so far.
## Fixes
- Armadillo Pet
  - Fixed the pet not consuming energy when moving in some directions.
  - Fixed instant breaking of Gemstones regardless of item Breaking Power.
  - Fixed the Tunneler ability breaking string from traps in Crystal Hollows.
  - Prevented item requirements being bypassed whilst riding the pet.
  - Fixed an issue with autopet rules regenerating all the pet's energy.
  - Fixed general issues with the energy increasing faster than it should.
  - You can no longer shoot the Armadillo for points during the Jerry's Workshop event.
  - Fixed collections not being counted when not using Telekinesis on the pet.
  - Improved the responsiveness of the Armadillo Pet when you first get on it.
- Double Jump Boots
  - Reworked how items that have the "Double Jump" item ability work to reduce exploits.
  - Instead of double tapping spacebar it now functions by pressing your sneak key (L-SHIFT by default) whilst in the air.
  - Fixed an issue where the Tarantula Pet perk "Eight Legs" was not decreasing the mana cost of these boots and Spirit Boots accordingly.
  - To help with this change the height of the jump has also been slightly buffed.
  - This change will also interfere less with Cookie Buff and Mushroom Soup flight.
- General Pet Changes
  - We have taken this opportunity to rework how our internal pet system works to hopefully help prevent any further "pet stacking" issues.
  - This was tested on alpha for a few weeks but be aware that there may be some small differences from the old system to watch out for.
  - Due to this rework we now have the added benefit of Pet Rules now supporting custom items in the UIs properly.
- Misc
  - Added distance checks to the following areas:
    - Dungeon Terminal Puzzles
    - Player Profile Menus
  - Added a cooldown to Slayer Minibosses spawning to prevent exploits.
  - Fixed an issue where player-specific mobs such as sea creatures or special zealots would give the pickup priority to the wrong player
  - You can now use "metric suffixes" (e.g 12k or 1m) on the Bazaar price input.
  - Fixed some client crashes when on 1.8-1.13 in certain menus.
  - Fixed some issues with Spirit Boots:
    - Fixed armor slot positioning being wrong.
    - Fixed the Tarantula pet perk "Eight Legs" not affecting the mana cost of this item.
@TheMGRF & @skyerzz - Bugs

# Changelog/2022/January 18
Categories: 2022 Updates, Updates
0.12.2
[January 18] SkyBlock Stranded
Updated: January 18, 2022
Hello,
The first time you joined Hypixel SkyBlock, you quickly took that portal and landed on the same hub millions of other players wandered on, but what if...
What if you never took that portal?
## What is SkyBlock Stranded?
Stranded is a new mode where players stay on their own island forever. The rules are as follow:
- Solo or Co-op.
- Ironman rules.
- You cannot leave your island.
The primary goal of Stranded is to unlock all the collections, but since most of them are not available directly from your island in Hypixel SkyBlock, many new mechanics were added to Stranded to implement them all. We won't explain all the changes, but here are the largest ones:
- Fishing Sea Creatures is available on your island.
- There is a new icon inside the SkyBlock menu that lets you spawn specific SkyBlock NPCs anywhere you want.
- If you can obtain Villagers, they have a custom trading system.
All the SkyBlock collections are currently available, except Mithril and Hard Stone, but no more spoilers. You'll have to figure out how to obtain the other collections through Jerry!
## How do I play Stranded?
Exactly like Ironman, when creating a new profile you can select Stranded under the Gamemode category.
## What is the future of Stranded?
Stranded will evolve along SkyBlock, as we plan on adding more of the content directly on your island. Also, it is possible that many Stranded features will get added to the main mode.
Thanks for reading, have fun!

# Changelog/2022/July 27
Categories: 2022 Updates, Updates
PATCH
[July 27] Bug fixes
Updated: July 27, 2022
Hey everyone!
I'm MFN, a new SkyBlock developer at Hypixel! I've been working on some bug fixes and changes that are listed below:
## ► Bug Fixes
- If your inventory is full when a special Zealot drops a Summoning Eye, it now goes to your stash
- The Blessing fishing rod enchantment now drops multiple single-item stacks (e.g. Music Discs)
- Pickobulus ability no longer breaks portal blocks
- Precursor Eye lazer is now deactivated when swapping helmets
- Firework Star is no longer listed twice when choosing a Storage Ender Chest icon
- Goblin eggs no longer hatch in the Dungeon Hub
- InfiniDirt wand now works with farmland blocks
- Trick or Treat Chest drops can no longer be picked up by other players
- When leveling up, the Pristine enchantment now has a tooltip
- Aurora Staff no longer fires through blocks
- Dying while riding an Armadillo or Rock no longer teleports you back to them on death
- Jax shows Sell hopper instead of Close barrier
- Blast o' Lantern can no longer be placed
- NPC shops now show the correct amount purchased when bulk-buying
- Fixed bypassing NPC shop daily purchase limit
- Fixed clipping through walls using the Horse pet
- Fishing bag now only contains fishing items
- InfiniTorch no longer takes money when you can't place it
- Farming for Dummies enchanted book displays Farming Fortune stat in the Anvil menu
- Crafting a new minion or joining a co-op now notifies co-op members using your actual username and rank
- Fixed God Potions and other effects wiping in Dungeons
## ► Other Changes
- Equipping a Skeleton Helmet no longer starts with 3 bones; though it regenerates as normal
That's all for now and I hope you continue to enjoy SkyBlock

# Changelog/2022/July 28
Categories: 2022 Updates, Updates
PATCH
[July 28] Auction House Maximum Bid Limit Increase
Updated: July 28, 2022
Hey,
Today we rolled some changes to the backend systems for the Auction House. One of the included changes was to remove the 32-bit integer limitation. This now means that bids are no longer limited to 2.1 billion (2,147,483,647) and instead we have introduced a new limit of 10 billion.
That's all for now! We will continue to monitor and provide any future updates.

# Changelog/2022/June 2
Categories: 2022 Updates, Updates
PATCH
Changes to Cake Souls and Spooky Pies
Updated: June 2, 2022
Hey Guys
We've just rolled an update to skyblock that makes the following changes:
- Cake Souls will no longer show any names other than the captured soul, the time of capture and the name of the player who captured it.
- Once a soul is captured, the item will become coop-soulbound
- Spooky Pies are now coop-soulbound.
We understand that this change can be sudden and weird to most players but it is necessary for the health of the game at this time. This change exists to combat some of the more obscure parts of boosting and coin selling. Ultimately, this is something we should have done earlier, and have decided to rip the band-aid off and do now.

Hey all!
Over the last few weeks we have fixed an absolute boat load of bugs, however we've been incredibly busy so we have a big backlog of patch notes. That said, here's a big cumulative list of all of them!
Bug Fixes
- Fixed Dungeon Master Mode 7 mage beams not dealing the correct damage to dragons

  - Fixed Redstone now always giving extra drops in Crystal Hollows
  - Fixed Redstone sometimes not working with the Compact Enchantment
  - Fixed Redstone sometimes not firing for the Refined reforge
- Fixed Slayer Bosses regenerating health after they have already died
- Fixed Pickonimbus durability not degrading
- Fixed Drill rarity not updating in chat messages after being recombobulated
- Fixed applying drill upgrades taking the entire stack
- Fixed Smelting Touch disabling Farming Fortune
- Fixed Juju Shortbow hitting 3 mobs on impact sometimes not working
- Fixed Horseman's Candle being placeable in Stranded mode
- Fixed Mercenary Armor set bonus not working in Stranded
- Fixed Zealots not aggro-ing on Stranded
- Refined reforge fixes:
- Fixed Refined reforge giving Enchanted Ink Sacs when mining Lapis
- Fixed Refined not dropping Enchanted Mithril when mining Mithril
- Refined reforge now gives a chat message on activation like Compact and Pristine
- Fixed emptying an empty gemstone sack sending an inaccurate message
- Fixed the Suus NPC quest becoming impossible to complete after the first attempt
- Fixed issues with translations in several Crystal Hollows messages
- Fixed Maxwell reporting incorrect stats
- Fixed a large variety of stat items reporting incorrect stats after the Crimson Isle update
- Fixed Drill Engine Parts not showing their 2nd stat boost
- Fixed several Mythic Flying Fish perks not working
- Fixed Explosive Bow causing lag spikes and added a mob cap (like Gauntlet of Contagion)

- Terror Armor Fixes:
  - Fixed Terror armor giving stacks when attacking players/other creatures that players can't attack

- Fixed Fire Aspect damage being affected by Rekindled Ember armor when it's not equipped
- Fixed necromancy souls blocking their owner's arrows
- Fixed drill fuel notifications always using the default amount when there's a fuel tank or something that gives it more fuel capacity
- Fixed a method of getting negative drill fuel

- Allow players to claim the "Scam?" achievement by clicking on Bruuh again, if they've paid

- Hungry Hiker:
  - Fixed the hiker disappearing when he shouldn't and getting people soft-locked out of the quest
  - Swapped out the Rabbit Stew item for 30x Cooked Chicken

- Fixed second inferno apex not dropping from the minion

- Fixed not being able to use co-op chat as a ghost
- Fixed the Builder’s Wand incorrectly giving the “You are too close to the target block!” error message
- Fixed Bonzo's Staff no longer being usable on Private Islands
- Fixed Blessing 6 not being shown in Enchantment Guide
- Fixed the Basket of seeds says it is empty when it is not
- Fixed 5 Swing Range from Dark Claymore not stacking with the +0.1 melee range per Dominus stack from Crimson Armor
- Fixed Flaming Fists not doing correct damage
- Fixed Felix NPCs outdated dialogue
- Fixed Dungeoneering level up chat message displaying the incorrect health reward
- Fixed Combat level up message displaying incorrect reforge information
- Fixed some more collection requirement bypasses
- Renamed "Enchanted Ink Sack" to "Enchanted Ink Sac"
- Fixed Minions sometimes not working when offline
- Soup Flight Duration is now displayed while guesting
- Fixed a bug where this thread displayed as April instead of June
- Fixed a lot of typos.
QOL Additions
- The Teleport Pad cap has been increased to 28 after copious internal fixes and optimisations
- The Rock Paper Shears social item now displays extra info after each round and always go to a final round with 2 players
- Improved Private Island name filtering
- Gift items will now give directly to the player's inventory/stash instead of dropping on the ground, although a cosmetic drop still shows on the ground when opened.
- Updated the Dungeon Note system to use an Anvil UI to allow longer messages and improved its chat filter settings
- Improved the Quick Craft recipe order for Thunder and Magma Lord equipment
- Made the Personal Deletor work with minions
