- Applying a new drill part now returns old ones if you have enough coins
- You can now sell to Jax in the Hub
Many thanks for reporting all the bugs, we're doing our best to keep up to date and fix them as they are coming in. Make sure to continue reporting them HERE.

# Changelog/2022/June 23
Categories: 2022 Updates, Updates
This post contained non-SkyBlock information, which has been omitted for clarity.
ANNOUNCEMENT
Summer Event and Sale 2022
Updated: June 23, 2022
Hey all!
The Hypixel Summer Event is upon us and we have some big changes hopefully making summer a little cooler (pun intended) for everyone! We've put together some new content for players to enjoy on the server during the summer months! This summer will end on September 13th. Here's what's included:
- Summer Sale - up to 25% off on the in-game store!
- Summer Sale - up to 20% off the web store!
- New Summer Achievements
- Main Lobby Reskin with
- A whole new system for players to get summer cosmetics
- New SkyBlock summer bundles
- Summer Themed Maps
Read on for more detail about what's new!
## ► Summer Sale!
Our summer sale is back with the return of Golden Mystery Boxes and Golden Loot Chests for Bed Wars, SkyWars, and Murder Mystery!! Our summer sale will offer up to 25% discount on everything in the store in-game, while the web store will offer up to 20% discount.
Check out the web store here!
## ► SkyBlock Summer Bundles
Our SkyBlock Summer Bundles for ranked players return from last year with something new in each one! Below are previews of the new Sprays, Minion Skins, and Furniture from the higher rank bundles.
New Summer Sprays
New Summer Furniture
New Summer Minion Skins
## ► Regarding the future of Seasonal Events on Hypixel
As you guys will quickly notice, one major thing we are doing with this event is to try out moving away from randomized loot crates for new cosmetics. Over the last few years in the gaming industry, we've noticed a changing trend toward player perception of loot boxes - while they are initially fun, random chances to obtain completely random things can get frustrating to players, especially once someone decides they want something specific.
To mix things up a bit, we've decided to go to somewhat of a progression model but completely tied to the existing Hypixel ranks system. This way, we give more to our ranked players, while also allowing players to target specific cosmetics they want to earn through the new Event Silver currency.
Ultimately, our goal will be to completely phase out mystery boxes and loot chests from the entirety of Hypixel and allow players to earn or purchase cosmetics directly through other means. For regular mystery box/loot chest cosmetics, Hypixel Gold will become the main way to obtain them at reasonable prices, while event cosmetics will always be incorporated into the Event Silver system. We have not yet decided exactly how we're going to handle mystery dust, but they won't be forgotten in the conversion.
We really hope you guys enjoy the new changes and we're looking forward to upgrading the system even further for future events!

# Changelog/2022/March 1
Categories: 2022 Updates, Updates
PATCH
[0.12.3] Master Mode 7
Updated: March 1, 2022
Hello,
Over a year and half ago we released the Catacombs, and today we are releasing the last major update of this dungeon. We won't reveal much of the details of this update, but we can explain our mindset. As some of you know, shortly before releasing Floor 7 we had to take the difficult decision to stop producing Catacombs floors and revamping the Maxor fight to Necron's. The result was the removal of 3 planned boss fights and awkward voicelines in the Necron boss fight. Working on Floor 7 was a great experience and ever since release we had this strong urge to not only fix the boss fight but also add the 3 missing bosses back.
This update features:
- Multiple changes to Floor 7.
- Addition of Master Mode 7.
- Master Mode 6 regular monsters are now dealing less damage.
- Due to Floor 7 being slower to complete, the EXP and Loot from it are buffed accordingly.
- In the Higher or Lower secret room, Archer’s “Double Shot” perk is now disabled.
- The EXP gain from Floors 1-3 is increased by 20%
- Bats have permanent fire resistance
- Infinity boom cannot be sold to NPCs anymore, no more misclicks!
- Added an indication that calculates the run’s total score so far.
- Players can now choose who they want to teleport to with the Ghost Haunt ability.
- The homing Wither Skulls from Withermancers, Wither Miners, Wither Guards and other mobs of that type will now despawn if they haven't hit the target player in 5 seconds.
- Spring Boots effect will now properly be removed when dead and after being revived.
## Custom Rare Dyes
We also added multiple custom dyes to the game. At the moment the custom dyes are dropped from various places in the game, and are exceedingly rare. We will monitor how many of them are dropped in the game and might buff their rates over time.
As you can see from the screenshot above, when applied to an item, said item will have a new icon next to their name and the color of the dye will be displayed at the bottom, to make sure there is no confusion with regular items, or exotic items.
The pure white and pure black dyes are not currently available. Those 2 dyes will probably become very expensive bits items, simply because we don't want those 2 dyes to overshadow all the other ones, and also because some players have way too many bits. This is still up for debate.
## Catacombs levels
We are very happy with the power level and the amount of exp required to reach Catacombs 50. It would not make any sense for us to give players an incentive to keep leveling up above 50, but for those that do play the Catacombs after 50, we have now added cosmetic levels above 50. They are purely for bragging rights and don't function with any item or perk that scales with catacombs levels. Let us know what you think about this addition to the game.
## Credits
Master Mode 7 development and design: @Nitroholic_ and I Phase 5 Build: @Plummel Custom Rare Dyes: @william

Hey all!
We are here to inform you about the latest update that was just rolled to SkyBlock! Those were rolled to live last night, actually, but we still want to ensure you guys are aware of the change, and why.
- The Legion Ultimate Enchantment will no longer gain a buff from AFK players.

# Changelog/2022/November 9
Categories: 2022 Updates, Updates
0.16.1
SkyBlock Patch 0.16.1 - Spider's Den QoL, new mayor and more!
Updated: November 9, 2022
Hello,
Every now and then islands need to be improved to the new standards, today it's the Spider's Den! Oh, and there is a new Mayor in town.
## ► Spider's Den QoL
The Spider's Den is now the only combat island before the End, therefore we needed to make its content more early game so new players have more content before they reach combat level 12.
### Arachne Changes
The main takeaway is that Arachne, her Brood and her Keepers, are now a LOT easier to fight. Here's the list of the changes: Arachne(s), Arachne's Brood and Arachne Keepers now have:
- Much less health
- Much less damage
- Poison tics more often but deals way less damage
- Have improved basic drops (coins, combat exp, strings and eyes)
- New voicelines
- No more cobwebs spawn from the boss itself
- Beating Arachne grants 8 and 30 Spider Essence respectively
- Arachne's Keepers have 6 new spawn points (previously 3) and spawn a lot more often.
- Arachne's Keeper Fragments are now called Arachne's Calling, and have a better description.
- Dark Queen's Soul Drop is now simply called Dark Queen's Soul.
On top of that we added a new Armor Set and a new Sword, for early game:
Arachne's Armor has an 8 Set Bonus, once you wear all 8 pieces, you gain an extra 100 Health and Defense, quite decent early game! The Armor Pieces are dropped by Arachne, while the equipments are dropped by the Keepers. This armor is quite easy to obtain, therefore it is soulbound!
Arack is a new early game sword obtained from Arachne
The boss also has a chance to drop Arachne's Fang, an item needed to upgrade the Vaccine Talisman into it's ring form.
Both Arachne's Armor and Sword are now in the museum, as well as the Void Sword and Dragon Shortbow from the previous update.
### Spider Tamer
This man loves spiders, but hates Arachne...
The Spider Essence shop features 4 new perks, and a Travel Scroll.
- Empowered Agility reduces the mana cost of movement abilities.
- Vermin Control reduces your damage taken by spiders.
- Bane increases your damage towards spiders.
- Spider Training increases your Combat Wisdom while in the Spider's Den.
### Other changes
#### Shaggy
This NPC was a little bit too mysterious before. Shaggy now gives players simple scoreboard objectives to better guide players through the island content.
#### The Archeologist gives a new accessory
This item is there to help players find all the relics on their own. And if you happen to lose your compass fear not, Rusty will find it!
#### Bow balancing
Spider's Queen Stinger now has double damage (it simply doubles the damage of its ability) Venom's Touch damage is buffed from 200 to 250, and the starred version is buffed from 300 to 370 damage.
#### New areas names
Each main point of interest of the Spider's Den now has its own official area name.
#### Thunderstorms
The Spider's Den has always had a weather system, but now each rain has a chance to be a Thunderstorm.
Thunderstorms show a little lightning on the scoreboard and have the particularity of spawning giant Toxic Slimes, which have a chance to drop a new item, the Poison Sample.
On top of that, Rain on the Spider's Den now grants 50 extra Fishing Speed, and Thunderstorms grant an extra 3 Sea Creature Chance.
#### Vaccine ring and artifact
The Vaccine Talisman now only reduces poison by 10%, however it now works on Arachne's Venom. It also has 2 new tiers: The Vaccine Ring can be crafted from the Talisman, with 8 Arachne's Fangs The Vaccine Artifact can be crafted from the Ring, with 8 Poison Samples
#### Promising shovel
This item is now slightly more useful.
On top of obtaining all the way up to Efficiency X, it now has a chance to drop double flint, up to 10%.
## ► Finnegan
Finnegan is a new regular Mayor, he's a close friend to Jacob as you probably noticed.
## ► Bug Fixes
- Fixed the Builder NPC having some items twice
- Fixed the Shredder not activating some autopet rules.
- Fixed being able to update signs while muted.
- Changed the recipe of the Terminator bow so everyone can craft it (same amount of items)
## ► A note on future updates
As usual, we wanted to give you guys a quick glimpse on what's in the pipeline for SkyBlock. Please note that anything we talk about is subject to change (or may not even release!).
### Kuudra Tier 5
Kuudra Tier 5 is about ready to release, and should be expected in a week or two.
### Winter Island QoL
We are considering improving a few aspects of the Jerry Island. Details on this will come up at a later date (or with its release).
### Garden Update (part 1)
This update encountered some technical difficulties during Alpha testing (which is why we alpha tested to begin with), and we do not believe that it'll be ready for the next release, therefore it should release early 2023. We're sorry for the delay!
### SkyBlock Levels follow up
A follow up update to SkyBlock Levels is coming up soon, and has reached later stages of development. It should arrive on Alpha in the next few weeks.
### Wizard Portal
While there is still a big chunk of work to do, we are getting confident with an early 2023 release.
### Garden Update (part 2)
There will be more to The Garden at some point after its release, but it is very much too early to say how much, or when.
### Foraging update
We will have more to talk about with the Foraging Update when we release our final 1.0 content thread. Until then, we are not at a point where we can reveal much about the update just yet.
As always, take these previews with a grain of salt - a lot of it is subject to change. We are trying to be more transparent about what we're working on, but we also don't want to lock ourselves away from letting creativity take control. Only time will tell.
Credits:
@aPunch - Spider's Den QoL @Minikloon - New Mayor @Bloozing, @Citria and QA Team - Testing/QA @Jayavarmen - Game Design Mayor Finnegan was designed by a nerd team member @Stuffy! Remember that this list is not exhaustive. Developers on this list are often working on other projects.
Thanks for reading! And many thanks for continuing to report issues as we try and work our way through them. A special thanks to the players who helped in the testing and design of this update!

# Changelog/2022/October 19
Categories: 2022 Updates, Updates
0.16
SkyBlock Patch 0.16 - Kuudra Revamp, End Island QoL, Bits items and more!
Updated: October 19, 2022
Hello,
Today we are improving existing content for both early and late-game players.
## ► Kuudra Revamp
Since their release, Kuudra has been a little sick, but they feel much better now. Here are the most important significant changes:
### Shop Revamp
The shop still uses tokens, but instead of earning your own tokens, now whenever someone gains tokens, all players obtain the same amount. Once you have at least 50 tokens, you'll be able to choose a Route; there are 4 of them:
- Cannoneer: Perfect for protecting your teammates from tentacles, and dealing with flying creatures.
- Crowd Control: If your party is struggling with the waves of monsters, someone should take this route!
- Strategist: The best route to accomplish all objectives faster, a must-pick!
- Support: If you are strong enough to deal with the monsters but still want to help your teammates in the process.
Choosing a route means you'll have to stick to it for the rest of the fight. Inside each route, there are many perks you can level up.
### Objectives
There are now three distinct phases to the boss fight:
- Retrieving the supplies from the lava
- Building the Ballista
- Loading up and shooting the Ballista
While those 3 phases are very straightforward in the basic tier of the fight, higher tiers get more complicated and will require extra teamwork.
Here's a list of the rest of the changes:
- There is now a wave system going all the way up to 100. There is no reward for seeing all the waves; it is more of a soft difficulty mechanic - the longer you take, the harder it gets.
- The goal isn't to kill the tentacles anymore, but it can sometimes be useful to help your teammates collect supplies.
- You need a lava rod to do some of the objectives, but you can buy one in the shop. You'll also need a pickaxe in higher tiers.
- You can purchase a personal shop to have it in your hotbar for quick access.
- You can now access the shop while dead.
- The entrance fees are removed, however;
  - The free chest has much less loot.
- Paid chests contain way more Crimson Essence, however;
  - The price of the keys went up a lot in the Emissary shops.
- Hollow Armor is now released, along with a few other items that were locked behind higher tier of the fight.
Note: The Infernal Tier (or tier 5) isn't available just yet, but you can expect it to release in the very near future.
## ► End Island
The End has always been a staple of the early to mid-game; it was time to refresh some parts of its content.
### Lone Adventurer
Thanks to many community members, this lost adventurer found his way to the end island. He will teach new players what he learned from this island and even reward them with new items.
### Ender Armor Changes
As time goes by, we understand more and more which items should or shouldn't be soulbound.
The Ender Armor is part of the game's progression and should be easy to obtain - on your own. We are increasing the chances of obtaining the items while removing them from trading places.
- Ender Armor is now soulbound
- Ender Armor drop rate increased to 1%
- Added four new pieces of equipment
  - Ender Cloak - Dropped from Watchers
  - Ender Necklace - Dropped from Endermen
  - Ender Belt - Dropped from Obsidian Defenders
  - Ender Gloves - Dropped from Shiny Blocks
### Zealots Bruiser Hideout
The Dragon's Nest can be a pretty crowded and hostile place to new players who can barely deal with Zealots. Those portals teleport players to the Zealot Bruiser Hideout, where those Endermen reward more Combat EXP, have a greater chance to spawn special Zealots, but are also much stronger.
The goal is to get the strong players away from the nest and give new players a chance at fighting Zealots.
### Draconic Altar and Essence Shop
You can now sacrifice most of the items obtainable on this island, such as dragon gear, ender armor, and more. The main purpose is to obtain Dragon Essence, but sometimes the Altar grants you secondary rewards.
Tyzzo, the Dragon Ritualist, is a new Essence Shop.
Tyzzo only accepts Dragon Essence, and it focuses on activities of the End Island, but not only!
### Shiny Blocks
The loot table of Shiny Blocks got a full revamp, but the most important change is the addition of Endermite Nests. Killing the Endermites from those will drop Mite Gel, which is useful in many recipes and worth a nice sum of coins.
## ► Bits items
Elizabeth is selling a bunch of new Bits Items!
Simple, yet effective!
Very fancy!
Udel keeps on selling subpar phones just so he can sell better ones each year, despicable tactics. But at least with the Abiphone Contacts Trio, you'll have plenty of contacts!
There are now plenty of new Abiphone Cases, each with a different use.
Abicases are Rare Accessories, but only 1 of them will work at a time.
Choose wisely!
Good bugs become features.
Surprisingly good smell.
New unique custom dyes, very expensive, however.
## ► New Abiphone Contacts
Abiphones are getting more and more popular, and many new SkyBlock habitants own one; here is the list:
- Jacob
- Anita
- Fear Mongerer
- Jotraeline
- Mort
- Maxwell
- Jax
- Fred (new NPC in dwarven mines forge)
- Rusty
- George
- Zog
- Tia
- Elizabeth
- Trevor
- St. Jerry
- Geo
- Walter
- Shifty
- Plumber Joe
## ► Fast Travel
Recently the fast travel menu has become cluttered with its current design, so we've opted to segment up the menu into categories to give users a clearer and easier experience while allowing us to expand it in the future better.
## ► Powder Changes
- Moved most buffs to be a percentage extra powder.
- Fixed Powder Buff perk giving incorrect bonuses.
- Fixed the following locations not respecting powder buffs:
  - Picking up powder items (dye)
  - Daily Powder perk
  - Powder rewards from commissions
  - Goblin Killer perk
  - Mining Events
  - Dark Monoliths
  - Puzzler
  - Fetchur
  - Bednom
  - Powder Ghasts
  - Crystal Hollows chests
- Fixed chat messages not using the buffed amount of powder.
- Buffed Dark Monolith drop rates and amounts.
- Front Loaded perk now provides +2 base powder for your first 2500 ores.
- Fixed a lot of lore/color inconsistencies
## ► Bug Fixes
- Fixed normal item requirements not working in dungeons with dungeonized weapons (SLAYER REQ)
- Fixed Catacombs 50 not giving any SkyBlock Levels XP.
- Fixed Catacombs 50 SkyBlock level total not being correct.
- Fixed Spider’s Den missing the apostrophe in the scoreboard.
- Fixed some recipe items displaying the lore in the wrong order.
- Fixed the Emerald Blade being capped at 2M instead of 2B.
- Fixed progress bars in the commission menu being over multiple lines.
- Fixed a lot of “a” and “an” being colored in the dark auction, minion skins, furniture, and training dummies.
- Fixed wrongly colored parenthesis appearing in some collections.
- Fixed all the Mana Ray trophy fish using the same skin.
- Fixed Crystal Hollows Fairy Grotto Biomes not all using the correct zone.
- Fixed Fairy/Butterflies not spawning in Crystal Hollows.
- Fixed the HOTM Front Loaded perk remaining active after being disabled.
- Fixed typos.
Known issue: Magma Boss Bestiary is removed but will be back after a fix.
## ► Balancing
A few recipes changed:
- The Shark Water Orb requires
  - 8 Blue Shark Teeth instead of 16
  - 16 Nurse Shark Teeth instead of 32
  - 12 Enchanted Shark Fins instead of 16
- The Winter Water Orb requires
  - 32 Hunks of Blue Ice instead of 16
  - 16 Green Gifts instead of 8
- The Water Orb now requires
  - 16 Enchanted Prismarine Crystal instead of 0
  - 16 Enchanted Prismarine Shard instead of 32
- Ichthyic Gloves require 24 Blue Shark Teeth instead of 48
- Ichthyic Belt requires 16 Blue Shark Teeth instead of 32
- Ichthyic Cloak requires 16 Blue Shark Teeth instead of 32
The Rampart Armor is now easier to obtain:
- Bezals, Blazes, Wither Skeletons, Wither Specters, and Magma Cubes have much less health than before.
- The drop rate of the armor pieces is buffed from 0.1% to 0.2% on all of those mobs.
## ► A note on future updates
Here's a quick recap of what you can expect in the next few updates!
Happening pretty soon, but in no specific order:
- Garden Update (part 1)
- SkyBlock Levels follow up
- Wizard Portal
Happening moderately soon:
- Garden Update (part 2)
Happening in a little while:
- Foraging update (info soon)
Credits:
@Nitroholic_ - Kuudra Fight
@JacobRuby and @Plummel - Kuudra Tentacle and Ballista animations
@Dctr - End Update
@Minikloon - End Update + Bits items + Abiphone contacts
@TheMGRF - Fast Travel + Powder Changes + Bug Fixes
@Bloozing, @Citria, HPC and QA Team - Testing/QA
@Jayavarmen - Game Design
@Octaverei - Balancing Game Design
@LadyBleu - Skin arts
@Rozsa - End Island build changes
@Cheesey and @fudgiethewhale - Portalizer builds and Kuudra bossfight map changes
@DistrictGecko - Ballista armorstand design.
Remember that this list is not exhaustive. Developers on this list are often working on other projects.
Thanks for reading! And many thanks for continuing to report issues as we try and work our way through them. A special thanks to the players who helped in the testing and design of this update!

# Changelog/2022/September 10
Categories: 2022 Updates, Updates
ANNOUNCEMENT
Regarding the Perfect Gemstone situation (September 9, 2022)
Updated: September 10, 2022
Hey Everyone.
Today, for the health of SkyBlock, we've had to make a very difficult decision.
For the past few months, we have been looking to fix dupes and exploits leading to duplications of perfect gemstones, one of the most desirable items on the market. Sadly, the duplication of those items has gone too far and we have been forced tonight to make a very drastic change.
The first thing you guys need to know is that Perfect and Flawless Gemstones can no longer be stacked. This allows us to more accurately track where they are and how they are handled by players which also allows us better control over exploits related to them.
The second thing that will impact a lot of you is this - to annihilate a lot of contraband, we had to apply this change live. All existing stacks of Perfect and Flawless Gemstones have been turned into one single gemstone per stack.
Please note that this will not affect any Perfect or Flawless Gemstones that are applied to items, still in your forge, already in a stack of one, or that you can currently purchase from the Bazaar. As well, this will not affect stacks of Fine, Flawed, or Rough Gemstones of any type.
While this may sound drastic as it will effect many legitimate players, the amount of contraband that we have just deleted was a massive amount - an amount that we felt ultimately outweighed the amount effected from legitimate players. This will be good for the health of the gemstone economy and will probably lead to an increase of their value. However, we're definitely aware that some legitimate players will be affected by this.
We will be working this week to figure out the best way to handle said legitimate players, but please understand that we may be limited in how we compensate for the losses. Unless major losses are incurred, we may not be able to compensate everyone.
We would like to apologize for any inconvenience this may cause.
EDIT: Make sure you read this reply too!
Hey Guys I thought I'd pop in and answer some frequently asked questions in this thread.
This sucks I lost X and Y and I didn't read the part where you said there would be compensation
We have logs of every single piece of gemstone that were removed and we are planning on slowly returning a certain amount of them - potentially a limit per player. The goal of this was to remove the substantial amount of contraband that was circulating around, to hit hard the players who duped insane amounts of gemstones. A normal player would only have a small amount of gemstones. By doing it this way, the vast majority of players (especially legitimate players) will get most of what they lost back. How we're going to give those back and when is not something I can answer yet, but please understand that we'll do our best to get it done as soon as we can.
Why not just move them to stash when you removed them
The way we had to fix this was only going to happen in one way - any gemstones anyone has of any number stacked together becomes 1. Due to the urgency of the release, this was the most efficient way of making it happen fast before anyone caught wind of us reacting.
Because of this, a large amount of gemstones were removed extremely fast. If we had moved some of those to stash at the same time, the database would have collapsed unto itself. Obviously, no one wants that. The solution is to send lost gemstones through the stash in a slow manner - over the course of a few days. We will be beginning this process as soon as we can.
Why didn't you do anything sooner
Dupes are a plague within SkyBlock, and this much we can all agree. Running this big of a game on a modded interface within a game not designed for it (Minecraft) makes this an incredibly difficult task. Gemstones aren't the cause of the dupe, they are just what was used to dupe - this was not intended to be a fix to a dupe, just a fix to the damages dupes have caused over the last few months.
By making the change we've made, we've vastly reduced the speed at which someone can ruin the economy while duping, but we were sincerely trying to avoid it as much as we can. Especially within minecraft - we're all used to things stacking with each others, and we wanted to keep it that way. Obviously, this turned out to be a bad call, and last night was the last straw that forced our hand.
Hopefully this should shed some light on what's next.
EDIT #2 - We have completed the process of refunding the previously lost gemstones. In some cases more gemstones may have potentially been refunded than original lost, however this was the best option we had at the end of the day. The overall health of the economy and the game itself will be much better off now than had we not removed the large amounts of duped gemstones. We appreciate your patience during this event.

# Changelog/2022/September 15
Categories: 2022 Updates, Updates
PATCH
[September 15] Temporary Minion Simulation Patch
Updated: September 15, 2022
Hello.
Update 10/05/22: Minion simulation has been optimized and there is no longer a cap!
Temporary Change We've noticed that a few players are unable to join the game. It looks like the cause is minion simulation on login. While we optimize the simulation, we've reduced the actions limit accumulated on minions while offline from 100k to 14k.
What does it mean? For most player, nothing changes.
If you have let's say: - 30 cobble minions - Flycatchers - Sc3k - Plasma Buckets
If your island worked yesterday, then all is fine. If you couldn't log on your island, then you probably can today (might require 2 attempts). But with that setup, your offline resources will cap out in about 1 day instead of 10 days.
We are working on a better solution to minion simulation, after which the limit will be upped again. Thank you for your understanding.

# Changelog/2022/September 28
Categories: 2022 Updates, Updates
0.15
SkyBlock Patch 0.15 - Leveling, Balancing, and more!
Updated: September 28, 2022
Hello everyone!
Today we are releasing the next major patch of SkyBlock which includes a new form of progression along with many balancing and QoL changes!
► SkyBlock Leveling Today, we are tying up the multiple forms of denoting progression into a centralized system: SkyBlock Levels. Now, everyone will have a level that shows the progression they have in the game.
You can view the different ways to gain SkyBlock XP along with your specific breakdown:
View an individual breakdown of each XP Task
Along with this, you now see SkyBlock Levels shown in the player list (tab), and while they do not show in chat by default, you have a setting to toggle SkyBlock Levels in chat:
As well as denoting your general progress in the game, SkyBlock Levels also reward you for your progression:
- Gain permanent stat boosts based on your level!
- Various features such as Bazaar, Community Shop, the Wardrobe, and more, have been relocated to SkyBlock Levels progression.
- Unlock a new public Level Prefix at specific levels!
There is still a lot we want to do with levels, such as balancing, adding multiple ways to gain XP and also making it more clear what tasks players should focus on at each stage of their progression.
* Note: Levels are retroactive, upon logging in you will be automatically given a level respective to your progress in the game.
► Changes to Fairy Souls & Backpack Slots With the release of Leveling, Fairy Souls no longer give permanent stat boots. Now, they grant:
- SkyBlock XP
- Unlocking Backpack Slots
  - Backpack Slots 2-18 will now be locked by default. The more Fairy Souls you find, the more Backpack Slots you will unlock.
* Note: Backpacks that are already in locked slots will stay in those slots, no worries!
► Peak of the Mountain The following changes have been made:
- Added Peak of the Mountain 6
  - Cost - 500,000 Gemstone Powder
  - Rewards - +2 Gemstone Powder when mining Gemstones
- Added Peak of the Mountain 7
  - Cost - 750,000 Gemstone Powder
  - Rewards - 1 Token of the Mountain
- Heart of the Mountain 7 Reward: 2 -> 3 Token of the Mountain
► Croesus, Dungeon Treasures There is a new NPC in the Dungeon Hub called Croesus which hoards all the treasures from your dungeon runs and allows you to open them later.
Croesus in the Dungeon Hub around
You can keep up to 60 runs in Croesus, and each run has a 48 hours expiry date, so make sure you open your chests before they disapear!
► General Changes
- Added Curse of Greed effect, applied right now only on the Emerald Blade:
Curse of Greed effect
We are aware of the bug that caused players to lose coins on release, and we will refund ALL THE COINS that players lost from it.
Stay tuned for updates on the situation.
* Note: We felt like the Emerald Blade's risk of losing coins on death was being negated by too many effects that were later added to the game. This should bring back the fear factor of using this powerful weapon, without the need for us to nerf it.
► Balancing Changes
#### Pets
- Golden Dragon
  - Dragons greed: Buffed Strength per 5 Magic find from flat 0.5 to 0.5% (Max 5% Strength at 50 Magic Find)
- Flying Fish
  - Quick Reel (Legendary) = Buffed from 75 to 80 Fishing Speed
  - Quick Reel (Mythic) = Buffed from 75 to 80 Fishing Speed
- Zombie Pet
  - Nerf crafting recipe to 8 stacks of Enchanted Rotten Flesh instead of the current 8 Zombie Hearts.
  - Kat now requires 8 stacks of Enchanted Rotten Flesh instead.
#### = Gear
- Goldor Leggings
  - Health 250 -> 260
  - Defense 220 -> 240
- Juju Shortbow
  - Enderman Slayer Requirement Lvl 3 -> 5
- Dark Claymore
  - Added two combat Gemstone slots
  - Added 30 Critical Damage, from 0
- Livid Dagger
  - Nerfed Attack Speed from 100 to 50
- Shadow Fury
  - Buffed Strength from 125 to 130
  - Buffed Strength from 130 to 160
  - Buffed Crit Damage from 0 to 30
  - Buffed Crit Damage from 0 to 60
- Ragnarok Axe
  - Nerfed Weapon damage from 250 to 200
  - Nerfed the ability from 2x to 1.5x Strength
- Spirit Sceptre
  - Ability Mana cost goes from 250 to 200 Mana
- Super Cleaver
  - Buffed Weapon Damage from 85 to 105
  - Buffed Critical Damage from 0 to 20
- Hyper Cleaver
  - Buffed Weapon Damage from 145 to 175
  - Buffed Critical Damage from 0 to 50
- Giant Cleaver
  - Buffed Weapon Damage from 170 to 235
  - Buffed Critical Damage from 0 to 95
  - Buffed Strength from 60 to 95
#### Power Stones
- Furball (Itchy power)
  - Droprate 0.82% -> 1.5%
  - Attack speed 25% -> 30%
  - Walk Speed 10% -> 5%
- Luxurious Spool (Silky)
  - Crit damage 100% -> 95%
  - Speed 0% -> 5%
- Hazmat Enderman
  - Strength 20% -> 25%
  - Crit Damage 20% -> 25%
  - Defense 20% -> 25%
  - Health 20% -> 25%
  - Attack Speed 10% -> 15%
Sven Slayer The amount of Combat EXP required to summon Sven has been nerfed, which should make spawning Sven approximately 30% faster.
Also, we recently changed the main way to obtain the Luxurious Spool, it used to be a 100% drop from the Brood Mother, it now only has a 5% chance to drop from it, however you now have a 100% chance to obtain it when killing Arachne.
► Bugs Fixes
- HoTM Pickaxe abilities now show the correct call to action depending on the state.
- Bal Pet's stats now apply properly.
- Mineral armor now works on glowstone.
► A note on future updates Here's a quick recap of what you can expect in the next few updates!
Happening pretty soon but in no specific order:
- End Island QoL Update
- Kuudra revamp
- Garden Update (part 1)
- SkyBlock Levels follow up
- New bits items
Happening moderately soon:
- Wizard Portal
- Garden Update (part 2)

# Changelog/2022/September 6
Categories: 2022 Updates, Updates
PATCH
[September 6] Moby's Fishing Speed Chumpdate
Updated: September 6, 2022
Hey all!
Since 0.14 changed Fishing Speed to a player stat, fishing has been a slower experience.
We've added items and content to bring back players' fishing speed back up to the cap!
### Moby
Moby, found in the Mushroom Desert island
Moby is a new NPC in the Mushroom Desert, hidden away in the Glowing Mushroom Cave around -206, 43, -500.
They offer a small questline which introduces a new item for fishing: the Chum Bucket.
Chum Bucket in action
Players can now collaborate to gather Chums together and fill buckets to trade with Moby for one of 6 new items.
## New Enchants
You can now find the Bobbin's Scriptures very rarely from Yetis, Grim Reapers, Sea Emperors and Jawbus sea creatures.
The scriptures can be crafted with tons of fishing materials into the new Bobbin' Time ultimate enchant:
It's Bobbin' Time! (not Legion)
There is also now Piscary for Fishing Rods unlocked at enchanting level 8.
Piscary VI is available for grabs rarely in the experimentation table.
Piscary enchant
### Ammonite Pet Rework
The Ammonite pet has received 2 changes to its perk.
First, "Not a Snail" speed and defense have been moved to Gift of the Ammonite.
Second, "Not a Snail" has been replaced with "Expert Cave Fisher", which makes the Ammonite useful for underground fishing situations.
What even is an ammonite really?
### Other Content
- Lava Leeches now rarely drop the Pitchin' Koi for the Pitchin' reforge stone.
- Kuudra Mandibles, wherever they could be, will now also have +10 Fishing Speed.
- Added new equipment pieces using shark and crimson fishing ingredients:
One of 3 new equipment pieces for fishing (3 upgrade tiers each)
### Balance
- Buffed the 'Submerged' reforge to get +5 Fishing Speed.
- Pod Tactics from Dolphin pet 9->10 Fishing Speed and 10->30 blocks range.
- Mining Fiesta now grants +75 Mining Wisdom instead of +1.5x Mining XP.
- Cole's Mining XP Buff now grants +50 Mining Wisdom instead of multiplying the player's Mining Wisdom by 1.5x.
- Buffed Enchanting Skill's Conjurer perk 4->5% extra XP orbs per level.
  - This way, you can get 350 levels from a titanic when maxed enchanting.
- Added +20 Vitality to Mosquito Bow.
- Changed Holy Dragon Armor set bonus to use Health Regen:
Holy Dragon Armor!
- Moved the Health Regen from the Jellyfish Pet's Radiant Regeneration to a base stat on the pet and renamed the perk to Radiant Scyphozoa.
Happy Fishing! :hypixel_confused:

# Changelog/2023/April 11
Categories: 2023 AHN Updates, Updates
AHN
Alpha Testing 04/11/23 - Attribute Balancing and Testing
Updated: April 11, 2023
Hello!
Today we're looking to test on Alpha whether attributes are balanced when released on non-Crimson Isle items. Attributes are a special type of buff exclusive to certain items obtained from the Crimson Isle. You can read more about Attributes here.
The below functionality has been set up to allow players to test attributes:
- All items can have attributes applied to them as long as attributes for that item type exist.
- You can obtain all Attribute Shards and the Kuudra Washing Machine at Centauri's Featured Update Items menu.
  - Centauri's Featured Update Items menu now has a search function, allowing you to find the Attribute Shard level and type you want as fast as possible.
- You can apply up to two attributes to an item.
- Combine an item with the Kuudra Washing Machine in an anvil to remove all attributes on it.
- If you wish to apply a third attribute, you will need to remove all attributes from the item first.
- In addition, Centauri has a new functionality that allows you to clone your item. This allows players to test different combinations of attributes on the same item side by side.
- Mayor Diana's Mythological Ritual event is active for the duration of this Alpha test.
Balance Changes
- Mending and Vitality have been preemptively nerfed to no longer scale with Catacombs level bonus.
  - Vitality scaling off Catacombs level bonus gives way too much healing.
  - This will affect the Mender Helmet series, and their base stats will be adjusted accordingly if need so.
Designer's Note - Attributes will NOT be applied to items this way on release, the above functionalities have been set up for the ease of applying attributes to items for balance testing.
Why attributes on all items?
We're looking to allow attributes onto all items to make it much easier to design items that exceed the power levels of Crimson Isle items. If attributes were not applicable on items outside the Crimson Isle, they'd need bloated stat numbers and abilities to compete with Crimson Isle items remotely. Sword and Bow attributes are also heavily underutilized due to the lack of distribution and viable items of that type in the Crimson Isle. Allowing these attributes on all items will let sword and bow attributes see the light of day.
If this all goes to plan, Attribute Shards will be available way before players reach the Crimson Isle and have significantly more methods of obtaining them. The attributes system will also undergo major changes to make buying, selling, and combining Attribute Shards easier, with some attribute balancing in between.
Profile Backend Changes
We are also continuing our testing of some important backend profile changes that aim to improve the overall game and API stability. These changes primarily affect the loading of profile related data such as collections and inventories. So when playing, keep an eye out for any missing or inaccurate data on your profile members. Particularly noteworthy is that certain skills or levels may be slightly increased due to fixing some long standing down casting issues with our database code.
Summary
- Looking to test on Alpha whether attributes are balanced if they can be applied to all items.
- Attributes can be applied to items through the /attributemenu.
- Visit the Centauri NPC in the Alpha SkyBlock hub to get Attribute Shards and an item to wash away attributes!
- If testing goes well, Attributes can be obtained in places outside the Crimson Isle in the near future.
- Testing of new backend data changes, should look identical in-game!
If you encounter any issues - small, major, or critical, please report them here and make sure you select alpha.hypixel.net!
UPDATE 4/17/2023
- Alpha is now closed to the public.
UPDATE 4/12/2023
- Fixed pets sometimes getting dupe detected
- Made kuudra washing machine unstackable
- Prevented attributes being given to items from mob drops and crafting
- Allowed sword attributes to be applied to longswords
- Made the error message when combining attributes a bit more helpful
  - Testing of new backend data changes, should look identical in-game!

# Changelog/2023/April 20
Categories: 2023 AHN Updates, Updates
AHN
Alpha Testing 04/20/23 - Attribute Balancing and Testing 2
Updated: April 20, 2023
Hello again!
We're back with another round of testing on alpha. This session gives the community a glimpse into some planned balancing changes regarding Attributes while stress testing a bit more backend changes.
The following adjustments were made:
Attributes:
- Change Vitality from 3 Vitality per tier to 2 Vitality per tier
- Fixed fortitude to be a % instead of a multiplier
- Fixed Combo to work without Grandma Wolf equipped (but exists in your pet menu)
- Elite now increases damage dealt to Mythological creatures
- Dominance now works at 90% health instead of requiring 100%
- Mana Steal attribute now procs on kill instead of on every hit and renamed to Mana Recovery
- The Hunter attribute is buffed from 0.1 to 0.3 SCC per tier
- Mana Pool has been reduced from 20 intelligence per tier to 10 intelligence per tier
  - Aurora Armor has been compensated accordingly with increased ability damage to compete with Storm's Armor
- Mana Regeneration has been increased from 1% to 1.25% per tier
Dungeons:
- Mage beam now is affected by Attack Speed
- Hitbox of mobs when using mage beam has been slightly increased
- Mage beam range has been extended further for M7 dragon fight
- Increased Storm's defense in M7 by 50%
Other Changes:
- Mana Steal and Life Steal enchants to proc off of magic weapon hits
- Life Steal and Syphon enchants no longer conflict with Mana Steal.
- We are also still testing some significant backend profile changes. Make sure to report any issues with data not loading on the forums.
  - These changes primarily affect the loading of profile-related data, such as collections and inventories. So, watch for missing or inaccurate data on your profile members when playing.

# Changelog/2023/January 13
Categories: 2023 AHN Updates, Updates
AHN
[January 13] SkyBlock Menu Layout and Profile Performance Alpha Testing
Updated: January 13, 2023
Hey everyone,
Today we are opening up the Alpha network to test some performance improvements to the game as well as some layout changes to the SkyBlock Menu.
We know there might not be as many visual changes in this Alpha, but we would greatly appreciate many players attempting to play many different areas of the game for our testing.
## SkyBlock Menu Changes
We have made some changes to the layout of the SkyBlock Menu to improve clarity and fix over-cluttering. We want to gauge the communities feedback on these changes.
The modified SkyBlock Menu layout
Read more about SkyBlock Menu changes
What has changed:
- Moved the Trades menu inside the Recipe Book menu​
  - You can now instantly sell your items to the Booster Cookie menu if you have a Booster Cookie​
  - Moved the Leveling icon in the old place​
- Moved all 5 Bags into a new Bags menu that can be accessed from the main menu​
- Moved Active Effects to Your Profile menu​
- New Booster Cookie Menu​
  - Can now buy Booster Cookies directly from this menu​
  - Cookie Clicker v0.02​
    - Cookie Clicker now stays across lobbies!​
    - There is a Cookie Clicker leaderboard to view your position.​
  - All players have access to this menu from the main menu​
- The Crystal Hollows - Entrance Fast Travel is now automatically unlocked when first visiting the Crystal Hollows​
- If you do not have Pets, Wardrobe, Personal Bank, or any bags unlocked it will now show locked and * what is required to unlock instead of hiding from the menu​
- The Quick Warp to the Jerry Island has been moved to the Fast Travel menu​
- The Calendar and Events menu has been reduced to one line​
- The Upcoming Events and Personal Bank icons will now count down in the main menu in realtime​
Why have we made these changes?
The SkyBlock Menu has remained the same ever since the game has released. As new icons were needed, they were slowly added to the main menu. Over time, this menu became more crowded and crowded. We have decided to make these changes to not only allow the main menu to look nicer but also help with confusion towards new players. There are several icons that only show up at certain times or icons that need to be unlocked that the new player does not know about. We know a few of these changes will take some time to get used to and that is why we are releasing this change onto the Alpha Network in enough time for players to give us their feedback.
## Performance Improvements
Along with the new menu, we also are testing a lot of backend changes to the game relating to Profile Loading, NPCs and Zones. If you encounter an issue where your profile loads incorrectly or an NPC or Zone doesn't work properly, please report it!
### Profile Loading and Timeout Issues
We have identified an issue that is causing many large or late game profiles to be kicked from the game or timeout when attempting to switch islands or join SkyBlock. We have made some backend changes to the profile system which we hope will make improvements towards fixing this issue. If you are facing this issue on the server, we would like to invite you to join the Alpha to see if this issue still persists.
If you encounter any issues - small, major or critical, please report them here and make sure you select alpha.hypixel.net!
Thank you!

# Changelog/2023/January 26
Categories: 2023 AHN Updates, Updates
AHN
Alpha Testing 01/26/23 - The Garden
Updated: January 26, 2023
Hey everyone! Today we're opening alpha for The Garden, but we're doing things a bit different this time around. Today, we would like you all to test later game farming. Therefore, we've made it way easier to unlock plots and progress!
Modifications:
- Plots have very low requirements for cleaning and have most of the overgrowth removed
- Plots are free to unlock
- There is an NPC in the hub called Centauri that will give free items and coins!
EDIT: We are pleased with the data we've collected from this environment. We are going back to normal conditions for a final round of bug gathering and squashing -- though Centauri is still around to help. Because of this, we had to clear everyone's data. Thank you for your continued testing and support; it helps us out a lot!
If you encounter any issues - small, major or critical, please report them here and make sure you select alpha.hypixel.net!
Known issues - Please do not report these!
- You can place stacks of >1 Biofuel into the composter during the initial Garden quest
- When you first get Jerry on your island, you can ask him to leave but he doesn't. Jerry.
- If you reach SkyBlock Level 5 whilst on your island, Sam doesn't spawn until you reload the island
- You can soft-lock the Garden quest by unlocking all the plots before continuing the quest.
- Closing the menu to claim Sam's Scythe doesn't claim the item correctly
- On Stranded, Beth's entry in the logbook doesn't have any lore
- The Box of Seeds cannot be placed into the Personal Compactor talisman.
- Rejoining Garden instances can reroll NPC rewards.
- Some of Sam's dialogue can change colors when the text moves to a new line.
- When you get a RARE CROP!, it now plays the rare drop sound.
- You can break protected crops to get rare crops.
- You can break plot walls by breaking a tall grass with Sam's Scythe while wearing mineral armor.
- InfiniDirt Wand can now be used on the Garden.
- Various NPCs cannot be added to the Logbook when you interact with them normally.
- Fixed various typos.
- InfiniDirt Wand doesn't work in the Builder's Ruler.
- Weapons that fire Wither Skulls break blocks on impact.
- Breaking Melons with a Silk Touch Axe will not increment the Garden crop milestone.
- Sam is very pushy about wanting to give you a Sam's Scythe.
- On the Private Island, Sam can be moved around with Knockback
- Crop milestone #19 is very slightly too many crops.
- /invite doesn't bypass island visit permissions.
- Prismapumps, Teleport Pads, Day Savers, and Night Saverscannot be placed on the Garden.
  - Note that this does include some other Tile Entities, such as Hard Glass.
  - When these items are copied via a Custom Preset, they will not paste on the plot. This is intentional.
- You can't click to follow players in a party to travel to their Garden.
- The hologram in between Garden plots doesn't allow blocks to be placed under it.
- The compost menu may not close when it's currently composting
- An Emissary NPC may appear with double holograms
- Some Garden skins may have NPCs or menus in weird places. Feel free to keep reporting these if you find any!
- When rejoining an island, you have to listen to the NPC's dialogue again.
- While not fixed, you can right click the NPC again to instantly open the menu.
- While on a non-standard profile (ironman, stranded), the 'Plot: X' entry in the scoreboard does not appear.
- If the composter is full of organic matter, adding more organic matter to it will say it is 'Full of Fuel', instead of 'Full of Compost'.

[July 7] Item Linking, Stash QOL, & Backend Changes
Updated: July 7, 2023
What's poppin gamers!

  - You can now show off your items in chat by using /show or by typing [item] in your message.

# Changelog/2023/March 21/0
Categories: 2023 AHN Updates, Updates
Note: The thread on Hypixel's forums for the first Changelog below was renamed and edited to be used for the March 30, 2023 Changelog. A copy of the original version from March 21 is given below.
AHN
Alpha Testing 03/21/23 - The Garden Crop Growth
Updated: March 21, 2023
Hey everyone! Today we're opening alpha for custom crop growth in The Garden! This allows us to modify your crop growth speed per Garden Level. In order to do this, we've had to implement a lot of our own logic into farms, so we need to know what feels wrong and how well the servers perform with it.
Summary
- All crops have a +10% growth rate increase per Garden Level.
- Crops that require soil to grow will have a 40% debuff on dry soil.
- Growth logic is handled differently -- this means that farming quirks like cactus only growing next to certain blocks may not work or act slightly different. We've done our best to replicate it, but please report changes that may be impactful or annoying.
If you encounter any issues - small, major or critical, please report them here and make sure you select alpha.hypixel.net!
Known issues - Please do not report these!
- Pumpkin and Melon stems will grow their respective crops slower if they've been bone mealed.
- Cactus will still grow when adjacent to full blocks.
- Fences can connect with Cactus.
- Mushrooms will grow and automagically break themselves when they grow in a protected area.

# Changelog/2023/March 24/0
Categories: 2023 AHN Updates, Updates
AHN
Alpha Testing 03/24/23 - The Garden 1.5!
Updated: March 24, 2023
Hi everyone! We're opening up Alpha today with various Garden changes from the past few sessions all combined together! There is a lot of new content here, so if you have any issues or bugs, feel free to report them HERE!
Composter Upgrades:
The Composter can now be upgraded with crops from your Garden! There are five upgrades, each with 25 levels. Each upgrade gives a small amount of SkyBlock XP.
- Speed - The Composter will create compost 20% faster per level.
- Multi Drop - The Composter has a 2% per level chance to give double Compost.
- Fuel Cap - The Composter will hold 30,000 more fuel per level.
- Organic Matter Cap - The Composter will hold 20,000 more Organic Matter per level.
- Cost Reduction - The Composter will require 1% less materials per level.
New Content:
- Significantly increased the NPC sell price of all crops, including rare crops.
- Visitors will now ask for significantly less items and give the same amount of Copper and Garden XP.
- Added Seymour's Armor set to the Museum.
- Increased the private island and Garden teleport pad limit to 56.
- Added a chat message when a visitor joins your Garden.
- Seeds are now affected by Farming Fortune.
- The wheat crop upgrade now gives Farming Fortune to seeds.
- Custom plot presets can now have special icons added to them.
- Nether wart now gives Farming XP instead of Alchemy XP.
- The next Farming Contest will always display in tab.
- Crop Milestones will now appear in tablist for the current crop you're farming.
- Strength will now appear in tab when you have a Mooshroom Cow pet equipped.
- All farming tools can now be sold on the Auction House.
- Added Skill Numeral support for all Garden Milestones.
- All farming tools can now only break crops.
- Each unlocked Garden plot gives +3 Farming Fortune and +5 SkyBlock XP.
- Seeds, Cropies, Squash, Fermentos, and Condensed Fermentos can now be added to the Composter.
- Color codes now work on signs in the Garden.
- Added 3 new Garden accessories.
- Added 2 new Garden enchants.
- Added 5 new Garden reforge stones.
- Added 2 new pet items.
- The crop growth changes located in this thread (https://hypixel.net/threads/5311272/) should now be available.
  - These changes are still subject to balancing and are not final.
Bugfixes:
- Fixed an issue where Alchemist would disappear when the chunk unloaded.
- Fixed teleport pads not removing correctly when pasting plots.
- Fixed Seraphine not being unlocked when talking to her in the Election Room.
- Added a confirmation menu when pasting a plot.
- Added Jake's Plushie to the SkyBlock Guide.
- Made some of the Farming Tools' descriptions clearer.
- All crops should grow just as good during the day as they do at night.
- Mycelium no longer spreads.
- Tilled soil will always be at maximum moisture, regardless if water is near it.
- Miscellaneous item optimizations
If you encounter any issues - small, major or critical, please report them here and make sure you select alpha.hypixel.net!

# Changelog/2023/November 8
Categories: 2023 AHN Updates, Updates
AHN
[November 8th] SkyBlock Alpha - Garden Pests & Jacob's Contest
Updated: November 8, 2023
Hi all,
Alpha is open this week with some new Garden content. To help test, login to the Alpha network and test your Garden and farming content to ensure Pests and Jacob's Contests are working properly.
There are too many additions and changes to list here. The only requirement you need to know is that Pests start appearing on your Garden at Garden Level 5.
UPDATE 11/12: We have made a number of changes to the Pests system since initial Alpha release, a list of which can be found below. If a change is not currently live on Alpha, please be a little patient!
- Pest drops rely on a new system that utilizes Farming Fortune in place of Magic Find.
  - For drops more common than 5% (which are exclusively crops), drops are multiplied by your farming fortune + your crop-specific fortune for that crop.
  - For drops rarer than 5%, drop chances are increased by your farming fortune in a manner similar to magic find.
- Following the aforementioned change, drop tables for pests have been changed, with two notable things happening:
  - Most rare drops have been made more common following feedback.
  - Instead of dropping a random number of its respective vanilla crop, each pest now drops 1 enchanted version of its base crop. This is affected by farming fortune + crop-specific fortune as mentioned previously, so a player with 1900 total fortune will drop 20 enchanted crops per pest.
- Pest Repellant and Pest Repellant Max can now be purchased from SkyMart for Copper. Each last 1 hour and can be consumed to reduce your chances of pests spawning by 2x and 4x respectively.
- Sprayonator now reduces the internal pest cooldown timer by 2x, meaning you are theoretically able to spawn pests 2x as often.
- Players can now deselect vinyls in the Chirping Stereo.
- Players can no longer damage regular mobs by hitting them with a vacuum.
- Bonus Pest Chance was removed from all vacuums and redistributed among the Pesthunter Accessory line.
- Added Jacob's Tickets to Garden Sack.
- Diamond and Platinum medals now award 1 gold, 1 silver and 1 gold, 1 bronze in addition to some extra bonus tickets.
  - Getting platinum in all contests awards a grey emblem.
  - Getting diamond in all contests awards a gold emblem.
- Crop Milestones have been reduced across the board.
- Added a prompt when a pest spawns that allows you to instantly TP to the target plot.
- Plot holograms and /desk now show how many pests are in a plot.
- Farming Fortune now decreases at 4-8 pests instead of 3-7.
- Harvest Harbringer potion effect buffed from 25 to 50 Farming Fortune.
- Beds have been disabled in The Garden.
- Bestiary max for each pest has been reduced from 400 to 250. Same amount of tiers (15) per pest
  - Instead of awarding MF + Strength, each pest bestiary tier awards 0.4 farming fortune.
- Cooldown of Pest Tracker reduced from 5s to 1s.
- The Chirping Stereo is now required to obtain the Infinivacuum Hooverius at SkyMart, and its vinyl ability has been incorporated into the vacuum.
  - The Atmospheric Filter has been turned into an accessory.
- Renamed Worm to Earthworm.
- 2 new Legendary Barn skins in SkyMart!
Further changes are in the works and should be on Alpha soon. We will continue to monitor feedback and make changes as needed. Thanks!
If you encounter any issues - small, major, or critical; please report them here and make sure you select alpha.hypixel.net!

# Changelog/2023/October 10/1
Categories: 2023 AHN Updates, Updates
AHN
Alpha Testing 10/10/23 - Ability Transfusion
Updated: October 10, 2023
Hello!
Today we are opening Alpha to test a new feature that we are planning to add very soon. This feature allows players to extract active abilities from items and transfuse them to other items. This means you will be able to place any ability of your choosing on any item (mostly weapons) of your choice, although certain restrictions will apply.
Below is the functionality that has been set up on Alpha to allow players to test:
- Rogier is an NPC that you can find on the End Island, straight across from the Lone Adventurer, in a small cave behind the big hole.
- Talking to Rogier will make him move into the Hub at 66 71 -61.
- Once you complete his quest, he'll give you the option to either extract, reset or transfuse items.
- To extract an item, place the item you would like to permanently unlock the ability of inside Rogier's menu and click accept. This will permanently co-op soulbind the item. However, you will now be able to transfuse the ability onto as many other items as you please.
- To transfuse an item, place the item you would like to add or change the ability of on the left side of the menu. Choose one of your extracted abilities on the right to transfuse the ability onto your item. This will temporarily co-op soulbound the item as long as the new ability is present on the weapon. To remove the co-op soulbind you can reset your ability at Rogier and it will be tradeable again.
- Certain items have abilities that cannot be extracted and transfused on other items, such as Aspect of the Jerry, for balancing or other reasons. If you feel like an ability should be able to be extracted and transfusable and it's currently not, please let us know!
- The difference between abilities and passives has been made more obvious on items. Certain abilities of weapons have been moved to passives. *To be classified as an ability, the ability must have an active trigger, which wasn't the case on a number of items such as the Cleaver. *Passives are not extractable or transfuseable!
- Transmission Tuner has been changed to work with Rogier.
- Wither Scrolls Implosion, Wither Shield and Shadow Warp are now Ability Scrolls and can be extracted.
Balance Changes
- Ability Damage Scaling has been changed for all abilities and now is the same for every weapon. This means there will no longer be abilities that scale slower or faster depending on your intelligence. The base ability damage of abilities have been changed accordingly.
- Certain weapons have been recategorized into Wands
Designer Notes We've been looking forward to this addition for quite some time now, and we would like to share some of our reasons on why we ultimately decided to pursue this idea:
1. We want players to not feel restricted to specific weapons solely because they favor one item's ability over others, as that may hinder players from swapping to better gear with better stats as often during their progression through the game.
2. Being able to transfer abilities on other items will shift the meta of items more frequently than before and allow for more versatile builds that may change depending on your playstyle.
3. It will be much easier for us to implement new weapons into the game that are more impactful, as a simple stat increase can make a huge difference.
4. It will be much easier for us to balance abilities and items, as we now can balance abilities and items separately from each other.
We know it's a huge change to the game and we would like to make sure we implement this feature with all the balancing changes that might be necessary going forward before we release this update into SkyBlock.
If you encounter any issues - small, major, or critical, please report them here and make sure you select alpha.hypixel.net!

# Changelog/2023/April 18
Categories: 2023 Updates, Updates
0.18.3
SkyBlock Patch 0.18.3 - Garden Crop Growth & Presets, Bazaar & Sack Additions, Bug Fixes, & More
Updated: April 18, 2023
Hey Everyone! Today's Patch includes quite a lot of different things, including (but not limited to):
- The Long Awaited Crop Growth Changes (tm)
- Garden Preset Changes
- Bazaar Additions
- Trophy Fishing Pity System
- New Sacks and Updates to old Sacks
- Winter Island Balancing and other fixes
- Stranded Changes and Bug Fixes
- A massive amount of changes and other bug fixes (Over 100!)
As you can see, there is quite a lot packed into this update. Read below for the full SkyBlock v0.18.3 patch notes!
## Crop Growth Changes
After releasing The Garden, it became clear that growth rates were not balanced properly, but we couldn't change them with our existing code. So, we've spent the last few weeks tweaking it to satisfaction. We will monitor the current speeds and will make changes if necessary, and hopefully after this, players should be able to properly set up farming loops on their islands.
- Crops now grow 10% faster per garden level.
- No more light requirements for any crops.
- Mushrooms will now only spread on podzol and mycelium.
- Mushrooms will only grow on the same y-axis, on the 8 blocks immediately surrounding another mushroom.
- Soil mechanics (for wheat, potatoes, carrots, melon stems, and pumpkin stems):
  - Crops will not grow on completely dry soil.
  - Crops will grow with a 40% speed decrease on soil that is not at full moisture.
  - Crops will have no debuff on fully moist soil.
- Mycelium no longer spreads.
## Garden Preset Changes
Some of our pre-included presets were not up to standards. We've improved them for your convenience, and added a preset to allow you to reset a plot completely.
- Added a super flat default preset.
- Improved Melon and Pumpkin presets.
- The default mushroom preset now has more space for mushrooms to grow.
- The default cactus preset now has more rows, with less cactus in each row.
## Bazaar Additions
The Bazaar has been reorganized to include several new categories and many new items. Click the spoiler below to reveal all items added to the Bazaar!
- Added "/bz <search>" to quickly search the bazaar with a command
- New Category: Pet Upgrades
  - Lucky Clover Core, EXP Share Core, Tier Boost Core, Chyme, Simple Carrot Candy, Great Carrot Candy, Superb Carrot Candy, Ultimate Carrot Candy, Radioactive Vial, Enderman Cortex Rewriter, Jerry 3D Glasses, Vampire Fang
- New Category: Dungeons
  - Kismet Feather, Dungeon Chest Key, Superboom TNT, Trap, Spirit Leap, Inflatable Jerry, Decoy, Mimic Fragments, Fel Pearl, Bonzo Fragment, Scarf Fragment, Livid Fragment, Healing Tissue, Jolly Pink Rock, L.A.S.R Eye, Bigfoot's Lasso, Diamante Handle, Ancient Rose, Wither Catalyst, Spirit Wing, Spirit Bone
- New Category: Minion Upgrades

- New Category: Forge
  - Plasma, Glacite Jewel, Fuel Tank, Bejeweled Handle, Gemstone Mixture, Mithril Plate, Drill Engine, Golden Plate
- New Category: Crystal Hollows Materials
  - Divan Fragment, Bob-omb, Corleonite, Jungle Key, Control Switch, Superlite Motor, Synthetic Heart, Electron Transmitter, FTX 3070, Robotron Reflector (also moved Yoggies and Sludge Juice here)
- New Category: Flowers

## Trophy Fish Pity System
A new pity system has been introduced to help you when you get unlucky during Trophy Fishing:
- When you fish 100th of the same fish, you are guaranteed to get a Gold Tier fish if it wasn't obtained before
- When you fish 1,000th of the same fish, you are guaranteed to get a Diamond Tier if it wasn't obtained before

## Winter Island Balancing and Fixes

# Changelog/2023/August 1
Categories: 2023 Updates, Updates
0.19.2
SkyBlock Patch 0.19.2 - Bestiary, QoL, & Bug Fixes
Updated: August 1, 2023
Hello!
Welcome to SkyBlock Patch 0.19.2 . Hop on aboard to learn more about the updated Bestiary, new Experimentation Table item, general improvements, bug fixes, and more!
## Bestiary
The Bestiary is a place to track your mob kills and earn rewards for killing mobs. You can access the Bestiary with /bestiary (or its alias, /be). The Bestiary can be accessed without any special requirements.
New Mobs
We have added just over 100 mobs to the Bestiary! The Bestiary now encompasses mobs from sixteen categories. The new categories include the following:
- The Farming Islands
- Dwarven Mines
- Crystal Hollows
- Fishing (Water, Lava, and Seasonal)
- Mythological Events
- Jerry
- Kuudra
Many of the existing categories have also been updated with mobs that were previously missing. To find a specific mob's information in the Bestiary, you can also do /bestiary <mob> to quickly search for the mob!
Kill Requirements and Tier Scaling
Bestiary was originally designed to be a nearly infinite grind - a task that players could always turn to when they have nothing else to do in the game. SkyBlock was impossible to complete with Bestiary remaining an infinite grind. As such, we have updated the kill requirements for all mobs to be more reasonable for players that wish to gain Bestiary progression.
Previously, all non-private island mobs had the same kill tier cap of 41 and only a difference in scaling in terms of regular and boss mobs. In the new system, each mob now has its own kill tier caps. Kill scaling per tier has been standardized into 7 new kill brackets. The amount of mobs you have to kill to maximize a mob's tier is determined by its tier cap and kill brackets. More information about this system is available on the SkyBlock Wiki here .
When hovering over the mob in your Bestiary menu, you can see the amount of kills needed to progress towards the next tier, the total amount of kills needed to max the mob, and the tier the mob is capped at.
​
Bestiary Milestones Rescaled
Bestiary Milestone rewards have been rescaled along with adjustments to each mob's kill requirements and tiering. Combat EXP is now rewarded every 5 Bestiary Milestones instead of every 2 Bestiary Milestones to ensure that players get an appropriate amount of Combat EXP from the Bestiary relative to their combat level progression.
​
Claiming Bestiary Milestone Rewards
You now have control over when you want to claim your bestiary milestone rewards for the sweet Combat XP. You can access the Bestiary Milestone rewards menu by clicking on the Bestiary Milestone icon in the bottom right corner.
​ Claiming a bestiary milestone reward will claim that milestone's reward along with the rewards for all milestones preceding it. There will be a confirmation summing up the total rewards you are about to claim.
​ Bestiary Kill Credit
- Bestiary credit is now awarded when you assist in killing a mob.
  - This follows the behavior of the lootshare system: assist Bestiary credit is limited to 5 players. Players who dealt the most damage to the mob have a higher priority.
- All Kuudra mobs and certain dungeon mobs have shared bestiary credit within players in the same instance.
  - Lost Adventurer, Angry Archaeologist, Shadow Assassin, King Midas, Mimic, Wither Miner, Wither Husk, Wither Guard, and Terracottas have shared bestiary credit within your dungeon party and instance.
- The player who catches a sea creature will always get bestiary credit regardless of whether they dealt damage to their sea creature.
Mob Adjustments
- Heavily increased the spawn rates of Brood Mother and Arachne's Keeper.
  - There now can be multiple Arachne's Keeper alive at once.
- Heavily increased the spawn rate of Pack Magma Cube and Kada Knight.
- Heavily increased the spawn rate of Flaming Spider and Magma Cube Rider.
- Heavily increased the spawn rate (weight) of Zombie Miners.
- Doubled the mob cap of Golden Ghoul and Hellwisp.
- Slimes will now spawn at y=30 or below on your private island.
- Brood Mother and Key Guardian now only take a maximum of 5% of their health as damage per hit.
- Players deal 1% to legendary sea creatures caught by others for the first five seconds.
  - You still deal full damage to your own legendary sea creatures.
Other Adjustments
- Minion kills no longer count towards private island bestiary progress.
- Recommended Bestiary Milestones in the SkyBlock Level Guide for various stages of the game and the upgrade requirements for Grandma Wolf have been adjusted.
Bestiary Mob Tier Rewards
- Bonus coins per kill for each bestiary mob tier now work correctly.
- Bonus XP orbs per kill for each bestiary mob tier have been heavily increased.
- Magic Find per bestiary mob tier has been rescaled.
Missing Stats
Due to some oversights we made in the early design progress of SkyBlock, we are unable to recover the kills for the following mobs:
- Kuudra mobs, Bal, and Reindrake mob kills were not tracked
- The stats of level 1 Cow, Chicken, Sheep, Pig, and Zombie were merged into their minion counterparts and therefore do not count for Bestiary credit.
  - We did not differentiate between minion-spawned mobs and naturally spawned mobs when tracking the stats for these mobs.
## Other Changes
Fishing Effects Settings
A high percentage of our players are using mods that add visual indicators to make fishing easier. Without these visual aids, especially with many other players nearby, it can be quite difficult to reel in at the correct time. Therefore, we implemented similar features from our side, so everyone can use them without having to install a mod. This is specifically aimed at new players and players that do not want to install mods.
You can find the three new settings in the SkyBlock Menu under Settings → Personal → Fishing Settings.
Metaphysical Serum
Added the Metaphysical Serum to Super-Pairs rewards! The serum reduces the chain required for Experiment add-ons and can be obtained from doing your daily Experiments. The Metaphysical Serum can be bought and sold on the Bazaar.
​ Interfaces and Commands
- Added messages indicating when Booster Cookies and God Potions are about to run out.
- Added search functionality to Recipe Book menu.
- Added /getfromsacks command for moving specific item amounts to inventory. (/gfs alias)
- Improved Dungeons tab list, mainly adding Milestone progress.
- Added various SkyBlock Guide entries for Rift and Consumable tasks.
- Armor with more than 4 pieces now shows progress next to Full Set Bonus lore.
- Improved chests opened lore in Croesus menu.
- Added various SkyBlock XP tasks for consumables, including Serums and McGrubber's Burgers.
Items and Rewards
- Reworked Spirit Boots ghost ability to sneak to launch into the air. Holding sneak will propel you forward while a ghost.
- Splash Potions can no longer be modified through Brewing Stands.
- Phoenix Pet loot drop now goes directly to the player inventory or stash.
- Added Mixed Mite Gel to the Bazaar.
- Redstone and Emerald in the Mines of Divan are now affected by Mining Fortune.
- Croesus now gives loot directly instead of dropping it on the ground.
- Spirit Leaping into the Ice puzzle room in Dungeons now places you at the room spawn instead of at the player location.
- Dungeon Keys now go into Dungeon Sacks and will be removed directly from sacks if required.
- Can now donate the Jinxed Voodoo Doll to the Museum.
- Reaching Spider Slayer 7 will now give you +2 Crit Damage stat reward.
## Bug Fixes
Interface and Commands
- Fixed shift-clicking a Rune out of the altar adding unnecessary lore.
- Fixed not being able to quick-upgrade Vampire minions.
- Fixed coloring issue in Charlie's dialogue.
- Fixed Living Metal Heart not showing recipes when right-clicked.
- Fixed incorrect SkyBlock XP total being displayed for Harp Songs in the SkyBlock Guide menu.
- Fixed using CTRL+Q on a stack of Pumpkins printing an error message.
- Fixed lore text inside of the Jax menu.
- Fixed kcitS (Mirrored Stick) recipe showing vanilla wood as the ingredients.
- Fixed Cookie Clicker wipe caused by rapidly switching servers.
- Fixed smelting Wet Sponge granting Sponge collection progress.
- Fixed being able to open the Bingo menu in the Rift.
- Fixed /togglemusic not working properly.
- Fixed Flight Duration showing up in scoreboard if a Booster Cookie Buff is active.
- Fixed Flawless and Perfect gems showing sell prices when viewing Shop menus even though the items are not sellable.
- Fixed error message in chat when clicking an empty armor slot while browsing someone's Museum.
- Fixed Pickup Furniture icon in Carpentry Table menu overwriting the arrow menu icons.
Mining
- Fixed being able to mine Obsidian from Ember Armor ability.
- Fixed the Amber Material mining requirement being listed twice.
- Fixed dismounting a Rock Pet at Y=1 teleporting the player to the top of the world.
- Fixed the reforge stone unlock description being incorrect when leveling up the Mining Skill.
- Fixed being able to smelt Hemovibes.
- Fixed error message in Royal Pigeon referring to Dwarven Mines as "Dwarven Caves".
Fishing
- Fixed being able to fish in the Rift by right-clicking trapdoors.
- Fixed the Trophy Fish Pity system not working with the Golden Fish.
- Fixed Sea Creature counter not incrementing if someone else killed your Sea Creature.
- Fixed typo in Flying Fish's Rapid Decay perk description.
- Fixed buckets being placeable at the highest build level.
- Fixed Bronze and Silver Trophy Fishing Sacks missing some lore.
- Fixed Ice Bait not being bulk-buyable in the shop on the Winter island.
Combat and Loot
- Fixed Draconic Altar not putting items into Sacks.
- Fixed Nether minions losing all progress when players visit island without minion requirements.
- Fixed the Soulflow accessories resetting Overflow Mana gained from Gloomlock Grimoire.
- Fixed explosive arrows and the flame enchant breaking heavy pearls from a distance in the Matriarch.
- Fixed Dragon loot exploit through the use of other profiles.
- Fixed Dungeons Floor 7 arrow puzzle being breakable by Mage ghost ability.
- Fixed some Brood Mother drops being multiplied by Looting.
- Fixed quiver bug where bows could only be shot once.
- Fixed display message when a player tried to damage the eyes without talking to Porhtal.
- Fixed Witch Mask one-shotting Spooky Festival mobs.
- Fixed RNG Meter progress not getting reset if the selected RNG Meter drop is re-rolled.
- Fixed Arachne's Keeper message referring to the mob as "Arachne".
Enchanting
- Fixed an issue where enchantments applied though anvils had lower exp costs than intended.
- Fixed Compact enchantment not working on certain blocks.
- Fixed getting two Instant Find powerups in a row in the Experimentation Table, causing both to not work.
- Fixed Blessing enchantment not working on coins and Chum.
- Fixed One for All not having a skill level requirement.
- Fixed shop price reduction not working on some items in the Pig Shop.
Other Fixes
- Fixed the Spellbound Rune being placed in Rune Removal creating an Empty Rune.
- Fixed the trapper crest not increasing pelts collected while Jerry Mayor is on and Finnegan perks are active.
- Fixed being locked out of the Ring Of Broken Love from Detective Amog if you lost it after the quest was complete.
- Fixed the teleport sound being spammed while the protestor villagers are moving during the Barry protestor quest.
- Fixed Bingo and Alixer NPCs granting SkyBlock XP.
- Fixed bonus seeds from Rookie Hoe not going into your Agronomy Sack.
- Fixed Frosty not refilling your Snow Blaster properly.
- Fixed Night Vision not always applying in the Crimson Isle.
- Fixed Vampire Slayer mentioning the Hub; now properly references the Stillgore Chateau.
- Fixed Builder's Wand not working on Prismarine blocks.
- Fixed Wizard visitor in the Garden claiming the portal was not fixed.
- Fixed issue where players on newer versions of Minecraft would take damage when sneaking on non-full blocks.
- Fixed being able to place Repelling Candles in portal blocks.
- Fixed Jerrys spawning above the bedrock in the Crystal Hollows.
- Fixed being able to get stuck in a block when exiting a Rift Mirror Maze puzzle room using the exit button.
- Fixed the Tarantula Broodfather's cave spider separating from the rest of its body whilst in water.
- Fixed Jerry boxes not dropping when dying from fall damage, void damage, and fire damage.
- Fixed Auction stats missing the Mythic rarity.
- Fixed Keran dialogue speaking in third person.
- Fixed Charlie's dialogue mentioning the wrong zone.
- Fixed typo in Bluetooth Ring lore.
- Fixed Sack lore when stitched with Pocket Sack-in-a-Sacks.
- Fixed Theodolite being placeable on your island.
- Fixed Grandma Wolf in Rusty being unaffected by Crooked Artifact and other price modifiers.
- Fixed misleading lore in Precursor Eye claiming that mana is removed on damage when in reality it is removed on ability use.

# Changelog/2023/August 15
Categories: 2023 Updates, Updates
PATCH
[August 15] SkyBlock Patch Notes
Updated: August 15, 2023
Hey all! We are here to inform you about the latest update that was just rolled to SkyBlock!
- Fixed sometimes not being able to gain Combat EXP and Bestiary progress from killing mobs with abilities from weapons such as Hyperion.
- Fixed "Entering Dungeons floor" Autopet rule not working.
- Fixed being able to use Sinrecall Transmission in Jungle Temple (Crystal Hollows).
- Fixed Midas Staff ability not showing correctly when used above blocks like carpet.
- Fixed Midas Staff ability activating and taking Mana when no blocks are spawned and no damage can be dealt. This could happen when standing close to a wall. Now the ability doesn't activate and doesn't take any Mana. Keep in mind: An activated ability can still be stopped by blocks mid-way.
- Sea Creatures now despawn when their "owner" disconnects or leaves the server. This fixes a bug where you *could change profiles and earn Fishing EXP on a different profile.
- The Potato and Carrot areas in the Barn now use all three Crystals to regenerate the Crops instead of just one. This will speed up the Crop regeneration.
- The Nick Level setting now only appears in the Settings Menu when you're nicked.
- Reworded Toxic Arrow Poison description to be more accurate.
If you have a bug to report to us, please make a report HERE! Thanks to everyone who has reported bugs!
